Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了小区域估计（SAE）和基于模型的地理统计学（MBG）在患病率制图中的互补性，强调应从模型可解释性、协变量作用、推断目标及数据整合等方面进行更全面的比较，并提出针对实际任务的模拟验证方法优于传统交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高患病率制图的准确性和实用性，需要系统比较SAE与MBG方法在不同数据特征和推断目标下的优劣，并探讨如何更好地整合流行病学协变量和进行任务导向的模型验证。

Method: 通过对比分析SAE和MBG方法在模型可解释性、协变量使用、数据整合能力等方面的差异，提出将环境和社会经济风险因素作为中介变量处理调查设计变量的影响，并倡导采用任务特定的模拟验证方法评估模型性能。

Result: 研究表明，在MBG模型中不一定需要常规纳入调查设计变量，因为其影响可能已被可观测的风险因素所中介；同时，任务导向的模拟验证能比传统交叉验证提供更丰富的性能评估信息。

Conclusion: SAE与MBG各有优势，选择应基于具体应用需求；推荐使用可解释性强、融合多源数据且经过任务定制验证的建模策略以提升公共卫生决策支持能力。

Abstract: Small Area Estimation (SAE) and Model-Based Geostatistics (MBG) provide complementary approaches to prevalence mapping, with their relative advantages depending on the inferential goals and characteristics of the available data. We argue that a fuller comparison should consider model interpretability, the role of epidemiologically motivated covariates, inferential objectives beyond area-level prediction, integration of data from different spatial partitions and surveys, and task-specific model validation. In particular, we question whether survey design variables should routinely be included in MBG models when their effects may instead be mediated by measurable environmental and socio-economic risk factors. We further argue that simulation-based validation, tailored to the operational objectives of prevalence mapping, can provide a more informative assessment of model performance than conventional cross-validation alone.

</details>


### [82] [Causal Inference for Heterogeneous Extreme Quantiles with Heavy-Tailed Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03933)
*Xiaorui Wang,Juan-Juan Cai,Huixia Judy Wang,Jian Qing Shi,Yanlin Tang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种估计观测研究中重尾结果的条件极端分位数处理效应（CEQTEs）的框架，结合逆概率加权分位数回归与极值理论进行推断，并在理论上建立了IPW尾部分位数过程的弱收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在混杂因素和重尾结果的情况下，准确估计极端分位数上的处理效应具有挑战性，现有方法难以兼顾因果推断与极值建模，因此需要发展适用于观察性研究的CEQTE估计方法。

Method: 首先使用逆概率加权（IPW）分位数回归估计中间条件分位数，然后基于极值理论将其外推至极端水平；利用线性条件分位数模型下潜在结果的条件与边际分布共享相同极值指数（EVI）的性质，构建两种互补的Hill型EVI估计量。

Result: 建立了IPW尾部分位数过程在温和正则条件下的一般弱收敛性，无需最大吸引域假设；模拟研究表明所提方法在有限样本下表现良好；对NLSY79数据的应用发现大学教育对极高时薪的影响在不同协变量定义的子群中存在显著异质性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效结合了因果推断与极值统计方法，能够稳健地估计观察性研究中的条件极端分位数处理效应，为理解政策或干预在极端结果下的异质性影响提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a framework for estimating conditional extreme quantile treatment effects (CEQTEs) in observational studies with heavy-tailed outcomes. Our procedure first estimates intermediate conditional quantiles using inverse-probability-weighted (IPW) quantile regression and then extrapolates them to extreme levels using extreme value theory. Under a linear conditional quantile model, we show that the conditional and marginal distributions of each potential outcome share a common extreme value index (EVI), motivating two complementary Hill-type EVI estimators based on conditional and marginal information, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce an IPW tail quantile score process that bridges regression quantile score processes and uniform tail empirical processes while accounting for treatment assignment. We establish its functional weak convergence under mild regularity conditions, without requiring a max-domain-of-attraction condition. This result provides the probabilistic foundation for the asymptotic analysis of the proposed CEQTE estimators. Simulation studies demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance, and an application to NLSY79 data reveals substantial heterogeneity in the effect of college education on extremely high hourly wages across confounder-defined subpopulations.

</details>


### [83] [Model-assisted estimation with a training subsample: a two-phase sampling approach with design-based variance estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04082)
*María Eugenia Riaño*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单分割估计量的方差分解方法，通过将训练子样本视为第二阶段抽样，推导出精确的两阶段方差分解公式，并提出了无需更改点估计的解析和复制方差估计方法，在模拟中显示出接近标称覆盖的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论仅对分区平均、交叉拟合或对称化版本的模型辅助估计量进行不确定性量化，而实际应用中使用的单一分割估计量缺乏相应的方差分析框架。

Method: 将训练子样本视为概率抽样中的第二阶段抽样，利用Rao-Blackwell定理建立单分割估计量与其平均版本之间的方差关系，推导出适用于任意算法的两阶段方差分解，并针对树型预测器给出闭式解。

Result: 得到了适用于任意算法的精确两阶段方差分解；发现树型预测器的第二阶段方差可闭式计算，且其占总方差的比例随树复杂度增加而增大；提出了两种新的方差估计方法，在模拟中实现了近似标称覆盖水平。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了单分割模型辅助估计量的方差分析空白，提出的方差估计方法在不改变点估计的前提下显著改善了置信区间覆盖性能，且计算成本远低于分区平均方法。

Abstract: When a flexible prediction model is fitted on a training subsample drawn from a probability sample, the model-assisted estimator actually reported arises from one realized partition, yet existing theory quantifies uncertainty only for partition-averaged, cross-fitted, or symmetrized versions of it. We represent the training subsample as a second phase of sampling and derive, exactly and for any algorithm, a two-term variance decomposition and the variance family linking the single-partition estimator to its Rao-Blackwellized average, whose design bias it shares. For tree-type predictors the second-phase variance is computable in closed form, and its share of total variance grows with tree complexity, explaining documented variance underestimation. We propose an analytic and a replication variance estimator, neither altering the point estimate, and evaluate them by simulation: budgeting the second phase restores near-nominal coverage at a small fraction of the cost of partition averaging.

</details>

*Helen Zwölfer,Giovanni Cataldi,Umberto Borla,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本论文首次实现了在3+1维格点量子电动力学中对弦断裂过程的实时模拟，利用树张量网络研究了强耦合下电通量弦的动力学行为，并揭示了共振条件下弦的断裂机制及非共振时多种竞争通道的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接格点规范理论的量子模拟与真实物理维度中的QED和QCD，理解三维空间中实时弦动力学，特别是横向涨落和局部竞争过程的作用。

Method: 采用树张量网络方法，模拟3+1维U(1)格点规范理论中含动态物质的电通量弦淬火动力学。

Result: 在强耦合下发现弦在质量与规范耦合的特定共振条件下发生共振断裂，产生物质-反物质对屏蔽静态电荷；远离共振时，识别出包括粒子对产生、弦形变与扩展、通量环等多种竞争通道，其多重性导致弦区域被显著耗尽；发展了通道分辨的微扰理论，定量复现了这些动力学及其傅里叶谱。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来高维格点规范理论的量子模拟器提供了关键的诊断工具和基准测试方案。

Abstract: Understanding real-time string dynamics in three spatial dimensions is essential for connecting quantum simulations of lattice gauge theories (LGTs) to the physical dimensionality of QED and QCD, where transverse fluctuations and competing local processes proliferate. We present the first real-time simulations of string breaking in $3+1$D lattice quantum electrodynamics. Using tree tensor networks, we simulate the quench dynamics of electric flux strings in a $3\!+\!1$D U(1) LGT with dynamical matter. At strong coupling, the string breaks resonantly at a sharp resonance condition of mass and gauge couplings, converting electric energy into matter--antimatter pairs that screen the static charges. Off resonance, we classify all competing channels---pair production, string deformations and extensions, and flux loops---whose multiplicity, extensive for pair production and flux loops, depletes the string sector even far from resonance. A channel-resolved perturbation theory quantitatively reproduces these dynamics and their Fourier spectrum. Our results establish diagnostics and benchmarks for upcoming quantum simulators of higher-dimensional LGTs.

</details>


### [85] [Non-Abelian string melting and thermalization in an open lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04117)
*Erick Parra Verde,Giovanni Cataldi,Stefan Kühn,Giuseppe Magnifico,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 研究了开放系统中非阿贝尔SU(2)格点规范理论在热环境下的实时动力学，发现热介质会熔化色电通量弦，并揭示了非阿贝尔结构对热化时间和稳态的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非阿贝尔规范结构如何影响耗散环境中的实时动力学，弥补此前主要集中在阿贝尔情况的研究空白。

Method: 采用保持规范不变性的Lindblad主方程描述与热标量环境耦合的1+1维SU(2)杨-米尔斯格点规范理论，并使用张量网络进行求解。

Result: 热环境通过屏蔽通量和离域化色荷熔化通量弦；耗散熔化与相干断弦竞争导致热化时间随环境耦合呈非单调变化；强退相干极限下可通过Schrieffer-Wolff变换映射到经典排斥过程并解析求得热化时间；非阿贝尔结构导致稳态具有系统性的介子偏好，且热化时间随温度升高而降低。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于非阿贝尔格点规范理论的规范保持开放系统框架，揭示了非阿贝尔结构在热化动力学和弦动力学中的独特作用。

Abstract: Open-system lattice gauge theory (LGT) has so far been developed predominantly in Abelian settings, leaving open how genuinely non-Abelian gauge structure reshapes dissipative real-time dynamics. Here, we study a $1+1$D SU(2) Yang--Mills LGT with dynamical matter coupled to a thermal scalar environment through a gauge-preserving Lindblad evolution, which we solve using tensor networks. Starting from a quark--antiquark pair connected by a chromoelectric flux string, we find that the thermal medium melts the string by delocalizing the color charges and screening the flux; on resonance, this dissipative melting competes with and delays coherent string breaking. The thermalization time is non-monotonic in the environment coupling, decreasing through environment-assisted transport at weak dissipation before increasing in a quantum-Zeno regime. In the strong-dephasing limit, a Schrieffer--Wolff expansion maps the dynamics to a classical exclusion process and yields the Liouvillian thermalization time analytically. Beyond these generic open-system effects, the non-Abelian matter structure produces a systematic mesonic bias in the steady state, while the thermalization time decreases with temperature, in contrast to the Abelian Schwinger model trend and in qualitative agreement with pNRQCD studies of the quark--gluon plasma. These results establish a gauge-preserving framework for thermalization and string dynamics in open non-Abelian lattice gauge theories.

</details>


### [86] [Lattice QCD calculation of the pion-nucleon coupling $\bar{g}_0$ induced by the QCD $Θ$-term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04129)
*Fangcheng He,Tanmoy Bhattacharya,Rajan Gupta,Emanuele Mereghetti*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过2+1+1味HISQ组态的格点QCD计算，研究由QCD θ项诱导的CP破坏π核子耦合ḡ₀，在去除Nπ激发态污染并外推至物理π介子质量后获得较精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算CP破坏的π核子耦合ḡ₀对于理解强相互作用中的CP破坏机制及探索新物理具有重要意义。

Method: 使用MILC合作组生成的三个2+1+1味HISQ组态（格距约0.09 fm，π介子质量分别为313、226和138 MeV），通过核子基态间关联函数提取ḡ₀，并利用手征微扰理论和轴矢流守恒关系（AWI/PCAC）控制Nπ激发态污染。

Result: 第一种方法得到噪声较大的结果：ḡ₀/(2F_π) = -7(63)×10⁻³ θ̄；更精确的结果为：ḡ₀/(2F_π) = 17.4(1.9)×10⁻³ θ̄，后者基于低能有效场论或AWI方法。

Conclusion: AWI方法能有效控制Nπ激发态污染，显著提高ḡ₀计算精度，该方法可推广至其他核子矩阵元的计算中。

Abstract: We present lattice QCD results for the CP violating pion-nucleon coupling $\bar{g}_0$ induced by the QCD $\overlineΘ$ term from the analysis of three 2+1+1-flavor ensembles generated with highly improved staggered quarks (HISQ) by the MILC collaboration. These ensembles are at lattice spacing $a\approx 0.09~\text{fm}$ and pion masses of 313, 226 and 138 MeV, respectively. The coupling $\bar{g}_0$ is extracted in two ways. First, from the matrix element of the correlation between the pesudoscalar current and the topological charge evaluated between the nucleon ground state. The data for correlation functions with both the pseudoscalar and axial vector exhibit large contamination from the $Nπ$ excited state. We show that these can be controlled at the leading order using chiral perturbation theory ($χ$PT) and the axial Ward identity (AWI, also called the partially conserved axial current (PCAC) relation). The result after removing the $Nπ$ contamination and extrapolating to the physical pion mass is, however, noisy: $\bar{g}_0/(2F_π)=-7(63)\times 10^{-3} \,{\overlineΘ}$. The more precise result $\bar{g}_0/(2F_π)=17.4(1.9)\times 10^{-3} \,{\overlineΘ}$ is obtained using low energy effective field theory methods or equivalently the AWI. Since contamination from the $Nπ$ excited states arises in the calculation of many nucleon matrix elements, we give an extended discussion on them and the use of the AWI for controlling them in the calculation of $\bar g_0$.

</details>


### [87] [First Two-Hadron Form Factor from QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04142)
*Felipe G. Ortega-Gama,Raúl A. Briceño,Ivan M. Burbano,Robert G. Edwards*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次利用格点QCD计算了双强子散射态的能量依赖形式因子，通过有限体积方法非微扰地约束了无限体积下的π⁺π⁺ + γ → π⁺π⁺前向电磁振幅，并验证了该方法在满足Ward-Takahashi恒等式方面的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于格点QCD在欧几里得有限时空中无法直接获得渐近散射态，需发展非微扰方法来提取物理散射振幅，特别是多强子系统的电磁结构。

Method: 结合离散有限体积谱和电磁流的有限体积矩阵元，计算耦合π⁺π⁺有限体积态的三点关联函数，提取电磁矩阵元，并利用洛伦兹对称性、幺正性和解析性约束无限体积振幅。

Result: 成功约束了前向极限下π⁺π⁺ + γ → π⁺π⁺的无限体积振幅，将其描述为单一实值能量依赖的形式因子，且结果在所有考虑的能量和运动参考系中满足Ward-Takahashi恒等式。

Conclusion: 实现了首个基于QCD的第一性原理对双强子系统电磁形式因子的确定，验证了有限体积方法的正确性，为共振态和多强子束缚态的电磁及电弱响应研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present the first QCD determination of an energy-dependent form factor for a two-hadron scattering state. In particular, we calculate the QCD contribution to the forward electromagnetic $π^+π^+ + γ\toπ^+π^+$ amplitude at $m_π\approx 400$~MeV using lattice QCD. Because lattice calculations are performed in a finite Euclidean spacetime, where asymptotic scattering states are absent, this amplitude cannot be accessed directly from correlation functions. Instead, we can constrain this and related amplitudes nonperturbatively using a finite-volume formalism that requires two ingredients: the discrete finite-volume spectrum and finite-volume matrix elements of the electromagnetic current. We calculate three-point correlation functions coupling $π^+π^+$ finite-volume states and extract the corresponding electromagnetic matrix elements. Combining these results with the previously determined spectrum, we constrain the infinite-volume $π^+π^+ + γ\toπ^+π^+$ amplitude in the forward limit. Using constraints from Lorentz symmetry, unitarity, and analyticity, we describe this amplitude in terms of a single real-valued energy-dependent two-hadron form factor. The resulting amplitude and form factor agree with the Ward-Takahashi identity across all energies and moving frames considered, providing the first QCD validation of this finite-volume approach and a pathway toward first-principles studies of the electromagnetic structure and electroweak responses of resonances and multi-hadron bound states.

</details>

### [88] [Large-Language Models as a Cognitive Virus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03344)
*Ricard Solé,Giulio Ruffini,Francesca Castaldo,Marco Tuccio,Luis F. Seoane,Manlio de Domenico,Santiago F. Elena,David C. Krakauer,Michael Levin*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型（LLM）的传播类比为病毒传播，通过建模用户在未使用、使用和持续依赖状态间的转换，揭示社会传播、恢复和集体强化之间的相互作用可能导致临界点和技术锁定，甚至引发认知能力的急剧下降；同时，框架也指出了通过降低传播性和增强可逆性实现认知免疫的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益融入人类文化，理解其扩散机制及其对认知自主性的影响成为重要课题。作者希望通过借鉴流行病学模型，揭示LLM普及过程中可能出现的非线性集体转变及其潜在风险。

Method: 采用基于个体状态转移的动态传播模型，将用户分为未耦合、耦合和持续依赖三类，结合社会传播、恢复和集体强化机制，分析系统中的 tipping point、技术锁定和 runaway dynamics。

Result: 模型显示，当 Adoption 达到某一临界阈值后，微小的增长可能引发群体层面迅速转向持续依赖，并伴随认知能力的 abrupt 下降；同时识别出通过抑制传播和提升可逆性可实现认知免疫的条件。

Conclusion: LLM 的普及可能经历非线性的集体转变，带来认知自主性的重要挑战，但通过设计干预措施可避免失控依赖，维护人类的认知独立性。

Abstract: Large-language models (LLMs) are rapidly becoming part of human culture, reshaping how information is produced, transmitted, and used. Here we propose that their diffusion can be understood through a viral analogy, with LLM use spreading through populations, becoming embedded in cognitive and cultural practices. We model transitions among uncoupled, coupled, and persistently dependent users, and show that the interplay between social transmission, recovery, and collective reinforcement can generate tipping points and technological lock-in. A central consequence is the possibility of runaway dynamics: once a critical threshold is crossed, small increases in adoption can trigger rapid population-level shifts toward persistent dependence, with abrupt losses in cognitive competence. The same framework, however, identifies conditions for cognitive immunization, based on reducing transmission and facilitating reversibility. Our results highlight how LLM adoption may involve nonlinear collective transitions with important consequences for cognitive autonomy.

</details>

*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统决策模型无法充分描述人类复杂决策行为的问题，尤其是涉及情绪、偏见和动态信息交互的情况，本文提出了一个更贴近实际的决策网络模型。

Method: 构建了一个基于智能代理的决策网络模型，其中代理可以代表人、神经元或人工智能节点；该网络具有概率性、动态性和情感性，并考虑效用、情绪和记忆差异等影响因素；通过分析信息交换下的动态演化过程来研究决策误差的变化。

Result: 模型能够解释并解决如阿莱悖论等传统决策难题，在动态信息交互过程中决策误差逐渐降低；引入机器学习技术可调控代理行为，引导其选择特定选项。

Conclusion: 该决策网络模型能更真实地模拟复杂系统中的决策过程，适用于生物系统与人工智能系统的决策分析，并为干预和优化群体决策提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A new type of decision networks is suggested and its operation is analyzed. The network nodes are represented by intelligent agents who can denote either some biological beings, like humans, or neurons of the brain, or the nodes of artificial intelligence. The specifics of the network are in the following: It is probabilistic in the sense that the choice, accomplished by each agent, is characterized by the related probability. It is dynamic, with the probabilities varying in time due to the exchange of information between the agents. It is affective, because the agents choose between alternatives by taking account of utility as well as of biases and emotions. In general, it is heterogeneous, being composed of the groups of agents with different properties, for instance having long-term memory and short-term memory. The network dynamics, caused by the information exchange, results in decision error decrease. The network operation is illustrated by the example starting with the Allais paradox, its resolution, and the decision error diminution in the process of decision dynamics with information exchange. Resorting to machine-learning techniques it is possible to regulate the behavior of the network agents forcing them to choose particular alternatives.

</details>


### [90] [ESRIcascade.jl: A Julia package for fast computation of the Economic Systemic Risk Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03782)
*Mitja Devetak,Peter Klimek*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高经济系统性风险指数（ESRI）计算的效率，支持更大规模生产网络的频繁分析，推动ESRI在实证与方法论研究中的广泛应用。

Method: 开发了一个名为ESRIcascade.jl的Julia软件包，结合高性能计算与面向研究工作流的文档化接口，并通过基准测试验证其性能优势。

Result: ESRIcascade.jl比现有实现快140至300倍，在实际规模的合成经济中将计算时间从16小时减少到7分钟。

Conclusion: ESRIcascade.jl显著提升了ESRI计算效率，使对大型生产网络的多次分析变得可行，有助于推动相关领域的研究发展。

Abstract: This work presents ESRIcascade.jl, a Julia package for computing the Economic Systemic Risk Index (ESRI). The package combines computational efficiency with a documented interface designed for research workflows. Our benchmarks show that ESRIcascade.jl is from 140 to 300 times faster than existing implementations. For a realistically sized synthetic economy, this reduces computational time from 16 hours to 7 minutes. By reducing computation times, ESRIcascade.jl makes repeated analyses of larger production networks practical and feasible and may enable broader use of ESRI in empirical and methodological research. The package and its documentation are freely available at https://github.com/Devetak/ESRIcascade.jl under the MIT License.

</details>

### [91] [Buckling Prediction for Nonlinear Elastic Beams with Soft Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02956)
*Thien Tran-Duc,J. E. Bunder,A. J. Roberts*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的多尺度计算框架，用于预测含周期性软夹杂弹性梁的屈曲及后屈曲行为，通过局部微区计算结合宏尺度耦合算法，准确且高效地再现了屈曲起始与后续响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全尺度模拟计算成本高，难以有效分析含复杂微结构材料的非线性屈曲行为，因此需要一种既能保持精度又能降低计算代价的多尺度方法。

Method: 在前期线弹性多尺度框架基础上引入非线性，采用仅在稀疏子域（patch）进行微尺度计算，并通过已验证的patch耦合算法恢复宏尺度响应，结合雅可比矩阵特征值分析预测失稳起始。

Result: 结果显示夹杂刚度降低会减小临界屈曲应变和应力，使结构更易失稳，但后屈曲响应更平缓；不同插值阶数的影响被量化，且与全域模拟对比表明该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时保持高精度。

Conclusion: 所提框架能高效准确预测非均匀梁的屈曲与后屈曲行为，具有推广至异质梁、板、壳等工程结构的潜力。

Abstract: We develop an efficient and accurate multiscale computational framework for predicting the buckling and post-buckling behaviour of elastic beams containing periodically distributed soft inclusions. The framework extends our previous multiscale, patch, computational homogenisation for linear elasticity by incorporating nonlinearity, and thus enables accurate prediction of both the buckling onset and the subsequent post-buckling response. Microscale computations are performed only within a sparse set of small subdomains (patches), while the macroscale behaviour is recovered through a proven patch-coupling algorithm. The scheme is assessed through quarter- and half-domain patch computations for beams with inclusion-to-matrix Young's modulus ratios ranging from 0.001 to 1. The results show that reducing the inclusion stiffness lowers both the critical buckling strain and the critical buckling stress, indicating an increased susceptibility to instability, while producing a milder post-buckling response with smaller transverse deflections and stress drops. Eigenvalue analysis of the Jacobian matrix accurately predicts the onset of instability and the corresponding critical strain and stress. Bifurcation diagrams of the nonlinear buckled configurations under compressive loading, and a quantitative analysis of the effect of the interpolation order on the predicted buckling and post-buckling responses, are also presented. Comparisons with full-domain simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately predicts both the buckling threshold and the post-buckling behaviour while substantially reducing the computational cost. The methodology is readily extendable to heterogeneous beams, plates, shells, and other engineering structures.

</details>

Abstract: We present a high-order multiphysics solver for the simulation of microwave-heated flows. The solver couples a discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin (DPG) finite element method for the time-harmonic Maxwell equations with continuous Galerkin finite element methods for the heat equation and the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. We validate the electromagnetic solver against multiple benchmark problems: wave propagation in a rectangular waveguide, a cavity problem with a singular solution, and a microwave-heated obstacle problem, comparing our results against numerical and experimental data from the literature. The results confirm the validity of the implementation and demonstrate its ability to perform adaptive mesh refinement using the DPG method's built-in error estimator. The final part of the study showcases the capabilities of the multiphysics framework through simulations of microwave-heated flow around obstacles with singular geometric features. These results highlight the potential of the proposed framework for the simulation and optimization of microwave-assisted chemical processes. Finally, the developed high-order multiphysics solver has a low memory footprint, since the electromagnetic solver relies on a Conjugate Gradient (CG) iterative solver and the fluid solver is implemented in a matrix-free fashion, making the overall approach scalable and well-suited for large-scale parallel simulations.

</details>

Main category: math.NA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样加权方法在解流形上使用固定权重，限制了超降维的潜力，本文旨在通过引入随隐变量连续非线性变化的自适应权重来进一步压缩空间采样实体数量。

Result: 在两种非线性基准测试中，与标准线性降阶模型相比，采样积分点减少两个数量级以上；相对于固定权重流形模型，自适应权重进一步消除了约80%至97%的剩余点，且几乎保持原有精度。

Conclusion: MAW-ECM能有效挖掘超降维潜力，在非线性参数化问题中实现更高压缩率而不损失精度，为高效降阶建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Nonlinear-manifold reduced-order models for parametrized finite element problems can achieve substantial compression both in the number of generalized (latent) coordinates and, through sampling-and-weighting hyperreduction, in the number of sampled elements/integration points. Yet current sampling-and-weighting approaches employ weights that remain fixed over the solution manifold. We contend that this restriction leaves hyperreduction potential untapped: allowing the weights to vary continuously and nonlinearly with the latent coordinates can further decrease the number of sampled spatial entities. To exploit this possibility, we propose the Manifold-Adaptive-Weight Empirical Cubature Method (MAW-ECM). Starting from a feasible fixed-weight ECM rule, a greedy pruning strategy removes sampled entities through convex quadratic weight-redistribution problems enforcing local conditions and positivity. The method is assessed on two nonlinear benchmarks: homogenization of a metamaterial unit cell exhibiting negative incremental stiffness, and a history-dependent continuum-damage problem. In both cases, the nonlinear manifold is constructed from an initial linear compression followed by an input-informed identification of the latent coordinates as general linear combinations of the retained modal amplitudes, incorporating graph information when relevant to seek the intrinsic dimensionality of the solution manifold. We show that combining the nonlinear-manifold representation with MAW-ECM reduces the number of sampled integration points by more than two orders of magnitude relative to the corresponding standard linear reduced model. Relative to the fixed-weight manifold models alone, the adaptive weights eliminate approximately 80% of the remaining points in the homogenization benchmark and more than 97% in the damage benchmark, while essentially preserving their accuracy.

</details>


### [94] [Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion Method with Applications in Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03190)
*Maryam Bagherian,Albert Hung,Ivo Dinov,Joshua Welch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的张量补全框架CTTC，能够以张量形式融入模态间的辅助信息，提升生物医学中药物效应预测等任务的补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物医学问题可建模为张量补全问题，但现有方法仅能利用矩阵形式的辅助信息，限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion (CTTC) 框架，通过张量形式融合多模态辅助信息，并基于距离度量学习和群论设计交替优化算法求解。

Result: 在DTD和LINCS两个基准数据集上，CTTC在运行时间和RSE指标上均优于HaLRTC、CTRC、Cell和NTDDR等现有方法。

Conclusion: CTTC能有效利用张量形式的辅助信息，显著提升张量补全性能，尤其适用于药物效应预测等复杂生物医学任务。

Abstract: Many biomedical challenges can be posed as tensor completion problems where the observed entries of a multidimensional array (a tensor) are used to impute the missing values. In such settings, incorporating side information about the modes of the tensor, such as gene-gene similarity, can significantly enhance the solutions of the completion problem. Most existing tensor completion methods can only incorporate side information in the form of matrices. In this study, we introduce a novel framework to incorporate side information in the form of tensors. Our new approach, called Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion (CTTC), leverages the hidden connections among multimodal tensors to improve tensor completion performance. In addition to practical utility, CTTC has theoretical foundations in distance metric learning and group theory. We derive an alternating algorithm to solve the CTTC optimization problem and establish its convergence to a stationary point. Finally, we show that CTTC outperforms state-of-the-art tensor completion methods at predicting drug effects.
  Results: Compared with other tensor completion methods, including HaLRTC, CTRC, Cell, and NTDDR, CTTC demonstrates superior run-time and RSE tensor completion accuracy on two benchmark datasets, DTD and LINCS.

</details>


### [95] [A novel parallel approach for solving some free boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03214)

<details>

Method: 采用基于M-矩阵的直接并行方法确保解的唯一性；间接方法通过线性逼近每步最小化离散能量泛函，并结合Armijo步长规则；使用SPIKE算法的一般化快速递归版本处理一维问题，方向分裂法处理高维问题；并将框架拓展为约束二次规划求解器。

Result: 建立了直接和间接方法在光滑与非光滑能量泛函下的收敛性理论；实现了从一维到三维障碍问题的有效数值求解；成功应用于图像去模糊，恢复出原始图像；提供了数值实验验证理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行直接与间接迭代方法在理论上具有良好的收敛性，在计算上具备高效性，适用于多种障碍问题及实际应用如图像恢复，并展现出向优化问题拓展的潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel, efficient class of parallel direct and indirect iterative schemes to solve general obstacle and free boundary value problems. The uniqueness of the solution for the direct parallel method is established under the assumption that the model problem yields an $M$-matrix. The convergence analysis of the indirect approach is predicated on minimizing the discrete energy functional at each iterative stage of the linear approximation process. Under the aforementioned setting, we establish theoretical convergence results for both smooth and nonsmooth energy functionals defined over a convex set, in the sense of Ferris and Mangasarian \cite{ferris1994parallel}. For the numerical computation of the one-dimensional obstacle problem, we adopt a direct generalized parallel approach based on the SPIKE algorithm \cite{polizzi2006parallel}. To accelerate convergence and reduce computational complexity, a fast recursive version of the scheme is generalized to the nonsmooth case. For two- and three-dimensional obstacle problems, we employ a directional splitting method that treats each directional subproblem as a one-dimensional obstacle minimization problem. By framing the energy functional minimization as a parabolic time-dependent problem and utilizing an Armijo time-stepping rule during the solution update process, we successfully obtain results for higher-dimensional obstacles. Additionally, this study explores the possibility of extending the algorithm into a constrained quadratic programming optimization solver. We also evaluate the framework on image deblurring phenomena under the aforementioned setting, successfully recovering the original images. Finally, numerical illustrations are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>

Abstract: This paper addresses infinite-dimensional Bayesian inference for inverse problem of partial differential equations with model parameters in infinite-dimensional Hilbert space. To effectively incorporate prior information, we propose a novel continuous normalizing flows based infinite-dimensional model. Specifically, by introducing a well-defined neural ordinary differential equation in infinite-dimensional space, a simple reference measure can be transformed into a more complex measure which encodes the prior information. A corresponding theoretical framework is established to ensure the well-posedness of our proposed Bayesian prior in infinite-dimensional space. We also provide training methods of the prior for two distinct data settings, along with two sampling algorithms for the resulting Bayesian posterior. The proposed framework is applied to three representative inverse problems: the simple smooth inverse problem, inverse scattering problem, and the inverse heat conduction problem. Numerical experiments support the theoretical analysis and demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed algorithms.

</details>

Conclusion: 随机特征方法能够实现谱精度逼近，但其高精度特性伴随着严重的病态问题，二者源于相同的谱逼近机制，这为该方法的实际应用提供了理论基础与警示。

Abstract: We first prove spectral convergence of the random feature method (RFM) for multidimensional targets in Sobolev, Gevrey, ultra-analytic, and bandlimited classes. The analysis establishes general high-probability approximation estimates in the interpolation scale generated by a kernel integral operator. On a single event determined only by the sampled features, one random space approximates every target in a prescribed source ball; moreover, for each target, a single coefficient vector defines an approximant that attains spectral accuracy simultaneously in all admissible error norms. For both regularity-adapted frequency distributions and uniform distributions on growing frequency windows, the resulting rates range from super-exponential to algebraic, depending on the regularity of the target. Second, we establish abstract error estimates for strong- and weak-form RFM discretizations, thereby converting the preceding approximation bounds into convergence estimates for multidimensional second-order elliptic boundary value and eigenvalue problems. Finally, for random feature matrices (RFMtxs), we prove super-exponential singular-value decay with Fourier features and exponential decay with $\tanh$ features, together with corresponding condition-number lower bounds. The analysis identifies a common mechanism: the same spectral approximation that yields high accuracy also drives severe ill-conditioning.

</details>


### [98] [Semi-discrete quadratic Wasserstein energy and state-dependent Langevin exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03405)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解和优化半离散Wasserstein能量在存在站点碰撞时的行为，以及设计有效的随机优化算法以避免碰撞并收敛到全局最优解。

Method: 通过分析Wasserstein能量的梯度与Hessian结构，结合Laguerre单元的重心表达梯度，利用显式面公式给出Hessian；引入熵正则化的Langevin温度控制框架，并研究其对应的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程和Euler离散化链的遍历性。

Result: 证明了能量函数的局部Lipschitz连续性、全局半凹性、强制性和耗散性；所有全局极小值点均位于内部且无碰撞；在无碰撞区域具有C^2正则性；最优温度反馈是局部Lipschitz的；对于任意远离零的温度规则和足够小的步长，Gaussian Euler链是几何遍历的，最佳历史能量几乎必然收敛至全局最小值。

Conclusion: 该工作为半离散Wasserstein能量提供了完整的正则性理论基础，并提出了一类基于温度控制的可靠优化动力学，适用于高维无碰撞配置优化问题。

Abstract: We study the semi-discrete quadratic Wasserstein energy. The energy is nonsmooth at collisions of sites. We prove local Lipschitz continuity on the full configuration space, together with global semiconcavity, coercivity, and dissipativity; show that every global minimizer is interior and collision free; and establish $C^2$ regularity on the collision-free configuration space. The gradient is expressed through the barycenters of the balanced Laguerre cells, while the Hessian is given by an explicit facet formula and satisfies a global one-sided bound. We also solve the one-dimensional problem explicitly in each ordering chamber and give a two-site example on the unit square with non-minimizing Lloyd fixed points.
  For $d\ge 2$, we then formulate an entropy-regularized relaxed control of the Langevin temperature. The controlled dynamics is strongly well posed, nonexplosive, and collision free. Its value function is a classical interior solution of the exploratory Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation; the Laplacian of the value function is locally $C^1$, which yields a locally Lipschitz optimal temperature feedback. Independently of this optimal-control result, for every fixed Borel temperature rule bounded away from zero, and every sufficiently small step size, the associated Gaussian Euler chain is geometrically ergodic with a full-support invariant law. The raw iterates do not converge, whereas the best-so-far energy converges almost surely to the global minimum and the running record approaches the set of global minimizers.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptively-refinable polar-spline discrete differential forms: hierarchical construction, exactness, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03461)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等几何分析中，具有塌陷边奇点的参数化会导致B样条空间光滑性不足，限制了其在高阶偏微分方程和保持结构的离散化中的应用。本文旨在通过结合层次样条与极样条，解决这一问题并实现自适应细化。

Abstract: A common approach to representing disk-like and sphere-like geometries in isogeometric analysis is to use collapsed-edge singularities in the underlying parameterization. The resulting tensor-product B-spline spaces on such singular parameterizations lack the required smoothness to be used in isogeometric analysis. Polar splines (Toshniwal et al., CMAME 2017) rectify this issue by extracting a smoother subspace, and have been successfully used to discretize high-order partial differential equations, as well as to perform structure-preserving discretizations of the de Rham complex.The latter is particularly relevant for mixed formulations that appear in applications such as electromagnetism and fluid dynamics.
  The main contributions of this paper are twofold. We combine the polar spline construction with hierarchical splines to obtain adaptively-refinable polar splines. Moreover, we do so in the context of structure-preserving methods. That is, we show how to construct hierarchical polar spline $k$-form spaces, prove that the corresponding basis functions are linearly independent, and prove that they form a cohomologically-correct discrete de Rham complex.
  These contributions provide a mathematically sound foundation for adaptive structure-preserving simulations on polar geometries. We also provide numerical experiments that validate the theory and illustrate the practical behavior of the construction with problems where preserving the structure is mandatory. These include a study of spurious harmonics that highlights how cohomology-breaking refinements differ between the polar and tensor-product settings, a discretization of a Maxwell eigenvalue problem on a locally-refined polar mesh, and an adaptive convergence study for a Hodge--Laplace problem.

</details>

*Ilkhom Mukhammadiev,Diyora Salimova*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前馈神经网络已被证明能克服高维偏微分方程中的维度灾难，但残差网络在非线性PDE中的理论分析仍不充分。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究ResNets在半线性热方程中的表现。

Method: 通过将多层Picard估计器的一个确定性实现表示为ResNet，其中跳跃连接传递空间变量和标量累加器，残差分支逐步添加估计器的各个求和项，从而构造逼近解的网络结构。

Result: 在PDE数据满足多项式增长和网络可逼近性的假设下，存在具有至多ηd^ηε^{-η}个参数的ResNets，能在L²误差不超过ε的情况下逼近d维解；对于特定初始条件和激活函数，进一步得到参数数量的显式界C_ξd^{4+ξ}ε^{-(3+ξ)}。

Conclusion: ResNets能够有效克服半线性热方程数值求解中的维度灾难，且在合理假设下具备良好的逼近效率和理论保证。

Abstract: Rigorous results show that feedforward neural networks can overcome the curse of dimensionality in the numerical approximation of high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs), but comparatively little is known about residual neural networks (ResNets) in the nonlinear PDE setting. We prove that ResNets overcome the curse of dimensionality in the numerical approximation of solutions of semilinear heat equations with globally Lipschitz continuous, gradient-independent nonlinearities: under polynomial growth and network approximability hypotheses on the PDE data, there exist $η\in(0,\infty)$ and ResNets $Ψ_{d,\varepsilon}$, $d\in\mathbb{N}$, $\varepsilon\in(0,1]$, with at most $ηd^η\varepsilon^{-η}$ parameters whose realizations approximate the solution in dimension $d$ with an $L^2$-error of at most $\varepsilon$. The proof represents one deterministic realization of a multilevel Picard estimator by a ResNet whose shortcut connections transmit the spatial variable and a scalar accumulator, while the residual branches successively add the summands of the estimator. For ridge-sum initial conditions, admissible sigmoidal activations, and globally Lipschitz truncations of the nonlinearity, we obtain, for every $ξ>0$, the explicit bound $C_ξd^{4+ξ}\varepsilon^{-(3+ξ)}$ on the number of parameters.

</details>


### [101] [A fully globalized solver for discretized inverse elliptic coefficient problems with exact data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03631)
*Bastian Harrach*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的局部收敛算法及其全局优化变体，用于求解有限维非线性反问题，确保在有限步后自动切换到快速局部收敛算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆型反系数问题（如Calderón问题）具有强非线性和不适定性，传统数值求解器对初始值依赖性强，难以保证收敛。

Method: 设计了一种具有显式残差准则的局部收敛算法，并结合全局搜索策略形成全局化变体，确保在有限步后切换至局部快速收敛。

Result: 该算法在有限步内可自动从全局搜索过渡到局部收敛，提高了求解稳定性和效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效克服了非线性反问题对初值敏感的问题，具备良好的收敛保证和应用潜力。

Abstract: We consider finite-dimensional nonlinear inverse problems arising from finite element discretizations of elliptic inverse coefficient problems such as the Calderón problem with finitely many measurements and unknowns. Such inverse coefficient problems are notorious for their nonlinearity and ill-posedness, and numerical solvers tend to depend strongly on good initial values. In this work, we develop a new locally convergent algorithm with an explicit residual criterion that ensures convergence to the inverse problem solution, and a globalized variant that is guaranteed to automatically switch to the faster locally convergent algorithm after finitely many global search steps.

</details>


### [102] [Mini mixed finite element method for nearly incompressible linear elasticity problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03780)
*Zhijin Guan,Yue Feng,Hehu Xie,Chenguang Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mini元的混合有限元方法，用于求解近不可压缩线性弹性力学边值问题及其特征值问题，避免了传统低阶元在近不可压缩极限下的闭锁现象。理论分析和数值实验表明该方法在拉梅常数趋于无穷时仍具有最优收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准低阶有限元在近不可压缩极限下出现的体积闭锁问题，实现稳定、高效的数值模拟。

Method: 采用基于Mini元的混合有限元格式，并为特征值问题设计非嵌套增广子空间算法，在混合有限元框架下进行求解。

Result: 建立了混合变分格式的良好适定性，给出了关于拉梅常数一致的先验误差估计；对离散特征值逼近和迭代求解器进行了收敛性分析，证明收敛速率与拉梅常数无关；数值实验验证了理论结果，展示了最优收敛率和强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Mini元混合有限元方法结合增广子空间算法能有效、可靠地处理近不可压缩弹性力学问题，具备锁定自由特性且计算效率高。

Abstract: This paper addresses the numerical solution of nearly incompressible linear elasticity boundary value problems and their associated eigenvalue problems. A mixed finite element formulation based on Mini element is proposed to circumvent the locking phenomenon that plagues standard low-order elements in the nearly incompressible limit. For the boundary value problem, we establish the well-posedness of mixed variational formulation and derive a priori error estimates that are uniform with respect to the Lamé constant $\underlineλ$, thereby proving the method's locking-free property. For the eigenvalue problem, we develop an efficient non-nested augmented subspace algorithm designed within the mixed finite element framework. A comprehensive convergence analysis is provided for the discrete eigenvalue approximation and the proposed iterative solver, demonstrating that the convergence rates remain independent of $\underlineλ$. Numerical experiments on both problems confirm the theoretical conclusions, showing optimal convergence rates and robustness as $\underlineλ \to \infty$. The results validate the effectiveness of Mini element and proposed augmented subspace algorithm for reliable and efficient computation in the nearly incompressible regime.

</details>


### [103] [A stabilized scheme satisfying the discrete maximum principle for a time fractional convection-diffusion-reaction equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03808)
*Christos Pervolianakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维有界域上的时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程，提出了一种满足离散极值原理的稳定数值格式，并结合线性有限元和代数通量校正方法进行空间离散，采用L1方法进行时间离散，证明了半离散和全离散格式的适定性与极值原理，给出了非光滑初值下的误差估计，并通过数值实验验证了收敛阶和对层解的处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了准确求解具有非光滑初值的时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程，并保持物理意义上的极值原理，避免传统数值方法可能出现的非物理解。

Method: 结合线性有限元方法与代数通量校正技术构建稳定的半离散格式，使用能量估计方法分析误差，并采用L1方案进行时间离散以构造全离散格式。

Result: 建立了半离散格式的适定性和误差估计，证明了全离散格式的适定性与离散极值原理，数值实验验证了理论收敛阶并对含层解的问题表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的数值方法能有效求解时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程，在非光滑初值下保持稳定性和精度，同时满足离散极值原理，适用于具有复杂解结构的问题。

Abstract: We study a time fractional convection-diffusion-reaction equation in a bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^2$. A stabilized numerical scheme satisfying a discrete maximum principle is constructed by combining the conforming linear finite element method with the algebraic flux correction method. The resulting semi-discrete scheme is nonlinear, and its well-posedness is established. Assuming nonsmooth initial data, we derive error estimates for the semi-discrete scheme using energy arguments. For the temporal discretization, we employ the L1 method, obtaining a fully discrete scheme for which we prove well-posedness and the discrete maximum principle. We also present numerical experiments that validate the order of convergence as well as we test our schemes to solutions that possess layers.

</details>


### [104] [Closure complexity of longest-edge bisection for triangular meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04003)
*Yuwen Li,Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于最长边二分法的局部网格细化方法，并引入了串行最长边传播路径闭包策略，证明了自适应网格序列的累积闭包估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高三角形网格在自适应有限元分析中的效率与稳定性，需要对局部网格细化过程进行理论分析，尤其是控制网格复杂度的增长。

Method: 采用最长边二分法结合串行传播路径闭包策略，利用终端优先原则解决边选择的歧义；通过有限相似类定理将直径集合转化为有限个几何格点结构，并结合Binev-Dahmen-DeVore类型的收费论证来推导累积估计。

Result: 证明了对于任意自适应网格序列，其网格数量增长受标记边数之和的线性控制，即 #T_L - #T_0 ≲ Σ#M_ℓ，表明该方法具有良好的复杂度控制性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的最长边二分细化策略配合终端优先规则能有效保证网格细化过程的局部性和整体复杂度的最优增长，适用于高效的自适应数值计算。

Abstract: On triangular meshes, we analyze local mesh refinement based on longest-edge bisection equipped with the serial longest-edge propagation-path closure. Ties are resolved by terminal priority: if the incoming shared edge is a longest edge of the neighboring triangle, the pair is declared terminal and that edge is bisected. For every adaptive mesh sequence $\mathcal{T}_0, \mathcal{T}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{T}_L$ with marked subset sequence $\mathcal{M}_0, \mathcal{M}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{M}_{L-1}$, we prove the cumulative closure estimate $\#\mathcal{T}_L - \#\mathcal{T}_0 \lesssim \sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\#\mathcal{M}_\ell$. The proof has two ingredients. First, the finite-similarity-class theorem for planar longest-edge bisection turns the set of possible diameters into a finite union of $\sqrt{2}$-geometric lattices. Hence diameters grow by a uniform strict factor at every nonterminal step. This yields generation and spatial locality for all triangles created by a single mark. Second, a Binev--Dahmen--DeVore type charging argument converts this single-mark locality into the cumulative estimate.

</details>


### [105] [Conditioning of solutions to the Sylvester equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04050)
*Massimiliano Fasi,Behnam Hashemi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sylvester和Lyapunov方程解的条件数问题，表明即使系数矩阵良好条件，解仍可能极度病态，并给出了多种情形下的先验上界与下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Nick Higham提出的关于Sylvester和Lyapunov方程解的条件性问题，服务于数值算法的向后稳定性分析。

Method: 通过构造反例说明解的病态可能性，并利用矩阵指数、Zolotarev数等工具推导一般性先验上下界，在特定条件下（如对角化、对称正定、耗散性）给出更紧的上界估计。

Result: 证明了Sylvester方程的解可极度病态，即便所有输入矩阵及Kronecker和均良态；得到了多种结构下的条件数上下界，特别是在Lyapunov方程中当A为对称正定或严格耗散时的上界结果。

Conclusion: Sylvester和Lyapunov方程的解的条件性不能仅由系数矩阵的条件性决定，需结合具体结构进行分析，所得界限有助于理解数值算法的稳定性。

Abstract: We partially answer an open problem, posed by Nick Higham, concerning the conditioning of solutions to Sylvester and Lyapunov equations. The question arises in the backward stability analysis of numerical algorithms for these equations. We first show that the solution to the Sylvester equation $AX-XB = C$ can be arbitrarily ill-conditioned even if $A, B, C$ and the Kronecker sum $I \otimes A - B^T \otimes I$ are all perfectly conditioned. We then derive general a priori bounds on the condition number of the solution, as well as bounds for the Sylvester equation when $A$ and $B$ are diagonalizable. We also provide lower bounds involving matrix exponentials and Zolotarev numbers. For the Lyapunov equation $AX+XA^T = -C$, we obtain upper bounds in two settings: (i) when $A$ is symmetric positive definite while $C$ is symmetric negative definite, and (ii) when $A$ is strictly dissipative and $C$ is symmetric positive definite.

</details>


### [106] [A Multigrid Method for CutFEM and its Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04067)
*Michal Wichrowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于切割有限元离散化的几何多重网格的收敛性理论，采用顶点块平滑器处理非继承层级形式和物理域与活动域之间的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在切割有限元方法中由于网格切割导致的收敛性分析难题，尤其是在多层级方法中处理非继承形式和几何复杂性的问题。

Method: 利用离散延拓性质，建立两层收敛性界，并在额外假设下给出W循环界的分析；通过数值实验验证V循环的实际性能。

Result: 证明了关于网格尺寸和切割几何一致的两层收敛界，W循环需附加小条件；收敛常数随多项式次数p退化；降低幽灵罚参数可改善迭代次数；发现割裂模式的可见性随p指数下降。

Conclusion: 该理论为cutFEM框架下的几何多重网格法提供了坚实的收敛性基础，尽管收敛常数受p影响，但通过调整惩罚项可优化实际性能。

Abstract: We develop a convergence theory for geometric multigrid with vertex-patch smoothers applied to cut finite element discretizations of the Poisson problem. The framework addresses non-inherited level forms and the mismatch between the physical and active domains. Using the discrete extension property, we prove two-level convergence bounds uniform in the mesh size and the cut geometry, and W-cycle bounds under an additional smallness assumption on the two-level rate. The numerical experiments intentionally use the stronger V-cycle, for which no convergence bound is claimed here.
  The convergence constants degrade with the degree $p$. Lowering the ghost penalty improves iteration counts. An aligned two-cell model exhibits a semidefiniteness threshold of order $p^{-2}$, whereas the visibility scale of a degree-$p$ cut mode decreases exponentially. Experiments at the model threshold reduce the iteration counts, but do not establish an assembled-operator threshold.

</details>

### [107] [Global Multi-Maturity SPX-VIX Calibration Beyond Markovian Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04087)
*Atithi Acharya,Yue Sun,Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Charlie Che*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需马尔可夫拼接假设的全球性S&P 500与VIX波动率曲面联合校准框架，揭示了拼接法则可能丢失历史依赖信息，导致多周期衍生品定价差异，并通过增强的Bregman镜像下降算法在有限离散情形下保持对可观测矩的拟合精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Markov拼接方法因忽略历史路径依赖而导致全局可行路径律的局限，无法准确刻画跨期限依赖关系，从而影响多周期金融衍生品的定价一致性，本文旨在构建一个更普适且精确的联合校准框架。

Method: 提出块保持的SPX-Markov化方法，证明全局可行性与局部可行性等价；引入增强Bregman镜像下降算法，在有限离散系统中维持对报价矩的拟合，同时控制鞅和弥散残差；通过有限状态实例验证块保持性质及Markov化后的价格变化。

Result: 发现拼接法则仅为全局可行路径律的一个严格子集，Markov化会丢弃前期历史依赖信息，导致相同月度校准下多周期产品定价不同；数值实验显示该方法使最差拟合误差低于0.70波动点，显著优于循环行投影法约25倍；实证验证了跨期价格变动的存在性。

Conclusion: 精确的联合SPX-VIX校准需考虑非马尔可夫的历史依赖结构，仅依赖相邻期限信息不足以唯一确定路径律；引入跨周期信息、合适的目标函数或历史相关先验是实现合理定价的关键。

Abstract: We develop a global framework for joint S&P 500 (SPX)-VIX smile calibration across multiple maturities without the conditional-independence restriction induced by Markovian stitching. Exact local and global feasibility are equivalent: every globally feasible law has a block-preserving SPX-Markovization that leaves each monthly $(S_i,V_i,S_{i+1})$ law unchanged. Nevertheless, stitched laws can form a strict subset of globally feasible path laws because Markovization discards dependence on earlier history beyond the current SPX level. Adjacent smiles therefore cannot identify this dependence, and laws with identical monthly calibrations can price multi-period claims differently. Under the standard Markov reference, relative entropy selects the stitched minimum-information completion; non-Markov dependence requires cross-period information, an appropriate objective, or a history-dependent prior. For finite discretizations, we introduce an augmented-Bregman mirror-descent scheme. It preserves the fit to observable quote moments while controlling martingale and dispersion residuals. In a controlled infeasible affine system, this split keeps prescribed marginals about $25$ times tighter than cyclic row projection by exposing the discrepancy in the conditional rows. An exact finite-state example verifies block preservation and exhibits material cross-period price changes after Markovization. On smoothed SPX and VIX surfaces, numerical calculations illustrate a finite-budget penalty path: the worst fitted-smile error remains below $0.70$ volatility points across the reported sweep while the bulk conditional diagnostics improve substantially.

</details>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Markovian Shock-Source Tracing and Multidimensional Asset Roles in Exchange Rates, Gold Futures, and Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03437)
*Seung Ho Choi,Seoin Jang,Hyunwoo Lee,Hayoung Choi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该研究通过三个维度（直接溢出传导、稳态溯源动态和多步上游连通性）分析国际金融网络中主要汇率、黄金期货和比特币的跨资产关联性，发现传统净溢出分类不足以揭示资产角色差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越传统的净溢出传递者-接收者分类，更全面地刻画资产在金融网络中的系统性角色。

Method: 采用VAR-GFEVD估计收益溢出，并构建行随机马尔可夫核进行反向溯源分析，结合稳态离开流与Viral Centrality算法评估多步上游可达性。

Result: 黄金期货是主要的直接净溢出源和稳态溯源节点，但Viral Centrality并非最高；部分被归类为净接收者的汇率具有较高Viral Centrality，表明其对上游源节点有广泛条件访问能力；比特币处于中间地位。引入美元指数后结果保持稳健。

Conclusion: 直接连通性、稳态溯源和多步上游影响无法仅从净溢出推断，需结合多种分析维度，且所有解释依赖于模型设定与网络构造假设。

Abstract: This study examines cross-asset connectedness in an international financial network of major exchange rates, gold futures, and Bitcoin. Moving beyond the conventional net transmitter--receiver classification, we characterize asset roles through three complementary dimensions: direct spillover transmission, stationary source-tracing dynamics, and multistep upstream connectivity. Return spillovers are estimated using VAR generalized forecast-error variance decomposition (VAR-GFEVD). The positive pairwise net-spillover structure is then mapped into a row-stochastic Markov kernel whose transitions trace dominant net-spillover sources in the reverse direction of the original transmission edges. A stationary departure flux describes long-run movement in this source-tracing chain, while Viral Centrality is evaluated by the deterministic probability-propagation algorithm of Fink et al. to approximate multistep upstream reach. Empirically, gold futures emerge as the dominant direct net transmitter and the leading stationary source-tracing node, but do not have the largest Viral Centrality. Several exchange-rate nodes classified as direct net receivers have relatively high Viral Centrality, indicating broad conditional access to upstream source nodes. Bitcoin occupies an intermediate role. An auxiliary specification including the U.S. Dollar Index yields qualitatively similar role differentiation. The results show that direct connectedness, stationary source tracing, and approximate multistep upstream reach cannot be inferred from NET spillovers alone. All diffusion interpretations are descriptive and conditional on the estimated network, row normalization, and restart closure.

</details>


### [109] [Calibration of neural viscoelastic models via full-field data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03645)
*Brain M. Riemer,Markus Kästner,Karl A. Kalina*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于全场数据的无监督学习框架，用于校准物理增强神经网络（PANN）在小应变粘弹性中的应用，仅需实验中可直接获取的全局反力和表面位移进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决传统模型依赖大量标注数据且难以保证热力学一致性的问题，提出一种结合物理约束与数据驱动的无监督方法，以提升材料建模的准确性与物理合理性。

Method: 将PANN嵌入广义标准材料理论，利用自由能和对偶耗散势的不变量表示确保热力学一致性和材料对称性；基于平衡间隙法构建约束优化问题，采用拟牛顿优化器与自动微分，并通过后向伴随法高效计算梯度以降低训练成本。

Result: 在合成数据（含噪声位移和力）上验证了该框架的有效性，显示其在多种变形速率和加载路径下均能实现高精度预测。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效利用实验可测变量进行无监督训练，实现了热力学一致、对称性保持且计算高效的粘弹性本构建模，具有良好的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose an unsupervised learning framework for calibrating a physics-augmented neural network (PANN) for small-strain viscoelasticity via full-field data. It only requires quantities that are directly accessible in real experiments for training, namely global reaction forces and surface displacements. The underlying PANN is embedded in the generalized standard materials theory, in which two scalar-valued potentials render the constitutive model thermodynamically consistent by construction, while invariant-based representations of the free energy and the dual dissipation potential additionally ensure material symmetry. Considering a thin specimen under the plane stress assumption, we formulate a constrained optimization problem based on the equilibrium gap method in combination with quasi-Newton optimizers and automatic differentiation. Thereby, the unknown out-of-plane strain follows from the plane stress condition and the evolution of the internal variables is captured by an implicit time integration scheme. The resulting system of nonlinear equations is solved via a local Newton iteration at quadrature point and time step. To drastically reduce the computational cost of training, the backward adjoint method is employed to compute the gradient of the target loss, instead of backpropagating through all Newton iteration steps. The proposed framework is demonstrated for synthetic data, including noisy displacements and forces, showing excellent agreement across a wide range of deformation rates and load paths.

</details>


### [110] [What Do CAE Simulation Agents Really Need Beyond a Generic Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03718)
*Jiasheng Shi,Tianhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 现代大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在计算机辅助工程（CAE）仿真中展现出强大潜力，通过通用框架即可高效完成自然语言到仿真的转换，无需复杂的多智能体结构；研究发现执行反馈修复和领域知识（如求解器教程）是提升性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在固定信息访问和修复预算下，现代通用LLM智能体框架是否足以替代专门设计的多智能体CAE系统，并识别对CAE仿真代理性能提升真正关键的因素。

Method: 基于FoamBench基准，比较单智能体通用框架与多智能体专用系统的性能表现，并进行消融实验分析执行反馈修复、脚本化反思和领域知识等组件的作用。

Result: 单智能体通用框架在FoamBench上达到96.4%的性能，优于多智能体专用系统的88.2%；执行反馈修复显著提升效果（从71.8%到96.4%），而脚本化反思无增益；引入求解器教程形式的领域知识带来最大增益（从80.9%到96.4%）。

Conclusion: 现代通用LLM智能体框架已具备支持高效CAE仿真的核心能力，执行反馈和领域知识注入是关键，复杂的多智能体架构和脚本化反思不再是必需。

Abstract: Computer-aided engineering (CAE) simulation is among the largest and most demanding areas of engineering, where setting up a solver such as OpenFOAM, FEniCS, or COMSOL takes real expertise. Large language model (LLM) agents promise to turn a natural-language request into a working simulation, and recent CAE agents add simulation-specific machinery: multi-agent decomposition, domain retrieval, and scripted reflection. That machinery suited weak base models; modern harnesses already supply multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and execution feedback. We ask what a CAE simulation agent still needs beyond a generic harness. With information access and repair budget held fixed, a single-agent harness matches or beats multi-agent specialized systems (FoamBench 96.4\% vs.\ 88.2\%).
  Ablations trace this to capabilities the harness already provides: execution-feedback repair lifts FoamBench from 71.8\% with no repair round to 96.4\%, while scripted reflection adds nothing. The one input that still helps is domain knowledge supplied as solver tutorials, our largest measured gain (80.9\% to 96.4\%).

</details>


### [111] [sp-DBA: a general framework for adaptive transform-domain computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03922)
*Jingkun Jiang,Pingchuan Deng,Yang Xia*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为sp-DBA的自适应加速框架，用于变换域工作流，能够在保持数值精度的同时实现高达28.1倍的局部加速和8.4倍的整体加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变换域自适应方法常因引入新数据结构或需重构流程而难以在大规模并行硬件上高效运行。

Method: 提出谱动态块激活（sp-DBA）框架，动态激活变换域中的计算块，集中资源于关键区域，兼容现有优化变换操作。

Result: 在材料科学、生物学和光通信的工作流中，实现最高28.1倍的变换域更新加速和8.4倍的整体加速；在最多八块GPU上的强/弱扩展测试表明其在分布式FFT中仍有效。

Conclusion: sp-DBA无需重构现有求解器即可引入执行级自适应性，拓展了现代并行系统上科学模拟和信号处理的规模、持续时间和复杂度。

Abstract: Transform-domain methods simplify analysis and computation, making them central to scientific computing and signal processing. However, existing adaptive strategies often introduce new data structures or require substantial workflow redesign, limiting efficient execution on massively parallel hardware. Here we present spectral dynamic block activation (sp-DBA), an adaptive acceleration framework for transform-domain workflows. sp-DBA dynamically activates transform-domain computation blocks as a calculation evolves, concentrating computation where it is needed while retaining numerical accuracy and optimized transform operations. Across representative workflows in materials science, biology, and optical communications, the demonstrated implementations achieve speedups of up to 28.1-fold for transform-domain updates and up to 8.4-fold overall acceleration; strong- and weak-scaling tests on up to eight GPUs show that the local adaptive update remains effective within distributed FFT workflows. By introducing execution-level adaptivity into mature transform-domain workflows without rebuilding existing solvers, sp-DBA extends the scale, duration, and complexity of scientific simulations and signal-processing calculations on modern parallel systems.

</details>


### [112] [Differentiable Hybrid Modelling for Learning and Optimising Chemical Transport Processes from Experimental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04011)
*Arthur Jessop,Mohammed Alsubeihi,Ben Moseley,Ashwin Kumar Rajagopalan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的可微分混合建模框架，结合JAX有限体积法与神经网络，用于从实验数据中学习群体平衡方程的本构关系和初始条件，并实现过程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输运模型依赖人为选定的本构关系和精确已知的初始条件，可能导致预测偏差和系统性误差；纯黑箱神经网络虽能拟合数据但缺乏物理一致性。需要一种兼具数据驱动灵活性和物理可解释性的建模方法。

Method: 构建一个可微分的混合建模范式，将JAX实现的有限体积法求解器与可学习的神经网络模块结合，联合训练以同时发现本构规律并拟合实验数据中的初始条件，并利用其可微性进行过程优化。

Result: 该框架能够有效从真实实验数据中学习群体平衡方程的未知本构关系和初始状态，提升模型预测准确性，并成功应用于目标变量的实验条件优化。

Conclusion: 可微分混合建模框架在化学分离等涉及质量、能量和动量传递的过程建模与优化中具有巨大潜力，实现了数据驱动学习与物理机理融合的统一。

Abstract: Reliable transport models are essential when modelling and optimising many chemical engineering processes, yet, most models assume hand-picked constitutive laws which may not reflect reality, and often assume initial conditions are known exactly. Both restrictions can significantly bias model predictions and lead to systematic error when used in predictive and control settings. Black-box neural surrogate alternatives for modelling can better match real example data, but are confined to the task they were trained on and cannot be interrogated for physical consistency. Here we introduce a general-purpose differentiable hybrid modelling framework for transport processes, specifically for the case of population balance equations. Our framework integrates a JAX finite volume population balance solver with learnable neural network components which are trained to both discover constitutive laws and fit initial conditions from real experimental data, allowing us to better model real experimental transport systems. Furthermore, we use our framework for process optimisation, using its differentiability to allow us to direct optimising experimental settings for quantities of interest. This work highlights the huge potential of such differentiable hybrid modelling frameworks for learning and optimising any given chemical separation which involves mass, energy, and/or momentum transport.

</details>


### [113] [Influence of Extruded Filament Shape on Buildability in 3D Concrete Printing: A Geometry-Informed Deep Learning-FEM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04028)
*Giacomo Rizzieri,Saif-Ur-Rehman,Jörg F. Unger,Annika Robens-Radermacher*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种几何信息建模框架，结合深度学习预测与层激活有限元方法，评估3D混凝土打印中真实纤维几何形状对可建造性的影响，验证了几何细节的重要性并提供了高效准确的建模建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限元方法常将3D混凝土打印层简化为矩形，可能影响可建造性预测的准确性，因此需要更真实的几何表示方法。

Method: 结合深度学习工具ShapeGen3DCP与层激活有限元法，生成基于材料与工艺参数的真实纤维几何模型，并通过实验数据验证及参数化研究进行分析。

Result: 真实纤维几何显著影响可建造性预测，尤其在自由流动沉积中；椭圆近似在保真度与简化之间达到良好平衡；基于体积守恒定义的矩形层比基于最大宽度或层间接触宽度的定义更具预测可靠性。

Conclusion: 将纤维几何纳入3D混凝土打印模拟至关重要，研究为可建造性评估中的几何表示选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The geometric morphology of deposited filaments can significantly influence the structural performance and stability of 3D concrete-printed (3DCP) structures. However, most finite element (FEM)-based approaches for buildability assessment represent printed layers as simplified rectangles, potentially limiting predictive accuracy. This study proposes a geometry-informed modelling framework that integrates the deep-learning-based filament shape prediction tool ShapeGen3DCP with a layer-activation FEM approach to investigate the effect of realistic filament geometries on buildability. The framework generates geometry-aware numerical models directly from material and process parameters, eliminating the need for experimental filament characterization or computationally intensive fluid-flow simulations. Validation against experimental data and a parametric study of rectilinear walls demonstrate that extrusion parameters and the resulting filament geometry can significantly influence buildability predictions. Realistic filament representations are particularly important for free-flow deposition, whereas layer-pressing strategies are less sensitive to geometric simplifications. Among the investigated representations, an elliptical approximation provides an effective balance between geometric fidelity and modelling simplicity. When rectangular representations are preferred to enable regular computational meshes for faster simulations, defining their dimensions based on volume conservation improves prediction reliability compared with calibrating them using either the maximum filament width or the interlayer contact width. Overall, the proposed methodology demonstrates the importance of incorporating filament geometry into 3DCP simulations and provides practical guidance for selecting efficient and accurate geometric representations for buildability assessment.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Computing with qLDPC Codes by Climbing the Chain Map Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02999)
*Rahul Sahay,David M. Long,Vedika Khemani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于qLDPC码逻辑计算的框架，通过链映射层次结构统一描述Pauli、Clifford和非Clifford逻辑操作，推动新逻辑门的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Pauli逻辑操作的简单同调描述扩展到Clifford和非Clifford操作，以统一和简化qLDPC码上的逻辑计算。

Method: 引入链映射层次结构——一族链复形，其同调类编码Clifford阶层中任意层级的逻辑酉操作和码手术操作，并利用可变形的Pauli逻辑操作寻找常数深度的非Clifford逻辑门实现。

Result: 发现了2D环面码上全逻辑Clifford群的常数深度酉实现，以及3D环面码上可寻址的逻辑CCZ门；并将方法推广至多编码量子比特的非流形码，实现了可寻址的非Clifford门。

Conclusion: 该框架为qLDPC码上的逻辑操作提供了统一、直观且实用的语言，有助于系统化设计和发现新的逻辑门。

Abstract: We develop a framework for logical computation with qLDPC codes that places logical Pauli, Clifford, and non-Clifford operations on the same footing. This brings the simple homological description of Pauli logicals to the patchwork landscape of logical Clifford and non-Clifford operations, providing a tool for the discovery of new logical gates. In particular, we define the chain map hierarchy: a family of chain complexes whose homology classes encode logical unitary and code surgery operations at any level of the Clifford hierarchy, precisely analogous to the chain complex description of Pauli logicals. Consequently, intuition for Pauli logicals can be leveraged to discover new logical operations on qLDPC codes. For instance, the familiar ability to deform Pauli logicals with stabilizers---i.e. boundaries of the chain complex---becomes a way to search for constant-depth unitary implementations of (non-)Clifford logical gates. Using this strategy, we discover constant-depth unitary implementations of the full logical Clifford group on any number of blocks of the 2D toric code, including within a single block, and addressable logical CCZ gates on any triple of logical qubits on any number of blocks of the 3D toric code. Beyond manifold codes, we find addressable non-Clifford gates on codes with many encoded qubits. The chain map hierarchy naturally encompasses and extends other constructions of logical gadgets, for instance providing a universal parameterization of cup products. As such, our work provides a unified, useful, and intuitive language for computing with qLDPC codes.

</details>


### [115] [Shuttling-aware dynamical decoupling for quantum charge-coupled devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03014)
*Linus Schulte,Aaron Sander,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向离子传输的动态解耦编译策略（SADD），通过联合优化重聚焦脉冲与局部离子重路由，在考虑传输约束的同时提升量子计算中的最终态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态解耦方法未考虑QCCD架构中离子传输对脉冲时序和相位积累的影响，导致保护效果下降，因此需要一种能协同调度脉冲与传输的方案。

Method: 提出SADD方法，作为一个离线编译步骤，联合选择重聚焦脉冲并进行局部离子重路由，同时保持逻辑门时序和总调度长度不变。

Result: 在去相位主导且噪声变化较慢的条件下，SADD相比原始调度和最近可行的Hahn回波基线显著提高了平均最终态保真度；利用重路由可实现原本不可行的脉冲时序，结合空间噪声信息还能进一步增益，但若传输引入过多误差则增益消失。

Conclusion: 协调动态解耦与离子传输是一种有效减少QCCD处理器中去相位效应的编译策略。

Abstract: Dynamical decoupling (DD) helps maintain high-fidelity quantum computations by suppressing dephasing noise through carefully timed refocusing pulses. In quantum charge-coupled device (QCCD) architectures, however, where ions are shuttled throughout the device, transport constrains when pulses can be applied and affects the phase accumulated by an ion. Conventional DD methods do not account for shuttling and may therefore schedule pulses that must be omitted or shifted after transport scheduling, weakening the protection from dephasing. We therefore introduce shuttling-aware dynamical decoupling (SADD), an offline compiler pass that jointly selects refocusing pulses and local ion rerouting while preserving logical-gate timings and the total schedule length. In benchmark simulations, SADD improves average final-state fidelity over both the original schedules and a simple nearest-feasible Hahn-echo baseline when dephasing dominates control and transport errors and varies slowly enough for DD. Rerouting enables otherwise infeasible pulse timings, while spatial information about the noise can provide further gains. These benefits disappear, however, when the added transport introduces too much error. Overall, our results show that coordinating DD with ion transport is an effective compiler strategy for reducing dephasing in QCCD processors.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum low-density lattice codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03021)
*Timo Hillmann,Jens Eisert,Francesco Arzani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低密度晶格码（LDLC）的GKP量子纠错码设计新方法，通过协同设计解码器与编码本身，实现了在无限维玻色模式中高效编码离散量子信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码在离散变量码级联下已有较多研究，但原生GKP码的构造与解码因计算复杂度高而困难，本文旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出协同设计策略：基于经典低密度晶格码（LDLC）构建适用于GKP框架的量子码，并利用模拟消息传递解码器进行高效解码。

Result: 所构造的GKP-LDLC码在模式数相当时，性能优于或相当于GKP-表面码；且使用类比消息传递解码器可实现接近现有最先进混合解码器的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将经典LDLC提升为GKP码并采用原生模拟解码器，展示了高效、线性时间解码的可行性，推动了原生GKP码的发展。

Abstract: Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes provide a family of promising schemes for encoding discrete quantum information (qudits) into infinite-dimensional bosonic modes based on mathematical lattices. While such codes, when concatenated with discrete-variable codes, are relatively well studied, the construction and decoding of native GKP codes has largely remained open due to the computationally hard problems encountered. To address this challenge, we advocate a strategy of co-designing the decoder and the quantum error-correcting code itself by constructing lattices for which decoding is feasible: The requirement of efficient decoding effectively determines the quantum error-correcting code. This construction is built on classical low-density lattice codes (LDLCs), a lattice analogue of low-density parity-check codes, here lifted to families of GKP codes. Concretely, we introduce quantum versions of classical, randomly constructed LDLCs. We show that after suitable dimensionality reduction these codes have code properties comparable to or better than concatenated GKP-surface codes of equal number of modes. However, the GKP-LDLCs constructed here do not have a strictly sparse parity check matrix, which motivates our study of the performance of natively analog message-passing decoders originally developed for LDLCs when applied to concatenated GKP-LDPC codes. We show that the fully analog, linear-time decoder achieves performances close to state-of-the-art hybrid qubit-analog decoders. To facilitate future research on the structure and performance of general GKP codes, the relevant source code will be released in open-source Julia packages LatticeDecoder.jl and SymplecticGKP.jl.

</details>


### [117] [Nanoscale magnetometry via collective many-body dynamics in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03039)
*Haoyang Gao,Piotr Put,Nathaniel T. Leitao,Nazlı U. Köylüoğlu,Andrew Maccabe,Mathew Mammen,Siddharth Dandavate,Lillian B. Hughes Wyatt,Leigh S. Martin,Ania C. Bleszynski Jayich,Hongkun Park,Mikhail D. Lukin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用室温固态中强相互作用的电子自旋系综，通过脉冲磁场梯度控制多体动力学，实现了高达7.9 dB的磁信号检测增益和8.8 dB的磁噪声传感增益，并展示了可调纳米尺度（低至50纳米）空间分辨的磁噪声探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多体量子系统在实际环境中用于高灵敏度磁传感的潜力，克服目前相互作用增强型量子传感器应用受限的难题。

Method: 在室温下使用约10^4个氮空位（NV）中心组成的偶极耦合自旋系综，结合脉冲磁场梯度实现对集体多体动力学的相干调控，进行磁信号与磁噪声的检测。

Result: 实现了7.9(2) dB的磁信号检测增益和8.8(3) dB的磁噪声传感增益，并开发出可在动量空间分辨、长度尺度连续可调（最低50纳米）的空间相关磁噪声探测方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于相互作用增强的量子传感器在纳米级生物成像和材料表征中的实际应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Many-body dynamics constitutes a promising approach for creating correlations between quantum particles which can be used for applications in sensing and metrology. However, utilizing this potential for substantial gains in practical settings is a challenging task with only a very few applications realized to date. Here, we demonstrate an approach to nanoscale magnetic sensing enabled by strongly interacting electronic spins in a room temperature solid. By coherently controlling collective many-body dynamics of a dipolar ensemble of $\sim 10^4$ nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centres in diamond with pulsed magnetic field gradients, we demonstrate practical metrological gain up to $7.9(2)\,\mathrm{dB}$ for magnetic signal detection and $8.8(3)\,\mathrm{dB}$ for magnetic noise sensing, fully accounting for experimental overheads. Finally, we combine these methods to demonstrate a momentum-space-resolved sensing modality that enables detection of spatially correlated magnetic noise at continuously tunable length scales down to 50 nanometers. These observations open the door toward practical applications of interaction-enhanced quantum sensors for nanoscale biological imaging and material characterization.

</details>


### [118] [Approximate cubic phase states in a trapped ion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03032)
*C. Ventura-Velázquez,Juan Mauricio Torres*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在束缚离子振动模式中生成具有立方相位态特征的量子态的方法，利用非线性Jaynes-Cummings相互作用，在初始为高占据数相干态的条件下，实现了对立方相位态的高保真度近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现基于连续变量的通用量子计算，需要引入非高斯操作，而立方相位态作为一种三阶非高斯资源态是关键。然而如何有效制备此类态仍具挑战。

Method: 利用束缚离子系统中原生的非线性Jaynes-Cummings相互作用，从一个高占据数的初始相干振动态出发，演化生成近似的立方相位态，并通过非线性正交分量组合的方差来表征该态。

Result: 生成的振动态在Wigner函数中展现出与立方相位态一致的振荡图案，且具有高保真度；同时给出了其‘立方性’的解析表达式，表明该近似态性能优异。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在合适的初始条件和相互作用下，束缚离子系统可自然演化出接近理想立方相位态的非高斯态，为连续变量量子计算提供了潜在的非高斯资源制备方案。

Abstract: Universal quantum computation with continuous variables cannot be attained solely with a set of Gaussian operations, it requires the addition of a non-Gaussian element, at least of third order in the quadrature operators, such as the cubic phase state. In this work, we present a method to generate a quantum state in the vibrational mode of a trapped ion that exhibits characteristics compatible with the cubic phase state, such as the distinctive oscillating pattern in its Wigner function. This state emerges from the nonlinear Jaynes-Cummings interaction native to the trapped-ion model, and under the assumption of an initial coherent vibrational state with a large occupation number. Consequently, the evolved vibrational state approximates the cubic phase state with high fidelity, and we use the variance of a nonlinear combination of the quadratures to characterize it. Finally, we provide an analytical expression for its cubicity that shows the high performance of the approximate vibrational cubic phase state.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser Quantum Sensors for Quantum Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03092)
*Naga Dileep Varikuti,Sourav Manna,Athreya Shankar,Arul Lakshminarayan,Vaibhav Madhok*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了非KAM系统在量子传感中的应用，发现共振条件下量子费舍信息（QFI）显著增强，且其增长与激发数的幂律行为相关，揭示了共振诱导的平移对称性作为一种新型计量资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非KAM系统在弱时间依赖扰动下进入经典混沌的特性，并利用其在共振时的高度敏感性提升量子传感性能。

Method: 结合数值分析与解析方法，计算非KAM系统中的量子费舍信息（QFI），研究其在弗洛凯幺正编码下的频率估计性能，并推导QFI的增长界限。

Result: 发现当满足共振条件时，QFI显著增强；根据平均激发数⟨n̂(t)⟩∼t^α，QFI满足I(t)≲t^(2α+2)；量子 kicked 谐振子在不同动力学 regime 下实现二次、四次和异常六次（t^6）增长，其中六次增长在R=2处解析证明，在R=4处数值验证。

Conclusion: 非KAM系统的共振诱导平移对称性可作为独立于混沌或临界性的新型计量资源，为高精度量子传感提供了新途径。

Abstract: Non-KAM (Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser) systems, when subjected to weak time-dependent perturbations, exhibit an abrupt transition to classical chaos through the breakdown of invariant phase-space tori. We showcase the utilization of non-KAM systems in the quantum regime as quantum sensors, leveraging their sensitivity at \textit{resonances}. Quantum Fisher information (QFI) is a central quantity in quantum parameter estimation theory that measures how much information a quantum state contains about an unknown parameter that is encoded into it. In other words, it quantifies the sensitivity of a quantum state to small changes in that parameter. In this work, through numerical analysis in conjunction with analytical results, we study the performance of the non-KAM systems for quantum sensing applications by computing the QFI. We find that the growth of the QFI is remarkably enhanced when the resonance condition is satisfied. For frequency estimation under Floquet unitary encodings, we derive a transport bound: if the mean excitation number grows as $\langle\hat n(t)\rangle\sim t^α$, the QFI obeys $I(t)\lesssim t^{2α+2}$. The quantum kicked harmonic oscillator, a paradigmatic non-KAM system, realizes the full hierarchy: localized dynamics ($α=0$) yield quadratic growth, delocalized diffusion along stochastic webs ($α=1$) yields quartic growth, and translationally invariant resonances ($α=2$) saturate the bound with anomalous hexic growth, $I(t)\sim t^{6}$, established analytically at resonance $R=2$ and numerically at $R=4$. The enhancement stems from resonance-induced translational symmetry rather than exponential instability, identifying non-KAM resonances as a metrological resource distinct from chaos-assisted and criticality-based sensing.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum Quasi-Monte Carlo: a window for pre-asymptotic quantum advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03625)
*Paolo Recchia,Zhan Yu,Kelvin Koor,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子拟蒙特卡洛算法，结合低差异性网和量子振幅估计，在有限查询的实际场景中实现了对目标精度的预渐近优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典蒙特卡洛方法计算成本高，现有量子加速方法未探索拟蒙特卡洛路径，旨在通过结合低差异性网与量子振幅估计提升效率。

Method: 将准随机点集以叠加态形式相干制备，并结合量子振幅估计技术进行数值积分。

Result: 该方法在渐近意义上未超越经典拟蒙特卡洛，但在预渐近区域可通过调节电路分辨率和振幅估计参数减少函数查询次数。

Conclusion: 所提方法为实际有限查询场景下的量子数值积分提供了可调控的性能优势，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Numerical integration with Monte Carlo methods is a central computational task in many scientific and industrial applications, including financial derivative pricing and risk management. Classical Monte Carlo algorithms are computationally demanding: achieving an accuracy $ε$ typically requires a number of function evaluations scaling as $O(1/ε^2)$. Quantum-accelerated Monte Carlo methods based on quantum amplitude estimation can in principle quadratically improve this dependence. However, \textit{quasi}-Monte Carlo methods have not been explored in the quantum context. In this work, we introduce a quantum quasi-Monte Carlo algorithm that combines low-discrepancy nets with quantum amplitude estimation. The proposed method prepares the quasi-random point set coherently in superposition. The method does not yield an asymptotic improvement over classical quasi-Monte Carlo, since the total error separates into a discretization error, determined by the finite net, and a quantum estimation error. Instead, we explore a pre-asymptotic advantage window: for a target accuracy that would classically require $2^q$ low discrepancy points, one can prepare a higher-resolution net of size $2^Q$, with $Q>q$, in superposition and reach the same accuracy using significantly fewer function queries. This window can be controlled by tuning the circuit resolution and amplitude-estimation parameters, making the approach relevant for practical regimes where the number of queries is finite rather than asymptotically large.

</details>


### [121] [Discrete time crystals in disordered anisotropic Heisenberg chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04037)
*Francesco Formicola,Grazia Di Bello,Antonio De Candia,Giulio De Filippis,Carmine Antonio Perroni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过基于矩阵乘积态方法的大规模数值模拟，研究了在周期性驱动下具有海森堡相互作用的强无序自旋链中的离散时间晶体行为。研究发现，在多体局域化体系中，δ脉冲诱导的旋转会在自旋观测量中产生显著的次谐波响应，表现出时间晶体特性，并揭示了一个介于时间晶体与弗洛凯局域化响应之间的中间动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强无序自旋系统中离散时间晶体的存在及其稳定性，特别是在存在控制误差的情况下，以加深对非平衡物相和时间平移对称性自发破缺的理解。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的数值模拟方法，分析周期驱动下含海森堡相互作用的无序自旋链，计算包括纠缠熵、量子费舍尔信息、短程自旋关联和受限控制厄果量等指标，评估系统的时间晶体响应及其鲁棒性。

Result: 在多体局域化体系中观察到明显的半频次谐波响应，表明存在离散时间晶体行为；随着旋转角度误差增大，系统进入一个介于时间晶体与弗洛凯局域化之间的中间动力学区域，该区域的动力学特征类似于弱相关和安德森局域化现象。

Conclusion: 强无序海森堡自旋链在周期驱动下可展现出离散时间晶体行为，且在一定误差范围内保持稳定，中间动力学区域的发现为理解非平衡相变提供了新视角。

Abstract: A discrete time crystal is an out-of-equilibrium phase of matter characterized by the spontaneous breaking of discrete time-translation symmetry. Using extensive numerical simulations based on matrix-product-state methods, we provide evidence for discrete time-crystalline behavior in strongly disordered spin chains with Heisenberg interactions, including the isotropic point, subject to periodic driving. Starting from a many-body localized regime, we observe that rotations induced by delta kicks produce a pronounced subharmonic response at half the drive frequency in spin observables. We investigate the stability of this response against rotation-angle errors through entanglement entropy, quantum Fisher information, short-range spin correlations, and restricted-control ergotropy. Increasing the rotation error reveals an intermediate dynamical regime separating the time-crystalline and Floquet-localized responses. In this regime, most observables exhibit signatures of weakly correlated dynamics reminiscent of Anderson localization.

</details>


### [122] [Stabilization of dark states in emitter arrays coupled to a half-waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03040)
*Oriol Rubies-Bigorda,Susanne F. Yelin,Ana Asenjo-Garcia,Stuart J. Masson*

Main category: quant-ph
