TL;DR: 该研究展示了通过优化每个量子发射器的频率偏移，可在波导-镜面结构中实现完美的单激发暗态及近完美的多激发暗态，并可通过经典驱动场或少光子脉冲高保真度地确定性制备这些集体态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用电磁环境调控量子发射器的辐射特性，以实现长寿命的量子态存储和可控的量子相互作用。

Method: 理论分析结合数值优化，研究在波导一端加镜面的情况下，多个远距离量子发射器通过虚光子交换形成的集体态，并施加频率偏移来稳定暗态。

Result: 实现了单激发完美暗态（零衰变速率）和多激发近完美暗态的高保真度确定性制备。

Conclusion: 该方案可在超导量子比特平台上实现，为量子信息存储、量子网络和光控提供了新途径。

Abstract: The radiative properties of quantum emitters are profoundly influenced by their electromagnetic environment. When coupled to a waveguide terminated at one end by a mirror, distant emitters interact strongly via virtual photon exchange, leading to collective superradiant and subradiant states with enhanced or suppressed decay rates. We demonstrate that applying optimal frequency shifts to each emitter enables the formation of perfect single-excitation dark states (i.e. states with zero decay rate) and near-perfect multi-excitation dark states in small ensembles. These collective states can be deterministically prepared with high fidelity using classical driving fields or few-photon pulses propagating along the waveguide. These results, readily implementable in superconducting qubit platforms, open new avenues for quantum information storage, networking and control of light.

</details>


### [123] [Robust Hamiltonian engineering with subensemble control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03045)
*Wenjie Gong,Matteo Votto,Soonwon Choi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于全局控制脉冲序列的鲁棒协议，用于并行重塑多个自旋子系综中的相互作用，并提供了设计抗干扰目标哈密顿量的有效方法，适用于量子模拟与传感实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固体缺陷或多种原子阵列的多子系综系统中，缺乏有效方法同时调控子系综内外的相互作用，本文旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 通过分析控制脉冲序列的设计复杂性，提出必要或充分条件，并开发高效的数值策略来合成目标哈密顿量，实现对常见控制误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了寻找同时工程化子系综内外相互作用的脉冲序列是计算上困难的，但找到了可实现目标哈密顿量的条件，并成功应用于双组分原子系综中两模自旋压缩的鲁棒生成。

Conclusion: 该研究为最小化控制开销下的新型量子模拟和传感实验提供了实用工具箱。

Abstract: We present a robust protocol to reshape interactions in a spin ensemble based on global control pulse sequences applied to multiple subensembles in parallel. This setting arises naturally from ensembles of solid-state defects or multi-species atomic arrays. We show that it is provably computationally hard to find pulse sequences that simultaneously engineer interactions both within and between subensembles. Despite its formal hardness, we identify a set of necessary or sufficient conditions under which one can synthesize a target Hamiltonian from the native one. Moreover, we introduce efficient numerical strategies for designing pulse sequences that engineer target Hamiltonians robust against common control imperfections. As a specific application, we discuss the robust generation of two-mode spin squeezing in dual-species atomic ensembles, which is a challenging task without subensemble control. Our results provide a practical toolbox to design novel quantum simulation and sensing experiments with minimal control overhead.

</details>


### [124] [Resource-adaptive distributed fault tolerance with very noisy Bell pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03048)
*Moritz Schmidt,Martin Moureau,Benjamin Rodatz,Boldizsár Poór,Elie Mounzer,Linnea Grans-Samuelsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分布式量子计算架构中实现容错量子纠错和逻辑门操作的挑战，特别是在模块间交互受限且噪声较大的情况下。通过扩展“构造性容错”框架，提出了多种应对额外噪声的策略，并设计了高效的分布式稳定子测量电路，优化后显著减少了所需贝尔对的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式量子计算中，模块间的纠缠质量较差，如何有效实现容错操作是一个关键问题。需要发展新的方法来处理高噪声环境下的分布式量子纠错和逻辑门操作。

Method: 扩展了‘构造性容错’框架，提出故障改善策略，开发动态协议以实现时空权衡；设计可适应资源约束的分布式稳定子测量电路，并根据上下文优化电路以减少资源消耗。

Result: 集成解码使所需的蒸馏码距离减半，显著降低贝尔对需求；优化后的电路在表面码和颜色码中表现出对某些错误的鲁棒性，进一步减少所需贝尔对数量；数值模拟验证了在电路级噪声和互连噪声下的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的策略和电路设计有效提升了分布式容错量子计算的效率，降低了资源开销，为大规模量子计算机的实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Distributed architectures have been proposed as a pathway to large-scale quantum computers. Combined with the need for fault-tolerance, such architectures require distributed quantum error correction (DQEC) and distributed logical gates. An important challenge is how to realize DQEC primitives in the setting where interaction between modules is restricted to shared Bell pairs that are significantly noisier than on-chip operations. We extend the framework known as fault tolerance by construction to this setting, deriving different strategies for handling the additional noise. Through fault-improvement we recover conventional entanglement distillation, and also find more dynamical protocols that enable space-time trade-offs. We show that integrated decoding halves the required distillation code distance compared to entanglement distillation implemented using separate decoding, thus requiring significantly fewer Bell pairs. As a main focus of the work, we synthesize efficient circuits for an important primitive in distributed fault tolerance: distributed stabilizer measurements. These circuits can be adapted to resource constraints, e.g. on the Bell pair generation rate or the space available for on-chip auxiliary qubits. Noting that full local fault-tolerance is not always needed to preserve the correct scaling of logical error rates, we further optimize the circuits depending on the surrounding context. We consider in particular the surface code and the color code, both as distributed memories and in the case of lattice surgery across separate modules. Here, robustness to certain hook and readout errors reduces the number of required Bell pairs even further, compared to the context-free setting. We numerically benchmark the resulting implementations under circuit level noise with additional interconnect noise.

</details>


### [125] [Multi-Boundary Many-Body Quantum Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03053)
*Tal Schwartzman,Antonio F. Rotundo,Raz Monsonego,Shira Chapman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多体量子隐形传态在三个量子系统之间的信息传输，发现第三系统的存在能够提供信息扩散的空间或子系统分辨率，并揭示了与全息因果阴影类似的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受全息对偶中多边界虫洞几何的启发，研究单量子比特在三个量子系统间的多体隐形传态，以探索信息传播的分辨能力。

Method: 通过一维和全连接动力学模型，结合随机电路进行解析和数值分析，研究初始包含EPR对的三系统模型中的多体隐形传态协议。

Result: 发现第三系统会抑制隐形传态，当扩散的信息到达与其纠缠的量子比特区域时，成功传态所需的最小耦合和保真度依赖于注入点到该区域的距离；在全连接情况下，仅在早期和少量纠缠时可实现成功传态。

Conclusion: 第三系统的引入为多体隐形传态提供了空间或子系统层面的信息传播分辨能力，是迈向多体隐形传态网络的重要一步。

Abstract: Unlike standard quantum teleportation, many-body teleportation uses scrambling to transmit quantum information. In this protocol, initially localized information spreads over many degrees of freedom and is later refocused at the receiver by a simple coupling between the systems, followed by further many-body evolution. The protocol was developed from models of traversable wormholes in holography and has become a useful probe of scrambling. In particular, it can distinguish genuine scrambling from decoherence or noise when out-of-time-order correlators fail, and can reveal signatures of different scrambling mechanisms, including the distinctive behavior expected in holographic systems. Holography predicts that related protocols can transmit information between selected boundaries of multi-boundary wormhole geometries. Motivated by this setting, we study single-qubit many-body teleportation among three systems of qubits. The initial state consists of EPR pairs distributed among them, providing a simple analogue of an infinite-temperature three-boundary holographic state. We analyze the protocol with one-dimensional and all-to-all dynamics, both analytically and numerically using random circuits. We find that the third system suppresses teleportation once the spreading message reaches the region of qubits that are entangled with it. In one dimension, both the minimum coupling required for successful teleportation and the fidelity depend on the distance from the injection site to this region, a feature reminiscent of holographic causal shadows. For all-to-all dynamics, successful teleportation is instead restricted to early times and to sufficiently few qubits entangled with the third system. A third system therefore provides spatial, or subsystem, resolution of information spreading that is absent from the two-sided protocol. Our results offer a step toward many-body teleportation networks.

</details>


### [126] [Distinctness threshold for pseudorandom unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03065)
*Asad Raza,Jens Eisert,Bill Fefferman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子信息理论中统计伪随机性与计算伪随机性之间的关系，提出了一种无需基于统计伪随机结构（如2-设计）的计算伪随机酉系综构造方法，并引入“distinctness”性质来解释其必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解统计伪随机性（如酉设计）与计算伪随机性（PRU）之间的关系是当前量子信息理论中的关键问题。现有构造依赖于统计伪随机成分，但二者是否必然关联尚不明确。本文旨在探究计算伪随机性是否必须依赖于统计伪随机性。

Method: 通过将现有PRU构造中的酉2-设计替换为甚至不具备态1-设计性质但仍具有足够“distinctness”的系综，构建新的非自适应安全PRU。引入纠缠版本的反局域化（anticoncentration）来刻画distinctness，并分析其对PRU相干性和虚数性的约束。

Result: 证明了统计伪随机性并非实现计算伪随机性的必要条件；提出了不依赖于2-设计的新PRU构造；发现distinctness是PRU的关键性质，且能解释相干性与imaginarity的限制；识别出某些最大纠缠态下仍可实现实值PRU的情形；并利用缺乏distinctness否定了随机相位-Hadamard系综构成PRU的猜想。

Conclusion: 计算伪随机性可以独立于统计伪随机性存在，distinctness是实现PRU的核心属性，该结果深化了对两类伪随机性本质的理解，并拓展了PRU的构造路径。

Abstract: Pseudorandomness is increasingly recognized as a key property of ensembles in quantum information theory, statistical mechanics, and quantum many-body physics. Yet it appears in two conceptually different forms: statistical pseudorandomness, embodied by unitary designs, and computational pseudorandomness captured by pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs). The relationship between these two forms of pseudorandomness remains surprisingly poorly understood. Existing PRU constructions reveal this interplay where a statistically randomizing ingredient, a unitary design, is combined with classical cryptographic primitives to produce computational pseudorandomness. We show that statistical pseudorandomness is not necessary for computationally pseudorandom unitaries. We do this by replacing the unitary $2$-design layer in the existing constructions with ensembles that are not even state $1$-designs, yet are sufficiently {\em distinct}, a property we identify to be necessary for any PRU. This yields new non-adaptively secure PRU ensembles whose computational pseudorandomness is obtained without an underlying statistically pseudorandom quantum ensemble, such as a $2$-design. We characterize distinctness via an entangled analogue of anticoncentration and use it to show that distinctness already captures constraints on coherence and imaginarity of PRUs, while identifying broad classes of inputs for which the latter obstruction disappears, enabling real-valued PRUs even for certain (maximally) entangled states. As an application, we use lack of distinctness to constrain the conjectured pseudorandomness of the random phase-Hadamard ensemble to form a PRU.

</details>


### [127] [Spurious quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04157)
*Shashaank Khanna,Matthew F. Pusey,Roger Colbeck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在不同因果结构下量子关联的解释问题，指出某些看似非经典的量子关联在其他因果结构中可以有自然的经典解释，而无需精细调节；但同时也发现一些因果结构中的非经典量子关联在任何无精细调节的替代因果结构中都无法用经典方式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受贝尔因果结构及其对量子纠缠关联解释局限性的启发，探索是否存在其他因果结构能自然解释这些非经典关联，并研究此类解释是否需要精细调节。

Method: 通过比较不同因果结构下的量子与经典关联模型，分析哪些非经典关联可以在不违反相对论原则的情况下被经典化，并评估是否需要精细调谐。

Result: 发现部分看似非经典的量子关联在另一因果结构中可被自然经典解释且无需精细调节；但也存在某些因果结构中的非经典关联在所有无精细调节的替代结构中均无法经典化。

Conclusion: 并非所有非经典量子关联都必须依赖精细调节的经典解释，其可解释性依赖于所采用的因果结构；这揭示了因果假设在理解量子非定域性中的核心作用。

Abstract: In his seminal paper, Bell [Physics Physique Fizika 1, 195 (1964)] considers the correlations that result from space-like separated measurements on a pair of entangled particles. He uses relativity theory to motivate the Bell causal structure, then shows the existence of quantum correlations that cannot be explained classically within this causal structure. Classical explanations of such quantum correlations are possible in alternative causal structures, for instance, those that allow superluminal causal influences, but, as shown in [New Journal of Physics 17 033002 (2015)], all such alternative explanations require fine tuning (causation without correlation). Here we discuss the existence of spurious quantum correlations --- correlations that look quantum in one causal structure, but have a natural classical explanation in another. More precisely, there are causal structures that admit non-classical quantum correlations, but for which the same correlations have a classical explanation in another causal structure without fine tuning. The realisation in the other causal structure can also be achieved without breaking any natural constraints on the causal structure that follow from relativity theory. However, similarly to non-classical quantum correlations in the Bell causal structure, we find other causal structures with non-classical quantum correlations that do not have a classical causal explanation in any alternative causal structure without fine tuning.

</details>


### [128] [Optimized Matrix-Product State Simulations of Quantum Error Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03073)
*Asier Piñeiro Orioli,Chen Zhao,Guido Masella,Tommaso Macrì,Hengyun Zhou,Shannon Whitlock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了矩阵乘积态（MPS）技术可用于高效模拟包含非Clifford门的大规模量子纠错电路，并通过针对性优化显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速实现容错量子计算，需要能够精确模拟包含非Clifford门的量子纠错电路。现有方法在处理非Clifford门时存在限制，因此需要更高效的模拟手段。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）技术模拟量子纠错电路，并引入一系列针对降低纠缠（如剪切、压缩和重排序）的优化策略以减小键维数和运行时间。

Result: 实现了多种典型QEC电路的高效模拟，包括距离达11的旋转表面码、距离达9的逻辑贝尔态制备、仅用11个逻辑量子比特（187个物理量子比特）和最大键维64在40秒内完成的15到1魔态蒸馏电路，以及随T门线性扩展的深随机电路。

Conclusion: MPS方法结合电路级优化可有效处理任意门类型的QEC电路，是近Clifford模拟器的重要补充，为大规模量子纠错研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Simulating quantum error correction (QEC) circuits including non-Clifford gates at scale is important to accelerate progress toward fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we demonstrate that matrix product state (MPS) techniques can handle many QEC circuits exactly and without restriction on gate types. Crucially, we find that MPS efficiency depends sensitively on implementation choices, and we introduce a series of targeted optimizations that reduce bond dimensions and simulation time by several orders of magnitude compared to naive approaches. We illustrate this with examples including: (a) a rotated surface code quantum memory up to distance 11, (b) logical Bell-state preparation up to distance 9, (c) a 15-to-1 magic-state distillation circuit including hundreds of QEC rounds that we optimize to be simulated with only 11 logical qubits (187 physical qubits) and a maximal bond dimension of 64 in under 40 seconds, and (d) a narrow, deep random circuit that scales linearly with the number of T gates. These results demonstrate the importance of circuit-level optimizations and position MPS as a valuable complement to near-Clifford simulators for QEC circuits.

</details>


### [129] [Optimal control theory for measured quantum Schrödinger bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03097)
*Masayuki Ohzeki,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在连续监测的量子系统中，Schrödinger桥与熵最优传输中的桥势具有直接的测量理论意义，其方向导数与广义弱值的虚部相关，并将该结构与量子反馈控制和哈密顿控制综合联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Schrödinger桥和熵最优传输在量子系统中的深层理论联系，赋予桥势明确的物理意义，并建立其在量子控制中的应用框架。

Method: 基于条件量子轨迹理论的数学结构，推导出量子态空间上的Fokker-Planck方程，通过终端分布或测量效应进行条件化，引入Doob/Sinkhorn势，并分析其沿控制场的方向导数与弱值的关系。

Result: 发现桥势的方向导数对应于广义弱值的虚部；反向桥势作为效应型协态，其弱值导数提供局部控制信号；通过指定终端分布，Schrödinger桥诱导的漂移即为最小化二次代价的最优反馈控制解。

Conclusion: 弱测量自然地作为量子态传输的熵正则化机制，Schrödinger桥为从Sinkhorn缩放通向实用的量子反馈与哈密顿控制综合提供了理论路径。

Abstract: Schrödinger bridges and entropic optimal transport are usually formulated as stochastic interpolation problems between initial and final probability distributions. In their computational form, the bridge potentials are obtained by Sinkhorn or iterative proportional fitting, and are often regarded as auxiliary scaling functions. We show that for continuously monitored quantum systems, these potentials acquire a direct measurement-theoretic meaning. The mathematical structure of conditional quantum trajectory theory induces a Fokker--Planck equation on quantum state space. Conditioning this diffusion on a terminal distribution or a terminal measurement effect produces a Doob/Sinkhorn potential whose directional derivative along a unitary control vector field is the imaginary part of a generalized weak value. The same construction connects the Schrödinger-bridge viewpoint to the optimal-path framework for continuously monitored trajectories: the backward bridge potential plays the role of an effect-like costate, and its weak-value directional derivative gives the local control signal. By specifying the desired endpoint distribution, the induced drift produced by the Schrödinger bridge solution is the control solution that minimizes the quadratic cost feedback law, guiding the distribution to its desired endpoint. We quantify the control score of the available control Hamiltonian as a logarithmic directional derivative of the bridge potential, or an imaginary weak value. Three explicit examples identify weak measurement as a natural entropic regularization mechanism for quantum state transport and gives a route from Sinkhorn scaling to quantum feedback and Hamiltonian control synthesis for practical optimal control.

</details>


### [130] [Experimental validation of a compact fault-tolerant architecture for trapped ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03194)
*Noah Berthusen,Ali Lavasani,Asmae Benhemou,M. S. Allman,Joan Dreiling,Brian Estey,Cameron Foltz,Trent Jacobs,Michael Mills,Annie Jihyun Park,Adam P. Reed,David Hayes,Tzvetan S. Metodi,Andrew C. Potter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于$[[20,2,6]]$ $C_4$-Helix码的容错量子计算架构，展示了其在纠错、逻辑门操作和跨码种接口等方面的优越性能，证明该架构不仅限于量子存储，而是面向早期容错计算的有效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现有用的容错量子计算，仅具备低误差的量子存储是不够的。需要一个综合架构来协调高效的逻辑编码、低开销的逻辑操作以及非Clifford资源的接入，以支持通用量子计算。因此，本文旨在设计并验证适用于早期容错量子计算阶段的完整架构。

Method: 采用$[[20,2,6]]$ $C_4$-Helix码作为核心编码方案，并在98量子比特的离子阱量子处理器Quantinuum Helios上进行实验验证。通过重复的量子纠错循环、逻辑Clifford门组的基准测试以及与距离为5的表面码之间的容错链映射接口，评估架构的整体性能。同时使用电路级模拟预测未来物理保真度提升后的逻辑错误率。

Result: 实验实现了每逻辑量子比特每纠错周期$4.6^{+6.2}_{-2.6}\times10^{-5}$的错误率；两比特逻辑Clifford门的平均错误率为$2.8^{+1.0}_{-1.6}\times 10^{-4}$；成功构建了跨码种的三逻辑比特GHZ态，保真度下限达$99.925^{+0.068}_{-0.245}\%$；所有编码操作均优于未编码基线且无需后选择；模拟表明，随着物理保真度提升，逻辑错误率可进入$10^{-6}$–$10^{-8}$范围。

Conclusion: $C_4$-Helix码不仅可作为量子存储，更构成了一个经过硬件验证的完整容错计算架构，具备支持早期通用容错量子计算的能力。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is beginning to enable logical operations that outperform their unencoded physical counterparts, but useful fault-tolerant computation will require more than low-error quantum memory. An effective architecture must orchestrate efficient logical encoding, low-overhead logical operations, and access to the non-Clifford resources required for universal computation. Here, we introduce and experimentally validate such an architecture based on the $[[20,2,6]]$ $C_4$-Helix code, designed for the early fault-tolerant regime. Using Quantinuum Helios, a 98-qubit trapped-ion quantum processor, we experimentally demonstrate the principal components of this architecture: we perform repeated quantum error correction with an error of $4.6^{+6.2}_{-2.6}\times10^{-5}$ per logical qubit per QEC cycle. We benchmark the complete Clifford group on the two logical qubits of a single codeblock under active error correction, obtaining an error of $2.8^{+1.0}_{-1.6}\times 10^{-4}$ per two-qubit logical Clifford. We further demonstrate a fault-tolerant chain-map interface between $C_4$-Helix and a distance-5 surface code, preparing a heterogeneous three-logical-qubit GHZ state with a fidelity lower bound of $99.925^{+0.068}_{-0.245}\%$. In each case, the encoded implementation outperforms its corresponding unencoded physical baseline without relying on postselection. Circuit-level simulations indicate that improvements in physical fidelity bring the same architecture into the $10^{-6}$-$10^{-8}$ logical-error regime targeted for early fault-tolerant computation. Together, these results establish $C_4$-Helix as a hardware-validated fault-tolerant architecture rather than a bare quantum memory.

</details>


### [131] [Discretization-Aware Fine-Tuning for Quantum Machine Learning with Chemical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03220)
*Shunji Matsuura,Sonika Johri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DAFT的方法，用于优化量子机器学习中的数据表示，以应对近期内量子设备在高维经典数据编码上的瓶颈问题。通过减少跨类别碰撞，DAFT显著提升了量子分类性能，并在特定条件下实现了超越经典模型的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于近期量子设备在编码高维经典数据时存在信息瓶颈，导致不同标签样本可能被映射到相同的离散比特串中，从而影响量子机器学习的性能。本文旨在研究如何在严重的信息压缩下优化数据表示以提升模型表现。

Method: 提出了离散化感知微调（DAFT）方法，通过对预训练化学基础模型进行微调，使其生成在量化后仍具信息性的表示。该方法引入可微的软碰撞损失函数来降低碰撞概率。

Result: 在BBBP分子属性预测任务中，使用ChemBERTa-77M模型，DAFT将碰撞数量减少了几个数量级，并使量子分类准确率提高了12个百分点以上。在10个量子比特下，量子模型的表现超过了相同输入条件下的经典模型（0.883 vs. 0.855，p = 0.026）。

Conclusion: 在信息受限的情况下，实现量子优势的关键在于将连续表示与离散量子编码对齐。DAFT有效解决了高维数据在低维量子系统中因量化导致的信息损失问题，使得量子模型在适当表示下能够超越经典模型。

Abstract: A key challenge in practical quantum machine learning (QML), particularly for discriminative tasks such as classification, is the limited capacity of near-term quantum devices to encode high-dimensional classical data into small quantum registers. In optimized basis-encoded (bit-bit) settings, this constraint leads to cross-class collisions, where samples with different labels are mapped to the same discrete bit-string and thus become indistinguishable to any downstream model. In this work, we investigate how data representation affects QML performance under such severe information bottlenecks. We introduce discretization-aware fine-tuning (DAFT), a method that adapts a pre-trained chemical foundation model to produce representations that remain informative after quantization. DAFT reduces collision probability through a differentiable soft collision loss. We evaluate both quantum and classical models under a controlled setting in which they receive identical discretized bit-string inputs, isolating the effect of representation from model architecture. On the blood-brain barrier penetration (BBBP) molecular property prediction benchmark using ChemBERTa-77M, DAFT reduces collision counts by several orders of magnitude and improves quantum classification accuracy by more than 12 percentage points compared to a frozen backbone. Importantly, without DAFT, classical models outperform QML under the same input constraints. With DAFT, however, this comparison reverses at higher qubit counts. At 10 qubits, the quantum model surpasses a matched classical baseline trained on identical bit-strings (0.883 vs. 0.855, $p = 0.026$). These results show that, in information-constrained regimes, achieving a quantum advantage critically depends on aligning continuous representations with discrete quantum encodings.

</details>


### [132] [Revealing Many-Body Phases at finite temperatures through Quantum Coherence Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03226)
*Antonio Palamara,Nicola Lo Gullo,Antonello Sindona,Francesco Plastina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子相干性全概率分布的诊断框架，用于探测有限温度下单粒子和多体相变。利用该框架研究了非相互作用Aubry-André模型的局域化转变及其相互作用对应体系，发现相干性分布的熵是识别量子相变的强有力指标，且对热涨落具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在有限温度下有效探测单粒子和多体系统的量子相变，特别是面对热涨落影响时仍能保持敏感性的物理量。

Method: 引入基于量子相干性全概率分布的诊断框架，分析其各阶矩及分布熵在非相互作用与相互作用Aubry-André模型中的行为。

Result: 相干性分布的各阶矩可清晰标识量子相变；相干性分布的熵表现出更强的判别能力，并在热涨落增强时仍保持对量子相的显著响应。

Conclusion: 量子相干性分布的熵是一种强大且稳健的工具，适用于有限温度下识别单粒子和多体系统的量子相变。

Abstract: We introduce a diagnostic framework based on the full probability distribution of quantum coherence to probe single-particle and many-body phases at finite temperature. We first apply this approach to the localization transition in the non-interacting Aubry-André model and then extend the analysis to its interacting counterpart, whose phase diagram is substantially richer. We show that the moments of the coherence distribution provide clear signatures of quantum phase transitions and capture the emergence of many-body phases. More importantly, we find that the entropy of the coherence distribution emerges as an even more powerful indicator, retaining clear signatures of the underlying quantum phases while remaining remarkably robust against increasing thermal fluctuations.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum Complexity in Nuclear Scattering and Fission Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03240)
*Saurabh V. Kadam,Antonio Bjelčić,Nicolas Schunck,Kyle Wendt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了核反应动力学中量子复杂性的两个关键指标——两体纠缠熵和非局域魔性（非稳定子性）的时间演化，发现在费米子散射和$^{240}$Pu裂变过程中均存在显著的量子复杂性，表明量子计算机在模拟核反应动力学方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算机在模拟强关联量子多体系统（如原子核）中的潜力，需理解目标问题的量子复杂性结构，特别是在核反应动力学中的表现。

Method: 通过分析一维强相互作用费米子模型中的散射过程（Negele势）以及基于含时Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov（TDHFB）理论的$^{240}$Pu裂变模拟，研究纠缠熵和非局域魔性的时间演化。

Result: 在两种动力学过程中均发现相互作用会动态生成纠缠和非局域魔性，且在裂变碎片的空间划分中仍保留显著的量子复杂性。

Conclusion: 核反应动力学中存在显著的非局域魔性和纠缠，表明量子计算机将在精确模拟此类系统中具有重大优势。

Abstract: Quantum computers promise advantages for simulating strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, like atomic nuclei, that are beyond the reach of classical computers. Realizing this potential requires understanding the quantum complexity structure of the target problem. We investigate the time-evolution of two key indicators of quantum complexity, bipartite entanglement entropy and non-local magic (non-stabilizerness), in nuclear reaction dynamics. We analyze two representative dynamical processes: scattering in a one-dimensional model of strongly interacting fermions governed by the Negele potential, and a realistic simulation of $^{240}$Pu fission within time-dependent Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov (TDHFB) theory. In the former case, we find that interactions dynamically generate both entanglement and non-local magic, leaving persistent signatures of quantum complexity in the outgoing states. In the latter, we observe that substantial quantum complexity survives in the spatial bipartition of daughter fragments well beyond scission. The presence of significant non-local magic and entanglement in both cases strongly indicate that quantum computers would provide substantial advantages for accurately simulating nuclear reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [134] [Nodal obstruction to conditioned-diffusion representations of the weak momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03242)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子薛定谔桥构造在双态振幅存在移动零点时的失效问题，指出传统扩散过程无法同时保持条件密度并避开节点曲线，并通过实验可观测现象验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨当预选和后选态的双态振幅出现移动零点时，量子薛定谔桥假设的局限性及其物理后果。

Method: 基于双态振幅φ*ψ的节点结构分析弱动量的行为，结合概率流、电流速度与扩散过程的关系，检验Doob h-变换和Bernstein条件化的适用范围。

Result: 发现当φ*ψ具有阶数为k的移动零点时，常扩散系数的扩散过程无法避免节点曲线，且所需流速在接近曲线时发散；Doob h-变换仅在无节点区域成立；实验上可通过干涉可见度测量识别电流通道中的洛伦兹型信号。

Conclusion: 量子薛定谔桥的经典构造在存在节点时失效，必须考虑弱动量中奇异性的影响，特别是在基于轨迹解释的量子实验中需区分由零点引起的退相干与真正不相干机制。

Abstract: Quantum Schrödinger bridge constructions establish the existence of a conditioned process under a positivity hypothesis on the endpoint data, and three independent ones adopt it. We show that hypothesis fails on a definite locus once the conditioned object is the two-state amplitude $φ^*ψ$ of a pre- and post-selected pair on configuration space, and that the failure is testable on recorded data. Whenever $φ^*ψ$ has a moving zero of order $k$ across which probability flows, no diffusion of constant diffusion coefficient can both carry the conditioned density and avoid the nodal curve: the current velocity it would need grows as the inverse $2k$-th power of the distance to that curve and points toward it on one side, placing the curve at finite scale distance, so it is reached and not merely approached. Identifying the weak momentum with a bridge drift fails independently: for a free particle post-selected in position the current velocity is exactly minus the drift of the bridge to the target. The positive Doob $h$-transform and the Bernstein conditioning built on it exist wherever $φ^*ψ$ is nodeless and fail on the nodal curve. There the osmotic part of the weak momentum diverges as the inverse distance to the zero and changes sign across it with a coefficient set by $k$, while the current part stays bounded; that boundedness rests on the signed factorization of the real-envelope class and hides the obstruction from any picture built on the current velocity alone. A measured fringe visibility fixes a single length, which puts a Lorentzian of order $10^{-3}$ into the current channel reconstructed in the two-slit trajectory experiment if the contrast is limited by an off-axis zero, and nothing there if by incoherence. The results are one-dimensional, covering the separable transverse field of a two-slit geometry, not general two-dimensional vortices.

</details>


### [135] [Discrete-time quantum walks with energy-dependent coins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03272)
*Alana Spak dos Santos,Thiago T. Tsutsui,Fabiano M. Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射量子行走框架扩展到能量依赖的点相互作用晶格，提出了一种与零程势散射矩阵直接相关的硬币算符，并在离散时间量子行走中实现了类似克罗尼格-彭ney模型的结构。研究分析了谱特性、位置概率分布和纠缠动力学，揭示了不同点相互作用下的传播与纠缠行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立离散时间量子行走与周期性点相互作用物理模型之间的联系，利用量子行走模拟复杂散射行为并探索可控的量子动力学特性。

Method: 基于硬币量子行走形式，引入与零程势散射矩阵对应的硬币算符，构建能量依赖的点相互作用模型，并分析其谱结构、位置概率演化和长时纠缠行为。

Result: 发现了不同类型的点相互作用（δ, δ', 交叉和非对称）具有显著不同的透射特性，影响准能量带隙、带宽、群速度及长时纠缠程度；透射模量控制了系统的主要动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该模型为模拟一维周期性点相互作用提供了有效的量子行走框架，展示了通过walker波数调控硬币操作的物理透明性，并实现了从增强到抑制扩散以及振荡纠缠的完整行为覆盖。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the scattering quantum walk (SQW) framework to a lattice of energy-dependent point interactions. This yields, within the coined quantum walk (CQW) formalism, a coin operator that is directly related to the scattering matrix of zero-range potentials. The model thus provides a discrete-time quantum-walk (DTQW) analog of a periodic array of point interactions of the Kronig-Penney type, where the walker's wavenumber serves as a continuous, physically transparent control parameter for the coin operation. We analyze the spectra and the dynamics of position probability and entanglement, yielding distinct results for specific energies and point interactions. We relate the spectral structure to the spatial probability distribution and explicitly characterize the long-time entanglement behavior for each point interaction. The transmission modulus determines the quasienergy gap, bandwidth, and maximum group velocity, while also controlling the long-time coin-position entanglement for the initial state considered. The four families of one-dimensional point interactions ($δ$, $δ'$, crossed and asymmetric) realize qualitatively distinct transmission profiles and span the full range of behavior, including enhanced or strongly suppressed spreading and oscillatory entanglement.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum Vacuum Nonlinearities in Laser Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03314)
*Zain Mehdi,Joseph J. Hope,Simon A. Haine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于驻波激光干涉仪的全光方法来测试物质真空中光子-光子相互作用，无需外加磁场且使用常规激光源，具有随腔内循环功率非线性提升的灵敏度，有望用于探测超越标准模型的量子场性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索真空中光子-光子相互作用是理解量子电动力学和超越标准模型物理的关键，但该效应极弱，传统实验难以探测，因此需要高灵敏度且可行的实验室方案。

Method: 利用驻波激光干涉仪实现全光检测，通过增强腔内的循环功率提高对光子-光子散射信号的灵敏度，不依赖外部磁场和超强激光。

Result: 证明了基于量子电动力学预言的光子-光子散射可通过实验室尺度的干涉仪探测，灵敏度随循环功率非线性提升，并可扩展用于研究新物理。

Conclusion: 该方案为在实验室中探测真空非线性效应和超越标准模型的新物理提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We propose all-optical tests of photon-photon interactions in the matter vacuum using standing-wave laser interferometers, which do not require external magnetic fields and use conventional laser sources. We show that the interferometric detection of photon-photon scattering predicted by quantum electrodynamics is within reach of laboratory-scale experiments with a sensitivity that improves nonlinearly with the circulating power within the cavity. We outline how such an experiment could be adapted to probe properties of quantum fields beyond the Standard Model of particle physics.

</details>


### [137] [Fractalizing spacetime: Floquet codes with fractonic excitations that are immobile in space and time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03703)
*Juliette Soule,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将分形化从空间推广到时空的方法，用于构建具有时空受限激发的分形弗洛凯特码，展示了指数级时间响应周期和超线性容错距离的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展分形模型构建方法至时空领域，探索动态量子物态的新类型。

Method: 应用时空分形化技术构造分形弗洛凯特码，并分析其激发行为与容错性能。

Result: 实现了在空间和时间中均无移动拓扑激发的II型时空分形弗洛凯特码，表现出随系统尺寸指数增长的响应周期和超线性缩放的容错距离。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了内在动态量子相的新类别，为量子纠错提供了潜在更高效的时间开销方案。

Abstract: We generalize fractalization, a procedure for the construction of fracton models, from space to spacetime. We apply spacetime fractalization to construct fracton floquet codes with syndrome excitations that have limited mobility in space and time. This extends the notion of fracton order to intrinsically dynamical quantum phases of matter that are inequivalent to static fracton phases. We find spacetime type-II fracton floquet codes which have no topological excitations that are mobile in space or time. These codes exhibit an extreme form of quantum discrete time crystal order with response periods that scale exponentially in their linear system sizes. In this context, the no-strings rule that characterizes type-II fractons leads to a superlinear scaling of the floquet code fault-distance with time, potentially lowering the time overhead required for quantum error correction.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Estimation of Reduced QAOA Expressibility on Acyclic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03317)
*Bao Bach,Boris Tsvelikhovskiy,Jose Falla,Ilya Safro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式时间的经典算法，通过图的最短路径结构和度奇偶性递归区分顶点，有效分析对称性降低后的QAOA动力学李代数（DLA），揭示图结构如何影响量子变分算法的表达能力和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经典优化问题在等价形式下解相同，但其对应的量子算法（如QAOA）可能因对称性破缺而表现出不同的动力学行为。因此，需要一种高效方法来预测和分析这种对称性降低对量子电路表达能力的影响。

Method: 引入一种基于图结构的多项式时间经典算法，利用最短路径和度的奇偶性递归标记顶点，从而推断对称性降低后的QAOA的动力学李代数（DLA）结构，并识别子代数、估计DLA维度下界及可控子系统。

Result: 该算法能高效确定树状图上对称性降低QAOA的完整简化DLA，在完全分辨顶点时认证最大表达能力；即使未完全分辨，也能提供DLA维度的严格下界并识别可控部分。实验验证了该方法在诊断和指导变分量子算法方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 经典图结构可用于在运行前有效诊断和引导变分量子算法的量子动力学，为设计更高效的量子优化方案提供了理论工具和实用方法。

Abstract: Classically equivalent formulations of an optimization problem need not lead to equivalent quantum algorithms. For MaxCut, fixing the value of a single vertex removes a simple global symmetry without changing the underlying optimization problem, yet it can substantially alter the quantum dynamics generated by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). These dynamics are captured by the circuit's dynamical Lie algebra (DLA), whose direct construction can become exponentially expensive. Here we show that, for symmetry-reduced QAOA on trees, substantial information about the reduced DLA can instead be obtained efficiently from the graph alone. We introduce a polynomial classical algorithm that recursively distinguishes vertices using shortest path structure and degree parity. When all vertices are resolved individually, the method determines the complete reduced DLA and, under the corresponding graph conditions, certifies maximal expressibility of the reduced QAOA ansatz. Even when full resolution is not achieved, the algorithm identifies embedded subalgebras, provides rigorous lower bounds on DLA dimension, and certifies controllable subsystems. Our theoretical and experimental results show how classical graph structure can be used to diagnose and potentially guide the quantum dynamics of variational algorithms before running them on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [139] [Variable-Cliff Nielsen Geometry and an Exponent -4/3 Lower Bound for the Infinite-Cliff Diameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03323)
*Honghuai Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在特定条件下PU(D)上的右不变一步悬崖度量d_Q，发现其典型距离和直径渐近于(π/√3)√Q_D，并证明无限悬崖Carnot-Carathéodory距离的指数下限至少为4/3，推翻了Brown的指数一猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子门空间中不同权重惩罚下的度量性质，检验Brown关于典型距离指数的猜想是否成立。

Method: 利用Haar测度分析在给定参数范围内的一步悬崖度量的球体体积衰减率，推导出典型距离与直径的渐近行为。

Result: 当M≪Q_D≪D^(8/3)M^(-4/3)时，Haar典型的距离和直径均渐近于(π/√3)√Q_D；选择合适的Q_D可得无限悬崖距离的指数下限为4/3，且两比特电路复杂度下界也为同阶。

Conclusion: 推翻了Brown的指数一猜想，确立了指数至少为4/3的下限，对量子电路复杂度提供了更强的理论下界。

Abstract: Let $D=2^n$ and $M=3n+9\binom n2$. We study the right-invariant one-step-cliff metric $d_Q$ on $\operatorname{PU}(D)$, with unit penalty on Pauli weights one and two and penalty $Q$ on all higher weights. If $M/Q_D\to0$ and $MQ_D^{3/4}/D^2\to0$, then, for every fixed $0<x<π/\sqrt3$, \[
  μ_D\bigl(B_{Q_D}([I],x\sqrt{Q_D})\bigr)\le e^{-c_xD^2}. \] Here $μ_D$ is normalized Haar measure. Thus the Haar-typical distance from the identity and the diameter are both asymptotic to $(π/\sqrt3)\sqrt{Q_D}$ throughout the window $M\ll Q_D\ll D^{8/3}M^{-4/3}$. Choosing $Q_D=κD^{8/3}M^{-4/3}$ with sufficiently small fixed $κ>0$ yields a Haar-typical lower bound of order $D^{4/3}M^{-2/3}$ for the corresponding infinite-cliff Carnot--Carathéodory distance, outside an $e^{-Ω(D^2)}$ exceptional set. The infinite-cliff diameter therefore has exponential lower rate at least $4/3$, disproving Brown's exponent-one conjecture. The same estimate gives a fixed-error no-ancilla two-qubit circuit lower bound of the same order.

</details>


### [140] [Redesigning quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03333)
*Matthew B. Weiss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从第一原理出发发展了QBism中量子力学的非经典一致性条件，强调参考测量的选择，并展示了其与广义概率理论框架的等价性，最终实现了对有限维量子力学的相干性蕴含及复射影设计的自测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从贝叶斯主观概率视角理解量子力学，通过引入适当的参考测量来揭示量子态空间结构的起源，并建立非经典一致性条件的基础。

Method: 采用主观贝叶斯概率方法，结合广义概率理论框架，分析参考测量在非经典一致性条件中的作用，特别选择复射影3-设计作为参考测量，推导出对总概率律的微小修正形式的一致性关系。

Result: 证明了非经典一致性关系通常可视为总概率律的温和修改；当参考测量对应于复射影3-设计时，量子态空间的形状已隐含在其概率特征中；并提出了一种无需依赖特定理论的复射影t-设计（t≥3）的自测试方法。

Conclusion: 仅需与一个适当选择的参考测量保持一致性，即可蕴含整个有限维量子力学的所有一致性要求，从而为量子理论提供了一种简洁的重建路径。

Abstract: QBism understands quantum mechanics to be probability theory supplemented by additional nonclassical coherence conditions. In this dissertation, we develop these nonclassical coherence conditions from first principles, emphasizing the role of a well chosen reference measurement. After treating standard probability on subjective Bayesian lines, we demonstrate an equivalence between the QBist approach and the existing framework of generalized probabilistic theories. We show that the fundamental nonclassical coherence relation may almost always be taken to be a gentle modification of the law of total probability, and give a coherentist account of when an experimental scenario has a classical explanation. Finally, we show that when the reference measurement is chosen to correspond to a complex projective 3-design, the shape of quantum state space is implicit in the probabilities which characterize the reference measurement itself. Thus coherence with this single reference measurement, properly understood, implies coherence with all of finite dimensional quantum mechanics. We then attempt a modest reconstruction of quantum theory along these lines, one practical consequence of which is a method for self-testing complex projective $t$-designs for $t\ge 3$ in a theory-agnostic way.

</details>


### [141] [Nonlinear Dissipation and Hopf Criticality in Driven Dissipative Collective Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03360)
*Shu Yang,Jun Wang,Weidong Li,Cangtao Zhou,Jian-Song Pan,Jianwen Jie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放多体系统中自持振荡的动力学机制，提出非线性协变耗散选择自持振荡背景，而对称性破缺通道的结构决定其局部响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放多体系统中自持振荡的产生机制，明确不同耗散结构和对称性破缺方式对振荡行为的影响。

Method: 基于分岔理论分析驱动-耗散集体自旋系统的平均场动力学，研究微观耗散结构如何决定振荡背景及其对U(1)对称性破缺的响应。

Result: 发现线性U(1)-协变跃迁只能产生极向固定点背景，而非线性协变耗散可通过超临界Hopf分岔稳定有限纬度的自持振荡流形；显式U(1)破缺下，精确共振导致可逆双零简并，有限失谐则展开为仅存在于自持振荡侧的真实有限频率Hopf边界；单一线性耗散U(1)破缺跃迁无法产生标准Hopf不稳定性。

Conclusion: 建立了普遍设计原则：非线性协变耗散选择自持振荡背景，而对称性破缺通道的结构决定其响应是双零、真实Hopf还是非Hopf型。

Abstract: Self-sustained oscillations in open many-body systems can be organized by two distinct dynamical ingredients: the selection of a finite-amplitude background attractor and the subsequent control of its neutral phase. We develop this bifurcation-based picture for driven-dissipative collective spins and show how the microscopic dissipative structure determines the available background and its response to explicit U(1) breaking. In the thermodynamic-limit mean-field dynamics, a single linear U(1)-covariant jump produces only polar fixed-point backgrounds, whereas nonlinear covariant dissipation provides amplitude-dependent saturation and stabilizes a finite-latitude self-sustained-oscillator manifold through a supercritical Hopf bifurcation. Under coherent U(1) breaking, exact resonance leads to a reversible double-zero degeneracy with vanishing critical frequency rather than a standard Hopf onset. Finite detuning unfolds this singularity into a genuine finite-frequency Hopf boundary, which exists only on the self- sustained-oscillator side and can be either supercritical or subcritical. By contrast, a single linear dissipative U(1)-breaking jump cannot generate a standard Hopf instability: when its phase-pinning invariant vanishes the azimuthal direction remains neutral, whereas otherwise the phase-locked fixed points have a purely real Jacobian spectrum. These results establish a general design principle: nonlinear covariant dissipation selects the self-sustained background, while the structure of the symmetry-breaking channel determines whether the resulting local response is double-zero, genuinely Hopf, or non-Hopf.

</details>


### [142] [An interpretation-independent formulation of the measurement problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03409)
*Antoine Soulas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新表述了量子力学中的测量问题，将其定义为一种与解释无关的现象，即量子物质无法用经典的概率理论描述，从而导致无法建立独立于认识论的本体论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于不同量子力学解释下测量问题呈现不同面貌，缺乏统一精确定义，因此需要一种不依赖具体解释的方式来澄清该问题的本质。

Method: 借鉴贝尔定理等理论无关的方法，从经验统计出发，基于量子系统违背Kolmogorov概率公理这一事实，将测量问题重构为认识论与本体论不可分离的问题。

Result: 提出测量问题的核心在于无法为量子系统构建一个独立于认知的客观实在（无‘上帝视角’），并指出这一特性是量子统计本身的特征而非可‘解决’的问题。

Conclusion: 这种重构有助于重新理解各种量子解释、揭示量子力学与广义相对论深层不兼容的原因，并为量子引力理论的探索提供指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we do not try to solve the measurement problem, but rather to properly formulate it. One of the reasons why it still lacks a precise, agreed definition is that the problem may take very different aspects depending on the interpretation of quantum mechanics embraced. Inspired by the methodology of theory-independent results like Bell's theorem, we propose to identify the common root of the puzzle in an interpretation-independent way i.e. as a property of the empirical statistics only, before deriving its philosophical consequences. The key observation is that quantum matter can not be described by a Kolmogorovian probabilistic theory. Arguing that the Kolmogorov axioms of probability theory are the postulates of epistemic uncertainty leads us to reformulate the measurement problem as the impossibility to build an ontology independent of epistemology for quantum matter. Said differently, there exists no God's-eye view on quantum systems. Although it is meaningless to solve the measurement problem as defined in this way, insofar as it is a feature of the universe's statistics, such a formulation may on the other hand bring side benefits. In particular, we argue that it allows to: (i) shed a new light on the variety of interpretations; (ii) propose a fundamental reason why quantum mechanics and general relativity are so incompatible, not relying on purely mathematical or technical arguments; (iii) guide the quest for quantum gravity.

</details>


### [143] [In defence of the Ehrenfest mean-field molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03419)
*Vladimir U. Nazarov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了在核的经典极限下，所谓的“平均场”Ehrenfest图像是一个精确理论，而非近似，并且与精确分解方法完全等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视电子-核耦合运动问题，旨在澄清Ehrenfest平均场方法的理论地位。

Method: 通过将Ehrenfest方法与精确分解（Exact Factorization）方法进行对比和理论分析，建立二者在经典核极限下的等价性。

Result: 证明了Ehrenfest平均场图像在核的经典极限下是精确的，而非近似方法。

Conclusion: Ehrenfest分子动力学是一种简单、有效且形式上精确的方法，可在含时密度泛函理论框架内使用。

Abstract: Re-visiting the problem of the coupled electronic--nuclear motion, we prove that the so called `mean-field' Ehrenfest picture is an exact theory, rather than an approximation, in the classical limit for nuclei. By establishing its full equivalence to the much more involved Exact Factorization approach to the problem (Abedi {\it et al.}, Europhys. Lett. {\bf 106}, 33001 (2014)), our conclusion rehabilitates the mean-field Ehrenfest molecular dynamics as a simple, effective, while formally exact scheme, amenable to the use within the framework of the time-dependent density functional theory.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum thermalization achieves optimal approximate quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04121)
*Aditi Venkatesh,Richard R. Allen,Saúl Pilatowsky-Cameo,Bingtian Ye,Soonwon Choi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子热化与量子纠错之间的联系，提出热化动力学在编码率、距离和热熵密度之间存在普适的最优关系，并证明其在有限温度下仍可达到量子Singleton界，揭示了热化过程本身具有最优编码结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索孤立多体系统如何通过量子热化实现信息保护，并利用量子纠错理论框架理解热化的内在编码特性。

Method: 将典型的晚期时间态视为码字，应用近似量子纠错的严格框架，结合数值模拟与基于Scrooge系综的理论分析，研究热化动力学的纠错性质。

Result: 发现编码率、码距与热熵密度之间存在普适关系；无限温下该曲线饱和量子Singleton界，有限温下Scrooge系综构造的码族达到熵Singleton界，且热化过程自身表现出最优纠错性能；守恒量仅泄露经典信息，纠错性持续至能量差达热涨落尺度。

Conclusion: 量子热化动力学具有普适的最优编码结构，不仅自然达到量子纠错的基本极限，还提供了新的最优码构造方案。

Abstract: Quantum thermalization explains how an isolated many-body system naturally evolves towards a thermal state, rendering information about the initial conditions inaccessible to local measurements. This is precisely the mechanism utilized in quantum error correction, where information is protected by design through a nonlocal encoding. In this work, we leverage this connection to port the rigorous framework of (approximate) quantum error correction to the study of quantum thermalization. Treating typical late-time states as codewords, we characterize the error-correcting properties of generic thermalizing dynamics. We numerically uncover a universal relationship between the encoding rate, distance, and thermal entropy density of the emergent code. At infinite temperature, this universal curve saturates the quantum Singleton bound, achieving the same optimal limit as Haar-random codes. At finite temperature, we introduce a code family based on the Scrooge ensemble, the natural thermal analogue of the Haar ensemble, and prove it saturates the entropic quantum Singleton bound, establishing this family as optimal within entropic constraints. Our extracted universal curve independently saturates this same bound, revealing that finite-temperature thermalization is itself optimal. Finally, we show how conserved quantities limit the error-correcting behavior of thermalization: codewords with differing energies, or other conserved charges, leak only classical information, and correctability persists until the difference reaches the scale of thermal fluctuations. Our results reveal a universal optimal coding structure in thermalizing dynamics, while introducing new optimal codes that achieve fundamental limits of approximate quantum error correction.

</details>


### [145] [Tunable topological enhancement of covariant quantum Fisher information via non-Bloch skin effect in non-Hermitian SSH lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03421)
*Qi-Cheng Wu,Yan-Hui Zhou,Tong Liu,Dong-Xu Chen,Chui-Ping Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将协变量子费舍信息（CQFI）扩展到多模非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger晶格，揭示了由非厄米皮肤效应驱动的可调谐拓扑增强机制，在开放边界条件下显著提升传感精度，并具有鲁棒性和多参数估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CQFI研究局限于单模系统，缺乏对多模非厄米系统中拓扑与皮肤效应协同作用下量子精密测量潜力的探索。

Method: 通过将CQFI理论推广至多模非厄米SSH晶格，结合非布洛赫理论分析开放边界下的广义布里渊区变形及其对量子 Fisher 信息的影响。

Result: 在开放边界条件下，CQFI的增强因子E(N)超过30（N~30），且总CQFI随系统尺寸线性增长；该增强对适度无序具有鲁棒性，并实现联合Cramér-Rao界降低达15个数量级的多参数估计。

Conclusion: 非厄米皮肤效应为量子计量提供了可在空间域实现的新型增强机制，兼容现有实验平台如拓扑电路、光子晶格和超导电路。

Abstract: The covariant quantum Fisher information (CQFI) has recently been established as the ultimate precision benchmark for pseudo-Hermitian sensors [Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 080802 (2026)], yet existing analyses are limited to single-mode systems. Here we extend the CQFI formalism to multi-mode non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger lattices and reveal tunable topological enhancement enabled by the non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE). Under open boundary conditions, the NHSE deforms the conventional Brillouin zone into a generalized Brillouin zone of radius r = exp(\k{appa}), where \k{appa} denotes the non-Bloch decay rate. While the total CQFI scales linearly with system size N, its prefactor depends critically on \k{appa}, yielding an enhancement factor E(N) = F_OBC / F_PBC that exceeds 30 for N ~ 30, substantially outperforming periodic-boundary sensors. The enhancement is robust against moderate local disorder and supports multi-parameter estimation, with the joint Cramér-Rao bound reduced by up to 15 orders of magnitude. These findings establish a spatial-domain mechanism for quantum metrology that complements time-domain strategies and is experimentally accessible using topoelectrical circuits, photonic lattices, and superconducting circuits with current technology.

</details>


### [146] [Convolution absorbing boundaries for explicit-circuit quantum simulation of the wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03440)
*Hoang Anh Nguyen,Ali Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过吸收边界条件实现开放域波动方程量子模拟的方法，解决了传统哈密顿模拟中反射问题。通过Schrodingerisation处理非幺正动力学，并设计了显式量子电路，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了后选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于哈密顿模拟的量子电路仅适用于封闭区域，导致波在边界反射，无法真实模拟开放系统的波动行为。为克服这一限制，需要引入吸收边界条件以模拟无反射的开放域。

Method: 采用卷积型吸收边界（复频率偏移的完美匹配层），通过指数核记忆变量实现；将非幺正动力学通过Schrodingerisation转化为可量子实现的形式；构造基于Bell基项演化的扩展电路，使用投影值算符串和二阶Trotter分解得到显式门级电路；并引入经典预计算的Lyapunov对称化器使生成元变为耗散型。

Result: 1. 实现了可在17至21个量子比特上端到端运行的显式量子电路，与精确参考解吻合度达0.1%至1%。
2. 发现吸收生成元的记忆形式具有不可约的不定厄米部分，其最大特征值随吸收强度平方根增大而增长，导致标准Schrodingerisation的后选择成本随模拟时间指数增长。
3. 提出Lyapunov对称化方法，将时间依赖的惩罚转换为时间无关的条件数（数百量级），且该条件数在网格加密下趋于饱和；在长时间模拟中，相较于未对称化方案，后选择成本降低4至26个数量级，且是唯一可行方案。

Conclusion: 通过引入吸收边界与Lyapunov对称化，成功实现了开放域波动方程的高效量子模拟，克服了传统方法因边界反射和后选择成本过高而导致的局限性，为非幺正物理过程的量子实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Explicit quantum circuits for the wave equation, built by Hamiltonian simulation, are restricted to closed domains, in which outgoing waves reflect off the edge of the computational region and return. We lift that restriction with absorbing boundaries of the convolution type - a complex-frequency-shifted perfectly matched layer realised through exponential-kernel memory variables - and make the resulting non-unitary dynamics quantum-implementable by Schrodingerisation. We obtain three results. First, explicit gate-level circuits for the absorbing evolution, obtained by extending a Bell- basis term-evolution circuit to projector-valued operator strings and Trotterising to second order; these run end to end on seventeen to twenty-one qubits and agree with exact references to within a tenth of a percent to a percent. Second, a structural obstruction: the memory form of the absorbing generator carries an irreducibly indefinite Hermitian part, whose largest eigenvalue grows as the square root of the absorption strength divided by the grid spacing and survives any diagonal rescaling of the memory fields. The known recovery threshold for Schrodingerisation then makes the post-selection cost grow exponentially in the simulated time. Third, a remedy: a Lyapunov symmetrizer, precomputed classically, renders the transformed generator dissipative and replaces that time-dependent penalty with a time-independent conditioning factor of several hundred, measured on grids of up to four thousand unknowns and saturating under mesh refinement. The crossover is early: past it the symmetrized recovery is cheaper by four to twenty-six orders of magnitude in post-selection cost, and at the longest horizons tested it is the only recovery that works.

</details>


### [147] [Weakly Driven and Finite Detuning Boundary Time Crystals Enabled by Low-Dissipation Dynamical Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03491)
*Xiang Guo,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhihai Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在弱驱动和有限失谐条件下实现边界时间晶体（BTCs）的新机制，利用共享耗散形成的低耗散动力学通道，实现了对初始状态不敏感的稳定极限环振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界时间晶体需要强驱动和精确共振，限制了其实验实现，因此需要一种在更宽松条件下仍能维持持久振荡的机制。

Method: 研究两个原子系综耦合到一个共同马尔可夫耗散库的系统，分析共享耗散如何形成低耗散动力学通道，并支持在弱驱动和失谐条件下的时间晶体行为。

Result: 发现共享耗散可组织出动态可访问的低耗散通道，使系统在弱驱动和有限失谐下仍能产生持久振荡，并选择出唯一的稳定极限环。

Conclusion: 低耗散动力学通道为实现鲁棒的边界时间晶体提供了新路径，显著放宽了对驱动强度和共振条件的要求。

Abstract: Spontaneous breaking of continuous time-translation symmetry in driven-dissipative systems gives rise to boundary time crystals (BTCs), characterized by persistent oscillations sustained by coherent driving and collective dissipation. Conventional BTCs, however, typically require strong driving and exact atom-drive resonance, imposing stringent constraints on their realization. Here we consider two atomic ensembles coupled to a common Markovian reservoir and show that shared dissipation organizes dissipation-free and low-dissipation modes into dynamically accessible low-dissipation channels, enabling BTCs under weak driving and finite detuning. Finite detuning further selects a unique stable limit cycle from an initial-state-dependent family of oscillatory trajectories. Our results establish low-dissipation dynamical channels as a route to robust BTCs under relaxed driving and resonance conditions.

</details>


### [148] [Topology-Dependent Enhancement of Entanglement Extraction in Repeater Graph States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03496)
*Poramat Chianvichai,Poramet Pathumsoot,Naphan Benchasattabuse,Michal Hajdušek,Rodney Van Meter,Sujin Suwanna*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在无记忆量子中继器架构——中继图态（RGS）中，如何通过优化其内部连接拓扑以最大化提取贝尔对的数量，克服传统RGS仅能从完全二分图态中提取单个贝尔对的瓶颈问题。模拟结果表明，贝尔对产率在低至中等边密度下最高，且随着网络跳数增加，需要更高的内部连接度以维持足够产率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子中继器依赖物理量子存储器，易受退相干影响且实现困难；RGS提供了一种无需存储器、抗光子损耗的新方案，但存在仅能提取单个贝尔对的瓶颈问题，亟需提升通信效率。

Method: 通过模拟分析不同连接拓扑结构下的RGS，研究其内部qubit连接方式对可提取贝尔对数量的影响，并评估在不同网络跳数下维持高产率所需的内部连接密度。

Result: 发现贝尔对的最大提取数量依赖于RGS的连接拓扑，在低到中等边密度时产率达到最优；随着网络跳数增加，需提高RGS的内部qubit连接度以维持足够的贝尔对产率。

Conclusion: 为实现高效的长距离量子通信，未来的RGS设计应趋向于具有更高内部qubit连接度的结构，以克服当前RGS架构中的产量瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Quantum repeaters are essential for establishing long-distance quantum communication to overcome the exponential decay of entanglement due to photon loss. Traditional repeater architectures rely on physical quantum memory, which introduces decoherence and poses significant practical implementation challenges. The repeater graph state (RGS) architecture offers a promising memory-less alternative that is inherently resilient to photon losses. A key challenge in implementing RGS lies in the requirement for highly efficient graph state generators and complex qubit measurement. In this work, we aim to investigate the strategies for extracting the maximum number of Bell pairs from the RGS structure via its qubit connection to resolve the well-known bottleneck problem of RGS in which only a single Bell pair can be extracted from a complete bipartite graph state. From simulations, we observe that the maximum number of extracted Bell pairs depends on its connection topology, where the Bell-pair yield tends to be maximal at low to moderate edge densities. As the number of network hops increases, the RGS must be equipped with higher inner-qubit connectivity to maintain a sufficient yield of extractable Bell pairs. Thus, the expected resource requirement shifts toward the use of RGSs with higher inner-qubit connectivity.

</details>


### [149] [Heralded one-, two- and three-photon states from waveguided parametric down-conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03542)
*Borrero Landazabal Daniel,Laiho Kaisa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单次点击概率和归一化阶乘矩测量的资源节约型量子光学分析方法，适用于存在较大实验缺陷的情况，并用于验证高阶非经典光态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在存在严重实验不完美性的情况下仍能有效分析量子光学态，需要一种鲁棒且资源高效的表征手段。

Method: 利用周期极化KTiOPO4波导中的参量下转换产生交叉偏振孪生光束，并通过单次点击概率和测量高达m=n+1阶的归一化阶乘矩gh(m)来表征至三光子数态。

Result: 成功提取了态的平均光子数和矩生成函数值，并测量了高阶归一化阶乘矩，验证了gh(m+1) < gh(m) < 1的违反，表明高阶非经典性。

Conclusion: 单次点击概率和归一化阶乘矩是分析非理想条件下多光子态的有效工具，为评估光场的高阶非经典性提供了便捷途径。

Abstract: The manifold coincidences and singles counting provides a resource-saving quantum-optics analysis tool, since it is appropriate even in the presence of heavy experimental imperfections. Here, we prepare cross-polarized twin beams in the telecommunication wavelength range via parametric down-conversion in a periodically-poled KTiOPO$_4$ waveguide and herald photon-number states up to three photons. First, we show that the single-click probability is a versatile tool not only for extracting the state's mean photon number but also for sampling values of the moment generating function being the core behind any quantum optical state. Second, we measure values of the normalized factorial moments of photon number, $g^{(m)}_{\text{h}}$, up to the order $m = n+1$ for the heralded $n$-photon state. These normalized photon correlations provide an expedient method for examining the higher-order non-classicality of light by violating the condition $g^{(m+1)}_{\text{h}} \ge g^{(m)}_{\text{h}} \ge 1$.

</details>


### [150] [Pulse-Controlled Topologically Protected Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03543)
*Jin Yang,Biao Xiong,Jibing Liu,Houguo Yu,Dehua Liu,Feng Mei,Chuanjia Shan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于脉冲控制超导量子比特链的拓扑保护长距离充电协议，实现无能量回流且鲁棒性强的量子电池充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决量子电池在长距离充电中的干扰问题，探索具有拓扑保护特性的充电机制以提高稳定性和效率。

Method: 通过动态调制脉冲介导的耦合，在超导量子比特链中实现拓扑保护的能量传输，并优化脉冲形状以加速充电过程。

Result: 实现了无能量回流、对脉冲控制缺陷鲁棒、能量完全可提取的长距离充电，且在较大系统中仍有效。

Conclusion: 该协议为长距离拓扑充电提供了通用框架，并为提升量子电池性能的最优控制策略奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum batteries have emerged as a promising new generation of energy-storage devices for powering quantum technologies. Long-distance charging is particularly attractive because it minimizes interference between the charger and the battery, thereby attracting considerable interest. Here, we propose a topologically protected long-distance charging protocol for quantum batteries based on a pulse-controlled superconducting qubit chain. By dynamically modulating the pulse-mediated couplings, we realize topologically protected energy transfer from the charger to the battery. We show that the charging process is free of energy backflow and remains robust against imperfections in pulse control. Moreover, the energy stored in the battery at the target time is fully extractable, and the protocol remains effective for relatively large system sizes. To further accelerate charging, we optimize the pulse shape and elucidate the underlying physical mechanism. Our pulse-controlled topological quantum battery protocol provides a versatile framework for implementing long-distance topological charging and establishes a theoretical foundation for designing optimal-control strategies to enhance quantum battery performance.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancing noise robustness in device-independent conference key agreement with asymmetric parity-CHSH inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03551)
*Makoto Ishihara,Wojciech Roga,Jonatan Bohr Brask,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多部分贝尔不等式——非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式，用于增强设备无关会议密钥协商的鲁棒性，并通过分析其在检测效率低下和噪声环境下的表现，证明了该方法结合噪声预处理能显著提高对缺陷的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低设备无关会议密钥协商对检测效率的高要求，提升其在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: 引入了一类新的多部分贝尔不等式（非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式），并推导出基于该不等式的协议中一方输出结果的条件冯·诺依曼熵的紧致解析下界，同时考虑了噪声预处理的影响。

Result: 分析表明，所提出的非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式结合噪声预处理能够显著提高协议对检测效率不足以及局部和全局去极化噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 新提出的非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式与噪声预处理相结合，可有效增强设备无关会议密钥协商协议在现实不完美条件下的安全性与实用性。

Abstract: Conference key agreement allows multiple remote parties to establish a shared secret key with information-theoretical security. In device-independent conference key agreement, security can be guaranteed with minimal assumptions on the devices used, provided that a violation of a Bell inequality is observed. However, implementations are extremely challenging because high detection efficiency is required to observe loophole-free Bell violations. Here, we enhance the robustness of device-independent conference key agreement by introducing a new family of multipartite Bell inequalities called the asymmetric parity-Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) inequalities. We derive a tight analytical lower bound on the conditional von Neumann entropy of the outcomes of one of the parties in a protocol based on this inequality, including noisy preprocessing. Using this bound, we analyze robustness to detection inefficiencies as well as local and global depolarizing noise. We show that the combination of the asymmetric parity-CHSH inequality and noisy preprocessing can significantly improve the robustness to imperfections.

</details>


### [152] [Optimizing Atom Transport, Gate-Count and Depth with Parity Twine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03583)
*Javad Kazemi,Michael Fellner,Riccardo J. Valencia-Tortora,Michael Schuler,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对中性原子量子处理器的奇偶架构（Parity Architecture）的高效实现方法，通过适配不同的原子布局、原生纠缠门和原子穿梭能力，利用奇偶绞线网络（PTNs）优化量子电路的硬件相关资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在中性原子量子平台上更高效地执行复杂量子算法，需解决门数量、电路深度和原子传输带来的资源开销问题，提升电路保真度。

Method: 将奇偶绞线网络（PTNs）适配到不同原子构型和原生纠缠门（如CZ、CZSWAP、iSWAP），构建支持静态与可移动中性原子平台的通用优化框架，并以量子傅里叶变换（QFT）为例进行评估。

Result: 在30量子比特QFT电路中，相比现有编译策略，实现了双量子比特门数、原子传输和电路深度的显著降低，估计电路保真度提高三个数量级。

Conclusion: PTNs为中性原子量子处理器提供了灵活且高效的硬件感知优化方案，具有广泛适用于多种量子算法的潜力。

Abstract: We present an efficient implementation of the Parity Architecture for neutral-atom quantum processors. We adapt Parity Twine Networks (PTNs) to different atom layouts, native entangling gates, and atom-shuttling capabilities. This provides a general framework for hardware-aware optimization of gate count, circuit depth, and atom transport for quantum circuits encoding arbitrary interaction graphs in a common basis. Specifically, we develop PTN constructions based on different native entangling-gate realizations, namely CZ, CZSWAP, and iSWAP, providing flexibility to accommodate different hardware capabilities on both static and mobile neutral-atom platforms. Using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) as a representative example, we demonstrate substantial reductions in two-qubit gate count, atom transport, and circuit depth. These resource savings translate into an estimated circuit fidelity three orders of magnitude higher than competing compilation strategies for a 30-qubit QFT. We further extend the construction to the recently introduced optimistic QFT and discuss the broader applicability of PTNs to other quantum algorithms on neutral-atom platforms.

</details>


### [153] [Entanglement dynamics of accelerated atoms with environment-induced interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03638)
*Xuerui Zhou,Chenhao Ma,Zixu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在存在反射边界的情况下，两个均匀加速的原子与涨落无质量标量场耦合时，环境诱导相互作用对纠缠动力学的影响。纠缠行为受到环境诱导的原子间和原子-板相互作用竞争的影响，并表现出非单调的加速度依赖性，暗示反乌伦贝克效应的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有反射边界条件下，环境诱导的相互作用如何影响加速原子系统的纠缠动力学。

Method: 分析两个垂直于边界排列的均匀加速原子与波动标量场的耦合系统，研究环境诱导的相互作用对纠缠演化的影响。

Result: 发现纠缠度（concurrence）的最大值随加速度非单调变化，表明在特定条件下即使考虑环境诱导的多种相互作用，仍可能出现反乌伦贝克现象。

Conclusion: 环境诱导的原子间和原子-板相互作用的竞争显著影响纠缠动力学，反乌伦贝克效应在某些情况下依然存在。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of environment-induced interactions on the entanglement dynamics of two uniformly accelerated atoms coupled to a fluctuating massless scalar field with a reflecting boundary. The two atoms are aligned vertically to the boundary. The entanglement behaviors are influenced by the competition between the environment-induced interatomic and the environment-induced atom-plate interactions, which can be characterized by certain critical values. The maximum of concurrence generated during evolution decreases non-monotonically with the acceleration, which implies the anti-Unruh phenomenon can exist for some situations even when both environment-induced interatomic and the environment-induced atom-plate interactions are considered.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum Hamiltonian Evolution for Coherent Quantum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03640)
*Ignacio B. Acedo,Javier Gonzalez-Conde,Pablo Rodriguez-Grasa,Barry C. Sanders,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了相干量子学习（CQL）框架，将模型参数视为受损失函数哈密顿量驱动的量子自由度，利用量子干涉实现训练过程中低损失配置的概率幅集中，无需经典梯度反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习依赖经典优化方法，无法充分利用量子系统的相干性；希望构建一种完全量子化的训练框架，使量子特性在学习过程中发挥核心作用。

Method: 将损失函数编码为哈密顿量，通过块编码和哈密顿量模拟技术，使处于叠加态的参数寄存器在幺正演化下自然集中于低损失区域，利用量子干涉替代梯度计算。

Result: 在二分类与干涉相位估计任务中验证了该方法的有效性，演化后的分布峰值出现在最优参数附近，性能与基于梯度的方法相当。

Conclusion: CQL实现了全量子化的学习过程，避免了经典反馈，为未来容错量子计算环境下的量子机器学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Coherent Quantum Learning (CQL), a training framework for quantum learning models in which the model parameters are quantum degrees of freedom evolved under a Hamiltonian that encodes the loss function. Current quantum machine learning retains classical optimization: parameters are updated by a classical outer loop using gradient estimates from measurements, and quantum coherence has no role in the training dynamics, just as in any classical treatment of the same problem. In the quantum case, a parameter register initialized in superposition evolves unitarily, and probability amplitude concentrates near low-loss configurations through interference, without gradient computation or classical feedback. We give an explicit construction using block encodings and Hamiltonian simulation, applicable to arbitrary parameterized circuits. Numerical experiments on binary classification and interferometric phase estimation confirm that the evolved distribution peaks at the optimal parameters, matching gradient-based performance. The construction is compatible in principle with fault-tolerant implementations and extends to batched training via sequential Hamiltonian evolution.

</details>


### [155] [Q-Edge: Symmetry-Reduced Quantum Simulation of Structured Extreme Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03706)
*Hongrui Zhang,Paolo Recchia,Ying Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对称性约简的轨道空间表示方法，用于高维多元极值依赖结构的可扩展量子模拟框架Q-Edge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多元极值模拟受限于依赖结构的组合复杂性，传统方法难以高效处理。

Method: 利用对称性将原始指数级增长的依赖空间压缩为紧凑的轨道空间，并在此基础上构建量子生成模型Q-Edge，直接在轨道空间中进行操作。

Result: 在30维问题中，约160万个角状态被压缩为256个轨道态，所需量子比特从约21个减少到8个，显著降低了计算资源需求。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种通用的计算原理，实现了对结构化极端依赖系统的可扩展量子模拟。

Abstract: High-dimensional simulation of multivariate extremes is fundamentally limited by the combinatorial complexity of dependence, often more than by the scarcity of extreme observations. We show that symmetry admits a lossless orbit-space representation that preserves structured extreme dependence while replacing an exponentially large dependence space with a compact set of symmetry classes. Based on this principle, we develop Q-Edge (Quantum Extreme Dependence Engine), a symmetry-reduced quantum framework that operates directly in orbit space, enabling scalable simulation and digital twins of structured extreme systems. By transferring symmetry into the data representation rather than the quantum circuit, Q-Edge allows unconstrained quantum generative models to exploit dramatically reduced state spaces. For a 30-dimensional problem, approximately 1.6 million angular states collapse to 256 orbit states, reducing the required quantum representation from about 21 qubits to 8. Our results establish a general computational principle for scalable quantum simulation of structured extreme dependence.

</details>


### [156] [Analog quantum simulation of bosonic and anyonic models with flux-driven transmons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03737)
*Isak Lyngfelt,Jorge Fernández-Pendás,Göran Johansson,Laura Garciá-Álvarez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用可调谐transmon量子比特阵列模拟Bose-Hubbard和anyon-Hubbard模型的通用协议，通过频率调制实现多体跃迁和复数跃迁幅值，首次实现了任意子哈伯德模型在transmon系统中的量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于强关联量子系统难以经典模拟，需要发展可在可控量子硬件中直接实现目标系统动力学的量子模拟方法。

Method: 使用电容耦合的flux-tunable transmons构成晶格，通过交替调制transmon频率共振驱动多体跃迁，并引入相位使跃迁幅值变为复数，从而调控局域相互作用和依赖密度的跃迁幅值。

Result: 数值模拟显示，在实验可实现参数下，能够重现Bose-Hubbard和anyon-Hubbard模型的特征动力学，包括相互作用导致的局域化和任意子量子行走的统计相依赖的不对称性。

Conclusion: 该协议为在超导电路平台上模拟玻色子和任意子多体物理提供了可行方案，拓展了量子模拟在强关联体系中的应用。

Abstract: When quantum particles interact, many-body phenomena that are hard to simulate classically emerge. Quantum analog simulation offers an alternative in which the target system's dynamics is directly realized in controllable quantum hardware. Here, we give a general protocol for simulating the Bose-Hubbard and anyon-Hubbard models using lattices of capacitively coupled flux-tunable transmons. By modulating the transmon frequencies in an alternating pattern, we resonantly drive multiple many-body transitions and can tune the on-site interaction and density-dependent hopping amplitudes for up to three bosons per site, with no additional restriction on the total particle number. By adding phases to the modulation, which renders the transition amplitudes complex-valued, we propose the first simulation protocol for the anyon-Hubbard model with transmons. Numerical simulations of the driven transmon arrays with experimentally realistic parameters reproduce the characteristic dynamics of the target models across a range of interaction strengths and statistical phases, including the interaction-dependent localization and the statistics-dependent asymmetry of the anyonic quantum walk.

</details>


### [157] [Thermodynamic Irreversibility from Inaccessible Endogenous Quantum Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03748)
*Borhan Ahmadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在孤立有限系统中，尽管微观动力学保持信息守恒，为何受限记录下的熵仍会上升。作者通过分解演化为可观测记录与隐藏微观结构的贡献，揭示了熵增源于隐藏的宏观间流，并分析了信息如何被组织以实现熵减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何在信息未丢失的情况下，系统仍表现出不可逆的熵增现象，澄清热力学行为与微观可逆性之间的关系。

Method: 采用投影记录方法，将动力学分解为仅由记录预测的部分和由未解析微观结构携带的修正项，并精确推导熵产生率，分离不同类型的电流并分析其能量间隙振幅。

Result: 发现所有瞬时变化均来自隐藏的宏观态间电流；记录本身的演化始于时间的二阶项；通过调控使电流功率集中可实现低熵宏观态重建；提出了区分隐藏活动与有限步返回的分布级检验方法。

Conclusion: 在指定记录和观测窗口内，不可逆性并非源于微观信息的丢失，而是这些信息未能有效组织恢复宏观秩序的电流。

Abstract: Microscopic unitary dynamics preserves all fine information, yet an isolated finite system can show a robust thermodynamic window in which the entropy associated with a restricted record rises. We ask why information hidden from the current record usually fails to rebuild a low-entropy macrostate. For a fixed projective record, every finite step separates exactly into the evolution predicted from the record alone and an exact correction carried by unresolved microscopic structure. The record-only contribution starts only at second order in time, so all instantaneous change of the record comes from hidden currents between macrostates. We derive the exact entropy rate, separate entropy-spreading from return-oriented currents, and resolve those currents into energy-gap amplitudes. Transitions with the same gap add coherently, revealing how the Hamiltonian and the microscopic state organize hidden information for return. In interacting mixing dynamics the current power is spread over many frequencies; free and deliberately commensurate controls progressively concentrate it, and the engineered dynamics reconstructs a low-entropy macrostate. An independent distribution-level test separates hidden dynamical activity from finite-step return, and an exact classical measure-preserving counterpart shows which parts of the construction are not uniquely quantum. Within the specified record and observation window, irreversibility is therefore not loss of microscopic information, but the failure of that information to organize currents that restore macroscopic order.

</details>


### [158] [Metricity of separable quantum optimal transport with the Hilbert-Schmidt cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03769)
*Tomasz Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了与反对称子空间正交投影相关的可分量子最优传输成本的平方根定义了密度矩阵之间的真正距离，等价于建立了由希尔伯特-施密特距离诱导的二阶Beatty-França量子最优传输的三角不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子最优传输中距离度量的三角不等式问题，特别是在反对称子空间和可分态情况下的度量性质。

Method: 利用凸包对偶性和独立于维度的埃尔米特算子插值技术，替代不可用的粘合论证。

Result: 证明了该构造满足三角不等式，从而确认其为真正的度量，并验证了基于可分SWAP保真度的距离的度量性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了特定量子最优传输构造的度量性质，为量子信息理论中的距离度量提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: We prove that the square root of the separable quantum optimal transport cost associated with the orthogonal projection onto the antisymmetric subspace defines a genuine distance between density matrices. Equivalently, this establishes the triangle inequality for the order-two Beatty-França quantum optimal transport construction induced by the Hilbert-Schmidt distance between pure states. The result also proves metricity of the corresponding distance derived from separable SWAP fidelity. The proof replaces the unavailable gluing argument by convex-roof duality and a dimension-independent interpolation result for Hermitian operators.

</details>


### [159] [Quantisation of Abstract Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03778)
*Mingsheng Ying,Zhicheng Zhang,Kean Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了抽象量子数据类型的代数框架，将经典数据类型的形式化规范提升到量子计算领域，并展示了该方法在量子数组和量子纠错码中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了为量子编程中的数据抽象提供坚实的代数基础，研究如何将经典的等式规范安全地扩展到量子设置中。

Method: 基于泛代数框架引入抽象量子数据类型的概念，定义经典数据类型的量子化，并证明其等式规范可在量子环境下保持正确性，同时涵盖比特预言机和相位预言机作为特例。

Result: 建立了量子数据类型的形式化体系，表明多种经典结构（如嵌入、同构、积）在满足一定条件下可在量子化后得以保留，并应用于量子数组和量子纠错码的建模。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子程序设计中的数据抽象提供了统一的代数基础，展示了经典数据类型向量子领域的系统性扩展是可行且具有良好结构性质的。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a notion of abstract quantum data type within the framework of universal algebra. This notion provides an algebraic foundation for describing data abstraction in quantum programming. We formally define a quantisation of classical data types and show that their equational specifications can be soundly lifted to the quantum setting. Two standard quantisation methods for classical functions, namely the bit oracle and the phase oracle, arise as special cases of this general construction. We illustrate the framework with applications to quantum arrays and quantum error-correcting codes, showing how they can be understood through the lens of data-type quantisation. We further establish conditions under which quantisation preserves structural relationships and constructions of classical data types, including embeddings, isomorphisms, and products.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Query Complexity of Finding a Tarski Fixed Point on a High-Dimensional Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03802)
*Tongyang Li,Weiran Ma,Ziyi Yang,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子查询模型中寻找单调函数Tarski不动点的复杂度下界，利用非负谱对抗方法证明了Ω(k log n)的量子查询下界，并提出了“树-滤过对抗方法”以简化对抗矩阵的构造，该方法为具有树状结构难题的量子复杂性下界分析提供了清晰的组合解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在多个领域有广泛应用，且经典模型下查询复杂度已被广泛研究，但其在量子计算模型下的查询复杂度仍知之甚少，尤其是缺乏紧的下界结果。

Method: 采用非负谱对抗方法（nonnegative spectral adversary method），并提出一种新的技术——树-滤过对抗方法（Tree--Filtration Adversary Method）来构建对抗矩阵，用于证明量子查询复杂度下界。

Result: 证明了在[n]^k上寻找单调函数Tarski不动点的量子查询复杂度下界为Ω(k log n)；在n=2和k=1两个极端情形下，该下界匹配已知的经典下界；当n < k时优于此前的最佳经典下界，当n ≥ k时接近已知经典下界（相差log n / log k因子）。

Conclusion: 本文给出了首个针对Tarski不动点问题的非平凡量子查询下界，并通过新提出的树-滤过对抗方法为具有树状结构问题的量子下界分析提供了有力且直观的新工具。

Abstract: The Knaster-Tarski fixed-point theorem states that every monotone function over a complete lattice has a fixed point. Beyond its fundamental role in order theory, the theorem and its algorithmic variants have found broad applications in areas such as economics, game theory, and programming languages. While the query complexity of finding a Tarski fixed point has been extensively studied in classical models, comparatively little is known in the quantum setting.
  We prove an $Ω(k\log n)$ quantum query lower bound for finding a fixed point of a monotone function on $[n]^k$, using the nonnegative spectral adversary method. In the two extremal regimes $n = 2$ and $k = 1$, our quantum lower bound matches the previous classical lower bounds $Ω(k)$ and $Ω(\log n)$, respectively. For $n, k\geq 2$, our bound improves the best previous classical lower bound when $n < k$ and is within a factor of $\log n / \log k$ compared to the known classical lower bound when $n \geq k$. To construct the adversary matrix, we develop the Tree--Filtration Adversary Method. Besides yielding our lower bound, the method offers a more transparent combinatorial interpretation of the nonnegative spectral adversary method. When the hard instances of a problem admit a tree-like organization and suggest an intuition analogous to classical decision-tree lower bounds, our method provide a promising approach to establishing quantum complexity lower bounds.

</details>


### [161] [Energetic Costs of Subspace Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03825)
*Jakub Czartowski,Felix C. Binder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了子空间量子纠错码中综合征记忆的能耗代价，通过冯·诺依曼熵确定了维持可重用综合征寄存器的理想功的下界，并分析了编码、噪声和测量结构对理想热力学负担的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错过程中，噪声引起的不确定性被转移到存储在辅助内存中的综合征信息，重复操作需要清除这些内存，这不可避免地导致能量消耗。为了理解并优化这一过程的能量成本，本文旨在量化综合征记忆清除所带来的能耗贡献。

Method: 从Knill-Laflamme条件出发，构建了一个有效的综合征态，其冯·诺依曼熵设定了维持可重用综合征寄存器所需理想功的下界。进一步分析了投影式综合征读出产生的额外熵，并通过测量效率低下来量化这种差距。特别针对独立局部Pauli噪声下的稳定子码，分析了两种经典级别的综合征表示：抽象误差标签级别和奇偶校验级别。

Result: 发现单量子比特错误之间的简并性减少了处理恢复标签时的主导阶熵；低权重的检查减少了低噪声环境下由个别测量结果产生的边际熵。识别出保留和单独擦除这些结果所带来的额外负担，即位级低效率，并以五量子比特码、Steane码、广义Shor码和旋转表面码为例说明了这两种成本。

Conclusion: 建立了综合征记忆能耗成本的层次结构，识别出控制子空间量子纠错理想热力学负担的编码、噪声和测量结构。

Abstract: Quantum error correction acts as an entropy pump, transferring noise-induced uncertainty from a protected quantum system into syndrome information stored in an auxiliary memory. Repeated operation requires this memory to be cleared which unavoidably contributes to the energetic cost of error correction. Here, we characterise this contribution for subspace quantum error-correcting codes and identify how it depends on the joint structure of the code, the noise, and the representation of the retained syndrome information. Starting from the Knill-Laflamme conditions, we construct an effective syndrome state whose von Neumann entropy sets a lower bound on the ideal work required to maintain a reusable syndrome register. Projective syndrome readout generally generates additional entropy, and we quantify the resulting gap through measurement inefficiency. We then specialise to stabiliser codes under independent local Pauli noise and analyse two classical levels of syndrome representation. At the level of abstract error labels, degeneracies among single-qubit errors reduce the leading-order entropy of processed recovery labels. At the parity-check level, lower-weight checks reduce the marginal entropy generated by individual measurement outcomes in the low-noise regime. We identify the additional burden associated with retaining and separately erasing these outcomes as a bit-level inefficiency, and illustrate both costs for the five-qubit, Steane, generalised Shor, and rotated surface codes. Our results establish a hierarchy of syndrome-memory energetic costs and identify the code, noise, and measurement structures that control the ideal thermodynamic burden of subspace quantum error correction.

</details>


### [162] [Exact Compatibility Geometry of Three-Qubit Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03864)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了三量子比特系统中成对纠缠与三重纠缠之间的置换对称支持关系，并通过四维兼容体统一了全局关系与CKW关系，给出了三量子比特纯态完全四维兼容性的半代数表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三量子比特系统中不同纠缠度量之间的全局关系，并统一已有结果以揭示纠缠成分间的相容性结构。

Method: 引入四维兼容体概念，利用半代数几何方法对三量子比特纯态的纠缠相容性进行刻画，并推广到任意由纠缠生成的度量。

Result: 建立了成对concurrence与三-tangle之间的极值关系，发现其极值仅由W型和GHZ型态实现；给出了四维兼容体的完整刻画，并实现了缺失两体纠缠分量的无参数区间预测。

Conclusion: 不同纠缠度量间存在统一的几何结构，兼容体方法为多体纠缠的相容性分析提供了普适框架。

Abstract: We present a permutation-symmetric supporting relation between the pairwise concurrences and the three-tangle in three-qubit system, whose extrema are attained only by the symmetric $W$ and GHZ local-unitary classes.
