Furthermore, by introducing the concept of a four-dimensional compatibility body, we show that our proposed global relation and the previous CKW relation can be uniformly viewed as different supporting directions of the same achievable set. Subsequently, we provide a necessary-and-sufficient semi-algebraic characterization of the complete four-dimensional compatibility of a three-qubit pure state. Based on this, we can conversely describe the compatibility between entangled components: given the remaining entangled components, the missing two-body entangled component can only take values within a precise parameter-free interval. We demonstrate that the above construction also applies to arbitrary concurrence-generated entanglement measures.

</details>


### [163] [Spin-to-polarization mapping with a coherent quantum dot-cavity receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03910)
*Adrià Medeiros,Vincent Vinel,Eliott Rambeau,Petr Steindl,Elham Mehdi,Manuel Gundín,Clément Millet,Petr Stepanov,Niccolo Somaschi,Aristide Lemaître,Isabelle Sagnes,Olivier Krebs,Pascale Senellart,Dario A. Fioretto,Loïc Lanco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过利用低噪声带电量子点-微柱腔器件中的自旋诱导克尔旋转，展示了光子偏振态与嵌入量子比特状态之间的一一映射关系，实现了基于单个反射光子检测对电子自旋投影的高保真度测量，并观察了后续自旋弛豫过程，为基于自旋-光子偏振映射的确定性逻辑门奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现光学量子技术中的确定性纠缠门需要光-物质界面能够可控地改变光子状态并与静态量子比特相互作用，关键在于建立散射光子状态与量子比特状态之间的一一对应关系。

Method: 使用时间分辨偏振测量方法，在低噪声带电量子点-微柱腔系统中利用自旋诱导的克尔旋转效应，探测反射光子的偏振变化以反映电子自旋状态。

Result: 通过单个反射光子检测可将电子自旋投影到其本征态，保真度达到95±2%；在三子辐射寿命决定的瞬态过程后，两种正交偏振态可分别对应特定的自旋本征态，并通过第二个反射光子观测到自旋弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 当前性能受限于电子自旋弛豫时间与三子辐射寿命之间的时间尺度竞争，但可通过使用具有更长弛豫时间的空穴自旋来改进，该工作为实现确定性量子逻辑门提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Coherent light-matter interfaces controllably modifying the state of a photon upon interaction with a stationary qubit are a key resource for implementing deterministic entangling gates for optical quantum technologies. This requires a one-to-one mapping between the state of the scattered photon and that of the embedded qubit. Here, we present an experimental signature of such a bijection by leveraging the spin-induced Kerr rotation present in a low-noise charged quantum dot-micropillar cavity device. Through time-resolved polarization measurements, we project the electron spin to one of its eigenstates with $95\pm2\%$ fidelity with a single reflected photon detection, and follow the subsequent spin relaxation through the detection of a second reflected photon. We demonstrate that, after a transient regime governed by the trion radiative lifetime, two orthogonal polarization states can be produced, each associated to a given spin eigenstate. While the current results are limited by a timescale competition between electron spin relaxation and trion radiative lifetime, they could be improved using hole spins displaying increased relaxation times. Our work paves the way towards deterministic logic gates exploiting this one-to-one mapping between a spin and the polarization of a scattered photon.

</details>


### [164] [Ultra-Precise Quantum Projective Designs in Constant Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03925)
*Qingyue Zhang,Junjie Chen,Zhou You,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种稀疏对易电路系综，可在相对误差意义下近似低阶Haar矩，实现渐近最优的对数相互作用度，并支持低深度和少辅助比特的高效实现，适用于多种量子信息任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的Haar随机性代价高昂且通常不必要，因此需要构造资源开销更低但仍能模拟关键Haar统计特性的随机量子对象。

Method: 构造一个由稀疏Clifford相位层和独立单比特Clifford门组成的对易电路系综，利用其内在的矩结构进行组合分析，特别在三阶时引入新的块分解方法。

Result: 该电路系综在相对误差下形成ε-近似的2阶和3阶投影设计，具有渐近最优的对数交互度；可在全连接架构上以O(log(n/ε))量子深度无辅助实现，或以深度七的常数深度（使用O(n log(n/ε))辅助比特）自适应实现。

Conclusion: 稀疏对易动力学可用极少的量子资源产生精确的Haar样统计特性，在随机表征、量子计量、算法和多体物理中有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Random quantum objects are powerful resources for quantum information processing, yet exact Haar randomness is costly and typically unnecessary. We introduce an explicit sparse commuting circuit ensemble on $n$ qubits that reproduces low-order Haar moments in the stringent relative-error sense. The circuit consists of a sparse Clifford phase layer followed by independent single-qubit Clifford gates. Acting on a simple product state, the resulting ensemble forms $ε$-approximate projective $2$- and $3$-designs in relative error, with the required logarithmic interaction degree being asymptotically optimal within this circuit family. It admits an ancilla-free implementation of quantum depth $O(\log(n/ε))$ on an all-to-all architecture, as well as an adaptive constant-depth implementation---in fact, depth seven---using $O(n\log(n/ε))$ ancilla qubits. Departing from existing shallow-design paradigms, our analysis exploits the intrinsic moment structure of commuting phase circuits; at third order, this requires a new block decomposition and combinatorial analysis that also suggests a route toward higher-order shallow designs. Our results show that precise Haar-like statistics can emerge from sparse commuting dynamics with remarkably low quantum resources, with applications to randomized characterization, quantum metrology, quantum algorithms, and many-body physics.

</details>


### [165] [Approximate maximum-likelihood decoding via truncated free energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03928)
*Yuanqi Liu,Weilei Zeng,Junjie Wu,Lingling Lao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为AMLD的黑盒框架，通过重用随机内解码器丢弃的候选样本来估计每个逻辑类的自由能，并选择最小化估计自由能的逻辑类，从而逼近最大似然解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码（MLD）虽然在理论上具有最优性能，但其精确计算是#P难问题，现有方法如最小权重解码（MWD）忽略了陪集简并性，导致性能损失，因此需要一种更优的近似方法。

Method: 引入AMLD框架，利用被随机解码器丢弃的候选样本，构建每类截断的自由能估计器，并选择使估计自由能最小的逻辑类作为输出，具有线性经典开销。

Result: 在伊辛解码器基准测试中，AMLD在环面码和色码上闭合了MWD与MLD之间最多83%的阈值差距；在6.6.6色码下，阈值从17.28%提升至18.62%；在[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码上，AMLD相比MWD在p=0.05时将逻辑错误率降低了13%。

Conclusion: AMLD是一种通用且高效的近似最大似然解码方法，能够在不依赖特定编码结构的情况下显著提升解码性能。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD) achieves the minimum logical error rate of stabilizer codes under known i.i.d. Pauli noise, but its exact evaluation is \#P-hard. Practical pipelines therefore approximate MLD by minimum-weight decoding (MWD), retaining only the lowest-weight recovery per syndrome and discarding the coset degeneracy. The minimum-weight search is in turn implemented by stochastic solvers. We introduce approximate maximum-likelihood decoding (AMLD), a black-box framework that recycles the candidate samples discarded by stochastic inner decoders into a per-class truncated free-energy estimator. For every logical class represented in the candidate pool, the estimator is provably bounded below by the exact free energy and above by the empirical minimum weight. AMLD returns the logical class minimizing the estimated free energy with linear classical overhead. In SA-based Ising-decoder benchmarks, AMLD closes up to $83\%$ of the MWD--MLD threshold gap across the toric and color codes under bit-flip and depolarizing noise. The largest threshold improvement, from $17.28\%$ to $18.62\%$, occurs on the $6.6.6$ color code under depolarizing noise. We further demonstrate AMLD on the $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate-bicycle code, whose bit-flip decoding problem has a hypergraph structure. This application requires neither matching-based enumeration nor code-specific tensor-network contraction. At $p=0.05$, AMLD reduces the logical error rate by $13\%$ relative to MWD evaluated on the same BP-OSD candidate pool.

</details>


### [166] [On the geometry and typicality of quantum magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03944)
*Zhenhuan Liu,Z-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了满足特定纯度条件的量子态位于稳定子多面体内且无魔性，揭示了高维稳定子多面体的几何复杂性，并给出了随机诱导态具有魔性的相变现象及多面体面数的指数级上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究n量子比特系统中量子态的魔性特征及其在稳定子多面体中的分布，理解高维下无魔态区域的几何结构和典型性。

Method: 结合纯度约束与高维多面体几何性质，分析稳定子多面体的内径、体积半径和面数，并利用Haar随机纯态的部分迹构造随机诱导态，研究其魔性的相变行为。

Result: 1. 纯度Tr(ρ²)≤1/(d−a∗)的态属于稳定子多面体；2. 给出了稳定子多面体希尔伯特-施密特内径和体积半径的定量估计；3. 随机诱导态在k⋆维度附近发生魔性相变，k⋆介于Ω(d²/log²d)与O(d²)之间；4. 稳定子多面体的面数在exp[Ω(d²/log²d)]到exp[O(d²log²d)]之间。

Conclusion: 稳定子多面体在高维下展现出接近最大可能的几何复杂性，其精确描述需要双指数数量的线性不等式，表明无魔态区域的刻画极为复杂。

Abstract: We prove that, for an $n$-qubit system of dimension $d=2^n$, every state satisfying $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^2)\le 1/(d-a_\ast)$, with $a_\ast=0.458327\cdots$, lies inside the stabilizer polytope and is therefore magic-free. Combining this result with general geometric properties of high-dimensional polytopes, we establish quantitative estimates for the Hilbert--Schmidt inradius and volume radius of the stabilizer polytope, and use them to characterize the typicality of magic in random induced states obtained by tracing out a $k$-dimensional subsystem from a $d\times k$-dimensional Haar-random pure state. We prove a sharp phase transition in the probability of such states having magic, whose transition dimension $k_\star$ is bounded between $Ω(d^2/\log^2d)$ and $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$. We further prove that the number of facets of the stabilizer polytope lies between $\exp[Ω(d^2/\log^2 d)]$ and $\exp[\mathcal{O}(d^2\log^2 d)]$, substantially improving upon the previous quasipolynomial lower bound and implying that any exact description of the magic-free region requires a doubly exponential number of linear inequalities in the number of qubits. Overall, our results show that the stabilizer polytope exhibits near-maximal geometric complexity allowed for a high-dimensional polytope with a certain number of vertices.

</details>


### [167] [Robust multi-hypothesis quantum-state discrimination under unknown common unitary perturbations via least favorable priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04020)
*Daichi Fujiki,Fuyuhiko Tanaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在未知共同酉扰动的情况下鲁棒的K元量子态区分问题，提出使用基于最不利先验（LFP）的贝叶斯最优联合测量，并通过半定规划计算LFP，显著提升了最坏情况下的成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子态识别中，未知的公共酉扰动作为干扰因素会影响测量性能，传统的固定测量策略在扰动下表现不佳，因此需要设计对扰动具有鲁棒性的测量方案。

Method: 将问题建模为对扰动参数空间的minimax决策问题，采用基于最不利先验（LFP）的贝叶斯最优联合测量；对于离散化的参数空间，通过半定规划计算LFP，并验证其对应的成功概率等于有限网格上的minimax成功概率。

Result: 数值实验表明，LFP基测量显著平坦化了成功概率曲线，提升了最坏情况下的成功概率，优于参考点最优测量和均匀先验贝叶斯测量；同时LFP权重集中在限制判别性能的关键扰动区域，具有诊断价值。

Conclusion: 基于LFP的测量不仅提供了鲁棒的量子态区分方案，还能识别影响性能的关键扰动区域，兼具构造性和诊断性优势。

Abstract: We study robust K-ary quantum-state discrimination when all candidate states are affected by the same unknown common unitary perturbation. The unknown perturbation does not represent the label to be identified, but acts as a nuisance factor that changes the performance of a fixed measurement. We formulate the problem as a minimax decision problem over the possible perturbations and propose using the Bayes-optimal collective measurement associated with a least favorable prior (LFP) on the nuisance-parameter space. For a finite discretization of this space, we show that the LFP can be computed by a semidefinite program and that the corresponding value coincides with the finite-grid minimax success probability. As numerical demonstrations, we consider a binary nonorthogonal qubit model and a nonorthogonal three-state qutrit model with an unknown common unitary perturbation. The LFP-based measurement substantially flattens the success-probability profile and improves the worst-case success probability compared with a reference-point optimal measurement and a uniform-prior Bayes measurement. The resulting LFP concentrates its weight on regions of the nuisance-parameter space that actively limit the robust discrimination performance, thereby providing both a constructive measurement design and a diagnostic description of the difficult nuisance-parameter regimes.

</details>


### [168] [Quantum-Enhanced Phase Estimation with Photon-Added Even and Odd Coherent States in an SU(1,1) Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04064)
*Abdelmajid El Maaroufi,Mouad Ait Maskour,Bouchra Maroufi,Mohammed Daoud,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在SU(1,1)干涉仪中使用m光子添加的偶极和奇极相干态作为非经典输入资源时的相位估计性能，发现光子添加显著提升了相位敏感性和量子Fisher信息，并使精度逐渐接近海森堡极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用非经典态（如光子添加的偶极/奇极相干态）提升干涉仪中的相位估计精度，突破标准量子极限。

Method: 采用强度探测和误差传播法评估相位敏感性，同时基于量子Cramér-Rao界和量子Fisher信息分析最终精度极限。

Result: 光子添加显著增强相位敏感性、提高量子Fisher信息并降低量子Cramér-Rao界；灵敏度超越标准量子极限并趋近于海森堡标度；偶极态略优于奇极态，但随着光子添加数增加，两者差异减小。

Conclusion: m光子添加的相干态能有效提升SU(1,1)干涉仪的相位估计精度，且随着光子数增加，系统对初始态奇偶性的依赖减弱，显示出向最优量子标度逼近的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate phase estimation in an SU(1,1) interferometer employing $m$-photon-added even and odd coherent states as nonclassical input resources. The phase sensitivity is evaluated through intensity detection and the error propagation method, while the ultimate precision limit is determined from the quantum Cramér-Rao bound with the quantum Fisher information serving as the relevant metrological quantity. Our results demonstrate that photon addition significantly enhances the phase sensitivity, increases the quantum Fisher information, and reduces the quantum Cramér-Rao bound, leading to a clear improvement over the corresponding even and odd coherent states. Furthermore, the achievable sensitivity exceeds the standard quantum limit and gradually approaches the Heisenberg scaling with increasing photon-addition number. We also find that the $m$-photon-added even coherent states exhibit a modest advantage over their odd counterparts. As $m$ increases, however, this distinction becomes progressively weaker, suggesting that photon addition diminishes the role of the initial parity of the coherent state in determining the interferometric performance.

</details>


### [169] [Small-quench Loschmidt dynamics near quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04065)
*Kohei Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了量子临界点附近小突然淬火后的短时Loschmidt动力学，发现初始二次增长由淬火算符的方差密度决定，并与静态关联函数直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子临界点附近的非平衡动力学行为，建立淬火响应与静态临界关联之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析淬火后Loschmidt率函数的早期演化，结合关联函数和标度理论，研究有限尺寸下的标度行为。

Result: 推导出Loschmidt率函数的初始二次系数等于淬火算符的方差密度，该密度对应于初态中关联函数的空间积分；在一维系统中，该系数可能有限、对数增强或代数增强。

Conclusion: 短时Loschmidt响应可作为探测量子临界关联的有效手段，其行为由淬火算符的标度维度决定。

Abstract: We study the short-time Loschmidt dynamics after a small sudden quench near a quantum critical point. We show that the initial quadratic growth of the Loschmidt rate function is governed by the variance of the quench operator per system size. For a local quench operator, this variance density is exactly equal to the spatial sum of the equal-time connected two-point correlation function in the initial ground state. This relation connects the early-time Loschmidt response to static critical correlations. Assuming power-law correlations at criticality, we classify the finite-size scaling of the short-time coefficient by the scaling dimension of the quench operator. In one dimension, the coefficient is finite, logarithmically enhanced, or algebraically enhanced with system size. We illustrate this operator dependence in the transverse-field Ising chain, where transverse-field and longitudinal-field quenches couple to different critical operators. We also discuss the first correction beyond the quadratic regime using the fourth cumulant of the post-quench Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [170] [Algebraic Operator Decomposition: A Partitioned Architecture for Noise-Resilient Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04076)
*Wladimir Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种算子分解架构（AOD），将全局算子映射为可独立执行的局部算子，降低量子电路深度，以经典重构和采样开销为代价，并基于Monoid和加法阿贝尔群上的线性算子向量空间理论，验证其在内积、级数展开、迹和卷积等场景的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低复杂量子电路的最大深度，缓解量子噪声影响，同时兼容现有量子纠错与错误缓解方法，需在量子执行前进行代数层面的预分解。

Method: 提出算子分解架构（AOD），基于Monoid设计模式（结合函数满足结合律且有单位元），构建MapReduce编程模型，使用加法(+)作为归约操作；定义加法阿贝尔群上线性算子的向量空间，实现对内积、级数展开、迹、卷积等操作的分解。

Result: 实现了将全局量子算子分解为局部可独立执行算子的方法，降低了量子电路深度；仿真结果显示该方法利用具有零‘负概率税’或相位冲突的稳定交换Monoid，具备良好数学基础与可行性。

Conclusion: AOD为量子计算提供了一种新的程序结构范式，可在不依赖硬件纠错的前提下有效压缩电路深度，适用于多种线性算子运算，是量子算法设计中有潜力的前置代数优化手段。

Abstract: We present an operator-decomposition architecture that mathematically maps a global operator into independently executable local operators, reducing the maximum quantum circuit depth at the cost of classical reconstruction and sampling overhead. By framing complex Quantum Circuits around an operator in a vector space that can be algebraically pre-decomposed, AOD complements quantum error correction and error-mitigation approaches by performing algebraic decomposition before quantum execution.
  Our approach leans in the computer science definition of a Monoid: a design pattern and mathematical concept consisting of a data type, a combining function that is associative, and a safe identity (neutral) element that does not change other values when combined. Simulation wise we define a MapReduce programming model where the addition (+) is the reducer, thus leveraging a naturally stable commutative monoid which carries zero "negative-probability tax" or phase conflicts.
  Furthermore, we define a Vector Space of Linear Operators over Additive Abelian Groups that benefit from this paradigm, including: Inner Products, Series expansions, Traces and Convolutions. Finally, we present the mathematical foundations and simulation results for this paradigm.

</details>


### [171] [Frequency-Multiplexed Parallel Gates for Quantum LDPC Codes in a Two-Dimensional Ion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04081)
*G. -X. Tang,L. -M. Duan,Y. -K. Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于频率复用的二维离子晶体中实现并行非局域纠缠门的方法，用于高效实现量子低密度奇偶校验码，具有高容错性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码在测量综合征时需要非局域纠缠门的问题，避免因物理移动量子比特导致的操作速度下降。

Method: 利用频率复用技术，在二维离子晶体上实现并行的非局域纠缠门；通过绝热条件抑制门的不保真度和串扰误差，并提高对囚禁频率慢漂移的鲁棒性。

Result: 在一个512离子的二维晶体上实现了[[248,10,18]]双变量自行车码的数值示例，优化了量子比特映射和频率带分配，表明适度激光功率即可实现并行操作，并在现实噪声参数下达到10^{-12}的逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 该方法为在离子阱系统中高效实现高码率qLDPC码提供了可行路径，具备良好的可扩展性和容错性能。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes admit high encoding rates but require nonlocal entangling gates for syndrome measurement. Instead of physically moving the qubits which slows down with the increasing qubit number, here we propose to achieve parallel nonlocal entangling gates on a two-dimensional (2D) ion crystal using frequency-multiplexing. Adiabatic conditions ensure the suppression of gate infidelity and crosstalk error, as well as their robustness against slow drift in the trap frequency which is a leading error source in ion trap. We consider a numerical example of a $[[248,10,18]]$ bivariate bicycle code on a 2D crystal of 512 ions. By optimizing the mapping of the qubits and the assignment of the frequency bands for multiplexing, we show that a moderate laser power is sufficient for parallelism, and that a logical error rate of $10^{-12}$ can be achieved under realistic noise parameters.

</details>


### [172] [Gravitationally Induced Entanglement Across an Event Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04104)
*Hatim Salih*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，在黑洞事件视界两侧的自由下落空间叠加态之间可以从无到有地产生纠缠，但径向提取该纠缠态会因非惯性减速引发软引力子辐射，导致退相干；而横向通过量子擦除可实现纠缠提取，揭示时空几何对纠缠的退化与远程传输之间的几何对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统认为穿过事件视界的粒子无法再与外部粒子纠缠的观点，探索引力背景下量子纠缠的动力学生成与提取机制。

Method: 基于引力推迟势模型，分析自由下落空间叠加态在跨越事件视界后如何生成纠缠，并研究径向与横向提取过程中非惯性运动及软引力子辐射的影响。

Result: 发现跨视界的纠缠可以动态生成，但径向提取会因软引力子轫致辐射导致严格相位退相干界限（Γ≥729/160πΦ）；而宏观光学质量系统可通过横向量子擦除实现纠缠 harvesting，并展示时空几何导致的纠缠退化与横向量子态传输的对偶性。

Conclusion: 时空几何结构决定了纠缠的可提取性：径向拖拽导致不可逆退相干，而横向允许纠缠态向无穷远传输，揭示了引力与量子信息之间的深刻几何联系。

Abstract: It has long been assumed that a particle---having crossed a black hole event horizon unentangled with another particle in the exterior universe---can no longer dynamically entangle with that particle. Here, we demonstrate within a gravitational retarded-potential model that entanglement can in fact be created from scratch between freely falling spatial superpositions across an event horizon. Yet, extracting this state radially requires non-inertial deceleration, triggering soft-graviton bremsstrahlung, and establishing a strict dephasing bound in terms of entangling phase, $Γ\ge\frac{729}{160π}Φ$. This decoheres the entangled state. By contrast, equivalent macroscopic optical masses allow local tangential harvesting of entanglement via quantum erasure, thus revealing a remarkable geometric duality: Spacetime irreversibly degrades entanglement the moment the localized mass is dragged away from the horizon, while allowing the transverse teleportation of its entangled state to infinity.

</details>


### [173] [Non-local Magic: closed-form solution and equivalence with magic of purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04119)
*Michele Viscardi,Lorenzo Leone,Alioscia Hamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数稳定子保真度的非局域魔法和纯化魔法的解析表征，揭示了它们在混合态资源理论中的深刻联系，并证明了非局域魔法等于约化密度算符酉轨道上纯化魔法的最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域魔法用于量化无法通过局域幺正变换消除的双部分量子态的非稳定化性，但其计算通常涉及复杂的优化问题。本文旨在为这一难题提供解析解决方案，并建立其在混合态资源理论中的解释。

Method: 利用对数稳定子保真度作为魔法度量，结合施密特谱分析与乌尔曼保真度的距离形式，推导出非局域魔法和纯化魔法的闭式解析解，并构建相应的资源理论框架。

Result: 证明了对于对数稳定子保真度，非局域魔法具有仅依赖于施密特谱的精确闭式解；引入了纯化魔法的概念并给出其基于距离的表述；进一步证明非局域魔法等于在约化密度算符的酉轨道上纯化魔法的最小值。

Conclusion: 该研究为非局域魔法提供了高效的解析刻画，并赋予其混合态资源理论的自然解释，揭示了纯化过程与非局域魔法之间的本质联系。

Abstract: Non-local magic quantifies the non-stabilizerness of a bipartite quantum state that cannot be removed by local unitary transformations. Despite its natural definition, its evaluation generally requires a difficult optimization over local unitaries. Here, we show that for the log-stabilizer fidelity this optimization admits an exact closed-form analytic solution depending only on the Schmidt spectrum. We then introduce the magic of purification, defined as the minimum pure-state magic over all purifications of a mixed state, and show that it naturally induces a resource theory whose free states are normalized stabilizer-code projectors. For the log-stabilizer fidelity, the magic of purification admits a distance-based formulation in terms of the Uhlmann fidelity. Remarkably, we prove that non-local magic coincides with the minimum magic of purification along the unitary orbit of the reduced density operator. Our results provide both an efficient analytical characterization and a mixed-state resource-theoretic interpretation of non-local magic.

</details>


### [174] [Characterizing Large Scale Quantum Systems with Error Per Circuit Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04132)
*Travis Hurant,Arian Vezvaee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Aniket Dalvi,Jude Alnas,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重叠的电路级量子基准测试方法EPCL，通过在两个独立量子寄存器上运行相同的随机电路并测量输出态之间的重叠来评估系统性能，无需理想输出的经典模拟或特定门集限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路级量子基准方法存在依赖结构化门集、需要经典模拟理想输出或无法反映全寄存器行为等问题，因此需要一种更通用且实用的性能评估手段。

Method: 提出Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL) 方法，通过对两个独立量子寄存器施加相同随机电路，测量其输出态重叠随电路深度的变化，估计有效层极化率。

Result: 推导了去极化模型下的重叠加权衰减行为，数值模拟表明EPCL在弱局部随机噪声下能准确恢复极化参数，在强噪声下仍保持单指数衰减；实验在IBM量子设备上验证了8和16量子比特系统的EPCL衰减现象。

Conclusion: EPCL是一种无需经典模拟理想输出且兼容任意门集的电路级量子基准方法，适用于评估全寄存器的累积噪声影响，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum benchmarks provide compact measures of performance that are important for evaluating and comparing quantum systems. Circuit-level benchmarks are particularly valuable because they capture the accumulated effects of noise across interacting operations, but existing approaches may require structured gate sets and costly compilation, classical simulation of reference outputs, or subsystem decompositions that do not capture full-register behavior. We introduce Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL), an overlap-based circuit-level benchmark that estimates an effective layer polarization by applying identical random circuits to two disjoint quantum registers and measuring the overlap between their output states as a function of circuit depth. EPCL avoids classical simulation of ideal output distributions and recovery to a known reference state, and is compatible with arbitrary gate sets, including non-Clifford gates.
  We derive the expected overlap decay under an ensemble-averaged depolarizing model and identify the assumptions under which the fitted decay parameter represents an effective layer polarization. Numerical simulations show that EPCL recovers the predicted polarization under weak local stochastic noise and remains well described by a single-exponential decay at stronger stochastic noise levels. The simulations further show that coherent errors associated with fixed entangling layers may require Pauli twirling or randomized compiling to produce the expected decay, while inter-register correlations contribute an additional covariance term to the measured overlap. Finally, experiments on IBM quantum hardware demonstrate clear EPCL decay in 8- and 16-qubit implementations. These results support EPCL as a method for measuring aggregate register performance without requiring classical simulation of ideal circuit outputs or restriction to structured gate sets.

</details>


### [175] [Efficient Conversion of Optical to Mechanical States Close to the Single-Quantum Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04150)
*Alexander Rolf Korsch,Liu Chen,Pedro V. Pinho,Boris Müllendorff,Jan N. Kirchhof,Yong Yu,Thiago P. Mayer Alegre,Simon Gröblacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在毫开尔文温度下实现了接近单量子水平的高效低噪声光子-声子态转换，利用弱相干光脉冲在光机械晶体中实现了高达0.76的转换效率和7.3微秒的机械存储寿命，展示了相干声子态的低热噪声转换，为确定性制备单量子级机械态提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现光学光子与机械激发之间的相干接口对于声子态工程和混合量子信息处理至关重要，但此前的工作受限于室温下的热噪声和经典信号输入，难以达到单量子水平的操作。

Method: 使用在毫开尔文温度下运行的光机械晶体系统，通过弱相干光输入脉冲（few-photon level）实现光子-声子态转换，并利用Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量验证转换后声子态的相干性和低热噪声水平。

Result: 实现了0.76的光子-声子转换效率、7.3微秒的机械存储寿命、超过4.5 MHz的可调转换带宽，以及仅9.0个热声子的低添加热噪声。

Conclusion: 该结果证明了光机械晶体作为GHz机械模式高效光学接口的潜力，为未来在确定性条件下制备单量子级别的机械态奠定了基础。

Abstract: Coherent interfaces between optical photons and mechanical excitations provide a promising route towards phonon-state engineering and hybrid quantum information processing. Cavity optomechanical systems enable such interfaces via optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT), allowing coherent mapping between traveling optical fields and localized mechanical modes. However, previous implementations of OMIT conversion protocols were limited to classical input signals with large coherent state photon numbers due to room temperature operation and corresponding thermal mechanical noise. Here, we demonstrate efficient low-noise photon-phonon state transfer close to the single-quantum regime in an optomechanical crystal operated at Millikelvin temperatures. Using weak coherent optical input pulses at the few-photon level, we achieve a record-level photon-phonon conversion efficiency of $η=0.76$, a mechanical storage lifetime of $T_\mathrm{1}=7.3~μs$, and a tunable conversion bandwidth exceeding 4.5 MHz. Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements of the retrieved signal demonstrate the coherent nature of the converted phononic state, evidencing low added thermal noise in the conversion process ($n_\mathrm{th}=9.0$). These results establish optomechanical crystals as efficient optical interfaces to GHz mechanical modes and provide a pathway toward deterministic single-quantum-level mechanical state preparation.

</details>


### [176] [Vanilla Exact Synthesis of CNOT Circuits is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04160)
*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了在无额外约束的普通精确CNOT综合问题中，其决策版本是NP完全的，优化版本是NP难的，并通过哈密顿路径问题的归约完成了证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基本设置下精确CNOT综合问题的计算复杂性长期未定的问题，特别是在无连接限制、无辅助比特、全连接情况下的复杂性。

Method: 通过将网格图上的哈密顿路径问题经由一元编码等距嵌入超立方体，再将其编码为CNOT综合问题；引入记录用辅助量子比特以在最终变换中编码路径访问信息，从而强制电路实现所需路径结构。

Result: 证明了普通精确CNOT综合问题的决策版本是NP完全的，优化版本是NP难的；该结果进一步表明GL(n,2)上的最短字问题、Cayley图上的距离计算、XOR程序最小化及相位多项式电路的精确综合等问题也是困难的。

Conclusion: 精确CNOT综合在最基础设定下已是计算上难以处理的问题，揭示了其内在复杂性，并为多个相关合成与代数问题提供了硬度依据。

Abstract: Exact CNOT synthesis asks for a minimum-size CNOT circuit implementing an invertible linear transformation. Although several related synthesis models have been shown to be computationally hard, their hardness proofs rely on additional structure such as restricted qubit connectivity, encoded inputs, or unrestricted intermediate variables. The complexity of the most basic setting---identity input, a fixed number of labelled qubits, no ancillas, and all-to-all CNOT connectivity---had remained unresolved. In this work, we prove that the decision version of this vanilla exact CNOT synthesis problem is NP-complete, and consequently that its optimization version is NP-hard. Our proof gives a polynomial-time reduction from the Hamiltonian-path problem on grid graphs in two steps. First, we isometrically embed the grid graph into a hypercube via a unary encoding map. We then encode this hypercube Hamiltonian path problem into vanilla exact CNOT synthesis. The main challenge is that CNOT synthesis specifies only the final parity matrix and cannot directly enforce the intermediate vertex visits required by a Hamiltonian path. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce extra recorder qubits that encode the required intermediate vertex visits into the final transformation, forcing any CNOT circuit implementation to realize the intended path structure. Beyond CNOT synthesis, our result directly implies hardness for several related problems, including the shortest word problem over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, distance computation on Cayley graphs over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, minimization of sequential XOR programs, and exact synthesis of phase polynomial circuits.

</details>


### [177] [Parameterised graph theory for tensor networks: entanglement rerouting, structural simplification, and agnostic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04165)
*Matthias C. Caro,Natalie McHugh,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用参数化图论研究张量网络态（TNS）在矩阵乘积态（MPS）和树状张量网络（TTN）表示中的复杂性，揭示了图参数如割宽、树割宽和学习复杂度对表示开销和学习复杂性的影响，并提出了适用于任意图结构的可实现与非可实现场景下的张量网络学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解图结构参数如何影响张量网络态的表示效率和学习复杂性，尤其是在MPS和TTN表示中的适用性和资源开销。

Method: 采用参数化图论方法，结合纠缠重路由技术，分析割宽、树割宽等图参数对表示开销的影响；扩展已有MPS学习算法至TTN及任意图上的张量网络，并提出新的图参数“学习复杂度”。

Result: 证明割宽和树割宽控制MPS/TTN表示的键维开销，树割宽还控制TTN中子系统的局部维度；给出依赖图结构的样本与计算复杂度上界；提出首个具有图依赖复杂性保证的非真实设定下张量网络学习算法。

Conclusion: 图的结构性参数（如割宽、树割宽、度数和树宽）决定了张量网络态的高效表示与可学习性，为张量网络模拟与量子态层析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Parameterised graph theory studies how the complexity of graph-theoretic problems depends on structural parameters of the input graph. This perspective has proved useful in analysing tensor-network simulation (Markov and Shi, 2008). Its implications for tensor-network representations and tomography are less well understood. In particular, which graph parameters determine whether a tensor-network state (TNS) admits a tractable matrix product state (MPS) or tree tensor network (TTN) representation, and which control the complexity of learning the state?
  We address these questions using parameterised graph theory. First, we show that cutwidth and tree-cutwidth bound the bond dimension overhead required to represent a TNS as an MPS or TTN. In the TTN case, tree-cutwidth also bounds the local dimension of the grouped subsystems. The proofs are based on entanglement rerouting, a tensor-network analogue of rerouting information in a classical network. Second, we derive graph-dependent upper bounds on the sample and computational complexity of realisable TNS tomography, with exponents that depend on cutwidth, tree-cutwidth, and a new graph parameter, learning complexity, which we bound in terms of degree and treewidth. We obtain these results by extending the disentangling MPS learner of (Cramer et al., 2010), as analysed further in (Bakshi et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025), to TTNs and to tensor networks on arbitrary known graphs. Finally, we extend the framework beyond the realisable setting. For an arbitrary input state, our agnostic learner outputs a pure state whose fidelity is within additive error $ε$ of the optimum over tensor-network states on the given graph with a given bond dimension, with explicit graph-dependent bounds on sample and computational complexity.

</details>


### [178] [Measurements on the separated subsystems of an entangled state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04178)
*Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，通过考虑张量积空间所源自的自由向量空间中的（陪集）态，来解析分离子系统上的测量投影，从而阐明纠缠态在分离测量下的行为，无需引入随机坍缩，非局域性源于子系统测量对结果的投影方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解在分离的子系统上进行测量时，如何从张量积基中解析出相关的纠缠态，现有方法对此尚不明确，尤其对于不可分态缺乏有效手段。

Method: 利用张量积空间构建背后的自由向量空间中的（陪集）态，分析一般纠缠态在分离子系统测量下的投影行为。

Result: 成功得到了任意一般纠缠态在分离子系统测量下可检测到的投影；该方法无需引入波函数的随机坍缩假设。

Conclusion: 非局域性的出现是由于对每个分离子系统的测量方式决定了可能结果的投影，而非瞬时超距作用，从而为量子纠缠的非局域性提供了新的解释框架。

Abstract: The entangled states of composite quantum systems are well studied. The particle-like nature of these systems also means that, while entangled, they can be physically separated and measurements performed on the separated subsystems. Measurements on the separated subsystems A and B should pertain to vectors in the local Hilbert space of each subsystem, but to date it has not been clear how to elucidate the relevant states of the separated subsystems because it is not obvious how to resolve them from states given in the tensor product basis, except for the separable states. Here it is shown that the projections of any general entangled state that are detected by measurements on the separated subsystems can be obtained considering the corresponding (cosets of) states in the free vector space from which the tensor product space is defined. The result eliminates the need to invoke random collapse, and from this perspective nonlocality arises because of the way measurements on each separated subsystem projects possible measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum communication and Bell nonlocality require infinite classical communication to simulate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04182)
*Carlos de Gois,Thyago S. R. Santos,Carlos Vieira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了任意固定维度量子系统经典模拟的难题，发现从四维（ququart）开始无法用有限的经典通信精确模拟量子通信和纠缠关联，但在三维（qutrit）情况下仍可实现，构造了使用357比特精确模拟qutrit通信的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子通信和量子关联能否被有限的经典通信模拟，尤其是在高维量子系统中的行为，以揭示量子与经典信息处理的根本差异。

Method: 通过构造性协议设计与信息论下界分析，结合共享随机性模型，研究不同量子维度下经典模拟所需的通信资源，并证明在四维及以上时不存在有限经典通信的精确模拟方案。

Result: 证明了从四维量子系统开始，无法用有限的经典通信精确模拟其通信过程及纠缠关联；而在三维情况下，提出了使用357个经典比特精确模拟qutrit通信的显式协议。

Conclusion: 量子系统经典模拟的能力在维度四出现质变，表明高维量子系统具有更强的非经典性，无法被有限经典资源完全复制，揭示了量子信息处理中维度依赖的本质特征。

Abstract: A quantum system of any fixed dimension can be prepared in a continuum of states, yet it cannot be used to transmit an unlimited amount of classical information. Similarly, the correlations observed between measurement outcomes on separate parts of a shared quantum system can be stronger than classical correlations, but they cannot transmit information. These fundamental limitations suggest that the statistics observed from quantum communication and quantum correlations may admit a simulation using a finite amount of classical communication. This expectation is confirmed in the smallest nontrivial quantum dimension, with two classical bits being necessary and sufficient to exactly simulate qubit communication and all correlations between qubits. Despite significant efforts during the previous decades, this remained the only solved case. Here we resolve both problems for every quantum dimension. The solution reveals an unexpected qualitative transition starting at dimension four: no finite amount of classical communication can exactly simulate ququart communication nor all quantum correlations of two entangled ququarts, even with unlimited shared randomness. One might have expected this transition, if it existed, to appear already for qutrits. Instead, we construct an explicit protocol that exactly simulates qutrit communication using $357$ classical bits, and consequently, all correlations of two entangled qutrits.

</details>
