# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [PSDWII: Physical-Structure-Driven Waveform Inversion Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03380)
*Shengchang Chen*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理结构驱动的波形反演成像框架（PSDWII），将地震波传播分解为三步物理过程，通过虚拟源机制统一表示全波形反演、地层物性成像和地层结构成像，实现了无需构建目标函数和梯度计算的新型反演方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波形反演依赖数学优化框架，需构建目标函数并计算梯度和Hessian矩阵，易受初始模型影响且计算成本高。本文旨在建立一种更符合物理机制的反演范式，避免这些数值难题。

Method: 基于地震数据与地下虚拟源直接相关的认识，将波传播分解为激发、散射（生成具有辐射特性的虚拟源）和接收三个步骤，利用传播算子的伴随（逆时延拓）构建虚拟源的线性反演投影，并通过区分不同虚拟源表达式实现多参数解耦，采用去卷积和线性反演分别处理FWI和角度域共成像道集反演。

Result: 建立了统一的数学框架，实现了全波形反演、地层物性成像和地层结构成像的物理机制统一表达；提出了三种具体方法：PSDFWI、PSDSI和PSDMig；新框架无需构造目标函数或计算梯度/Hessian逆。

Conclusion: PSDWII框架成功将波形反演从‘数学优化驱动’转变为‘物理结构驱动’，提供了一种更具物理意义、计算效率更高且稳定性更强的反演新范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Physical-Structure-Driven Waveform Inversion Imaging framework (PSDWII), grounded in the recognition that surface-recorded seismic data are directly related to subsurface virtual sources rather than to the model parameters themselves. Based on this recognition, we decompose seismic wave propagation into a three-step physical process: source excitation and incident wave propagation; interaction of the incident wave with heterogeneities, which generates virtual sources with distinct radiation patterns and excites secondary waves; and propagation and reception of the secondary waves. The adjoint of the propagation operator (reverse-time extrapolation) is then used to formulate a linear inversion projection for the virtual sources. By distinguishing different virtual-source expressions and their underlying physical mechanisms, we establish a unified mathematical representation for three inversion tasks: Full Waveform Inversion(FWI), stratigraphic physical properties imaging, and stratigraphic structures imaging. Deconvolution and linear inversion are respectively adopted to achieve multi-parameter decoupling in FWI and in angle-domain common-image gather inversion for stratigraphic physical properties imaging. The PSDWII framework shifts the conventional ``mathematical optimization driven'' paradigm of waveform inversion to a ``physical-structure-driven'' one. It neither constructs objective functions nor computes their gradients or Hessian inverses. Within this framework, we develop three specific methods: physical-structure-driven FWI (PSDFWI), physical-structure-driven stratigraphic physical properties imaging with angle-domain common-image gather inversion (PSDSI), and physical-structure-driven stratigraphic structures imaging (PSDMig).

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Improving precipitation forecasts in an AI weather model using observational data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03210)
*Julian F. Schmitt,Bertrand Delorme,Robert C. King,Yashica Patodia,Tapio Schneider,Aditi Sheshadri,Ravi Jain*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 通过将基于观测的降水数据直接纳入训练，可以显著改善降水预报。本研究使用IMERG数据微调图变换器架构，在中程降水预测中取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人工智能天气预报模型主要依赖于存在已知偏差（特别是降水方面）的ERA5再分析数据集进行训练，因此需要改进降水预测的准确性。

Method: 采用图变换器架构，并使用0.25°分辨率的IMERG降水数据进行微调，以提高对降水事件的预测能力。

Result: 该模型在中程连续排序概率得分上最多提升了19%，在极端降雨预测的Brier技巧评分上全球提高了57%，但在最强降水事件上物理模型仍更可靠。

Conclusion: 将基于观测的降水数据直接纳入AI天气预报模型训练可显著提升降水预报性能，尤其是在热带风暴和毛毛雨事件上表现更优。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence weather prediction (AIWP) systems now surpass state-of-the-art physical models for medium-range weather forecasting. Current global AIWP models are trained almost exclusively using one reanalysis dataset, ERA5, but it has known biases, particularly for precipitation. Here we fine-tune a graph-transformer architecture with IMERG precipitation data at 0.25° resolution. The resulting model improves medium-range continuous ranked probability scores by up to 19%, while also demonstrating superior skill for tropical storms and drizzle events. Our model exceeds the Brier skill score of state-of-the-art operational models on extreme rainfall prediction by 57% globally; however, a physics-based operational model remains more reliable for the heaviest precipitation events. Our results demonstrate that incorporating observations-based precipitation data directly into training can substantially improve precipitation forecasts.

</details>


### [182] [Added value of dynamical downscaling in sub-seasonal tropical cyclone forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03364)
*Taehyung Kim,Eunji Kim,Haerin Park,Dong-Hyun Cha,Johan Lee*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究评估了KMA的GloSea6模型在西太平洋地区对热带气旋（TC）的次季节预报能力，发现其在频率、路径、生命周期和强度上均存在低估，尤其在8月最显著。通过针对2016年8月进行动力降尺度模拟，显著改善了TC的预报，不仅直接提升了TC本身的模拟效果，还通过改进印度季风和环状遥相关（CGT）等大尺度环境场，间接优化了西北太平洋副热带高压（WNPSH）的变率预测，从而提高了中纬度和东亚地区TC活动的预报准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GloSea6模型在次季节尺度上对西太平洋热带气旋的预报存在系统性偏差，特别是在中纬度地区的TC活动预测不足，影响防灾预警能力，因此亟需探索改进方案。

Method: 利用1993–2016年GloSea6的次季节预报数据评估TC特征误差，并针对2016年8月这一预报技能最低的时段，采用动力降尺度方法进行高分辨率模拟，分析其对TC频率、强度、结构、生命周期及关键环境场（如WNPSH、印度季风、CGT）的影响。

Result: 动力降尺度显著提升了TC的模拟效果，包括频率、强度、结构和生命周期；同时更准确地再现了印度季风和CGT，进而改善了WNPSH变率的预测，使中纬度和东亚地区的TC活动预报更为准确，尽管存在轻微高估现象。

Conclusion: 动力降尺度不仅能直接改善次季节尺度上热带气旋的预报，还能通过优化影响TC活动的关键大尺度环流系统，间接提升预报性能，为改进气候模型中的TC预测提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Improving the sub-seasonal forecast of tropical cyclones (TCs) remains a significant challenge for climate models. This study evaluated the characteristics of tropical cyclones (TCs) in sub-seasonal forecasts using the Global Seasonal Forecast System 6 (GloSea6), an operational seasonal-to-sub-seasonal forecasting model operated by the Korea Meteorological Administration (KMA) during June-September (JJAS) from 1993 to 2016 over the western North Pacific (WNP). GloSea6 was found to underestimate TC frequency, tracks, particularly in mid-latitudes, lifetime, and intensity across all months, with the most significant errors occurring in August. To address these deficiencies, we examined whether applying dynamical downscaling to GloSea6 during August 2016, a period characterized by the lowest TC forecast skill in GloSea6, could improve sub-seasonal TC forecasts. The application of dynamical downscaling demonstrated added value by directly improving the simulation of TCs in terms of frequency, structure, intensity, and lifetime, although slight overestimations were observed. Furthermore, improved reproductions of the Indian monsoon and circumglobal teleconnection (CGT), both of which strongly influence the western North Pacific subtropical high (WNPSH), contributed to more accurate forecasts of WNPSH variability and, consequently, better predictions of TC activity in the mid-latitudes and East Asia. Therefore, dynamical downscaling can significantly advance sub-seasonal TC forecasts by not only directly improving TC characteristics but also indirectly enhancing the environmental fields (e.g., WNPSH, Indian monsoon, and CGT) that are critical to TC activity.

</details>


### [183] [Radiative and Dynamical Controls on the Land-Ocean Warming Contrast in Climate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03658)
*Paolo Giani,Arlene M. Fiore,Raffaele Ferrari,Paul A. O'Gorman,Vincent T. Cooper,Noelle E. Selin*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大气湿静能（MSE）输送的视角，整合了关于陆地-海洋变暖差异的能量与动力约束解释，提出一个可解释的模拟框架，揭示气候模型中该差异的控制机制及其与气候敏感性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对陆地增温快于海洋的现象缺乏统一解释，作者旨在结合能量和动力视角，深入理解该现象在不同气候模型中的表现及其与气候敏感性的联系。

Method: 利用大气湿静能（MSE）输送框架，分析CMIP6中22个气候模型的陆地-海洋变暖响应，并构建可解释的模拟器来再现这一差异。

Result: 发现陆地-海洋变暖差异由模型依赖的辐射基态与稳健的动力恢复机制共同决定；识别出两种模型体制：低气候敏感性模型中海洋稳定辐射反馈促进陆地增温，高敏感性模型中尽管辐射反馈倾向海洋增温，但强MSE输送反馈（约0.2 PW/K）逆转此趋势。

Conclusion: 陆地-海洋变暖差异的模型间差异与海陆辐射反馈比密切相关，表明该差异与气候敏感性存在系统性关联。

Abstract: Surface air over land warms substantially more than over the ocean under greenhouse forcing, a phenomenon known as the land-ocean warming contrast. Current explanations for this contrast are commonly expressed either in terms of energetic constraints, from top-of-atmosphere and surface energy balance, or dynamical constraints, from large-scale atmospheric dynamics. We show that these perspectives are complementary when viewed through the lens of atmospheric moist static energy (MSE) transport, and that connecting them yields new insight into the controls of the warming contrast and the spread in climate models. We use this framework to construct an interpretable emulator that reproduces the land-ocean warming response across 22 models from the latest Coupled Model Intercomparison Project (CMIP6). We find that the strength of the land-ocean warming contrast emerges from the interplay between a model-dependent radiative baseline and a robust dynamical restoring mechanism that favors greater warming over land. This interplay leads to two broad model regimes that align with climate sensitivity. In low-climate-sensitivity models, more stabilizing radiative feedbacks over the ocean directly favor greater land warming. In high-climate-sensitivity models, radiative feedbacks alone would instead favor greater ocean warming, but a strong MSE-transport feedback (approximately 0.2 PW/K) more than compensates for this tendency. The intermodel spread in the land-ocean warming contrast is closely related to the ratio of radiative feedbacks over land and ocean, highlighting a broader connection between climate sensitivity and the land-ocean warming contrast.

</details>


### [184] [Cold Extremes during Dansgaard-Oeschger Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03664)
*Ignacio del Amo,Peter Ditlevsen*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用CCSM4模拟末次盛冰期气候突变条件下极端低温的统计特征，采用非平稳广义极值（GEV）分布分析AMOC强度与极端气温的关系，并结合线性与非线性模型揭示不同气候态下极端事件的变化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解气候突变背景下极端冷事件的统计行为及其与大西洋经向翻转环流（AMOC）强度的关系，有助于揭示 abrupt 气候变化中的极端风险。

Method: 使用CCSM4对末次盛冰期的模拟数据，拟合非平稳线性GEV分布，并与非线性模型对比，分析GEV参数随AMOC强度变化的关系，结合海冰扩展、洋流变化等物理过程解释极端温度变化。

Result: 多个区域的GEV参数与AMOC强度在stadial和interstadial状态下呈近似线性关系，在状态转换时出现非线性跳跃；海冰进退和洋流强度变化显著影响极端温度的幅度与变率，并形成可与代用资料比较的遥相关。

Conclusion: 将GEV参数映射到AMOC强度可为不同气候模型和状态间的极端事件比较提供统一框架，但需考虑系统动力特性和地理位置的影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the statistics of extreme cold air surface temperatures in a climate that experiences abrupt changes. We employ a CCSM4 simulation of Last Glacial Maximum conditions that exhibits rapid switching between stadial and interstadial states as data. Non-stationary linear Generalized Extreme Value (GEV) distributions are fitted to find the regions that show the most prominent changes and associate them with physical processes. The results are then compared with a non-linear model, which gives a more detailed picture of how the parameters change as a function of the AMOC strength. While different regions of the world show different extremal behaviours, many regions show an approximately linear relationship between the parameters of the GEV distributions and the strength of the AMOC within the stadial and interstadial states, with some non-linear oscillation or jump where the transition between them takes place.
  Physical processes such as the expansion and retreat of the sea ice and the relative changes in the strength of the currents are shown to impact the magnitude and variability of the extremes, with significant changes observed in the three parameters of the GEV. They also create teleconnections that are compared whenever possible with various proxies for the temperature of the air and the water.
  We show how mapping the parameters of the GEV distributions into the AMOC strength gives a way to compare results between different climate models and different climate states. Comparisons, however, need to pay heed to the dynamical characteristics of the state and the location to be meaningful.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [The Surviving Fragment of the Zij-i Malikshahi: A Preliminary Edition, Translation, and Study of the 100-Star Catalogue in BnF Arabe 5968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02926)
*Rizoi Bakhromzod*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与塞尔柱苏丹马利克沙一世天文计划相关的100颗星表，该星表保存于《占星家规程》手稿中。通过对阿拉伯文本的转录和翻译，并与罗森菲尔德的俄译本对照，纠正了部分数据，并分析了其与托勒密数据的关系，表明这些数据是对托勒密体系系统性更新的结果，而非完全独立观测所得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确与扎拉吉历和马利克沙天文表相关的100颗星表的历史背景、来源及其在伊斯兰天文学中的地位。

Method: 对法国国家图书馆藏阿拉伯手稿BnF Arabe 5968中第224r-225r页的星表进行完整转录和直接英译，并以罗森菲尔德1961年的俄译本作为校勘依据；同时分析星表中恒星坐标与托勒密数据的差异与对应关系。

Result: 确认了96颗恒星的经度比托勒密值增加约14度26分，87颗纬度与托勒密一致；发现第8号星经度存在实质性修正；澄清了行星气质缩写形式；表明该星表是基于托勒密数据系统修订而成，而非全部独立观测结果。

Conclusion: 该100颗星表体现了中世纪伊斯兰天文学中观测、计算、传承与改编相结合的特点，是继承与改进古典知识传统的例证。

Abstract: The astronomical programme sponsored by the Saljuq Sultan Malikshah I in the late eleventh century occupies an important place in the history of Islamic astronomy. Umar Khayyam was a principal participant in this programme, which produced the Jalali or Maliki calendar and appears also to have resulted in a broader set of astronomical tables known as the Zij-i Malikshahi. A 100-star catalogue traditionally associated with these tables survives in the anonymous Arabic compendium Dustur al-munajjimin, preserved in the unique manuscript BnF Arabe 5968. This study distinguishes the catalogue from the Jalali calendar and from the lost or unidentified complete Zij-i Malikshahi, reconstructs its historical and manuscript context, and examines the provenance of BnF Arabe 5968. It presents a complete transcription of the catalogue from fols. 224r-225r and a new English translation made directly from the Arabic text, with B. A. Rosenfeld's 1961 Russian translation used as a control. The collation identifies several divergences, including a substantive correction to the longitude of entry 8, and clarifies the planetary temperament abbreviations. Rosenfeld and A. P. Yushkevich observed that 96 of the 100 stellar longitudes correspond to Ptolemaic values increased by about 14 degrees 26 minutes, while 87 latitudes agree with Ptolemy. These correspondences suggest systematic updating of inherited Ptolemaic data rather than 100 wholly independent observations, illustrating the combined roles of observation, computation, transmission, and adaptation in medieval Islamic astronomy.

</details>


### [186] [The Whittaker-Maxwell Scaffolding. Scaffolding or template: what remains of a model when it is removed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02949)
*Mar'ia Florentina Mendoza Ur'an*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判了Whittaker关于麦克斯韦电磁方程建立后分子涡旋模型即被抛弃的“脚手架”隐喻，指出该模型的部分实质内容仍以其他形式保留在方程中，并提出应以“模板”取代“脚手架”来更准确描述其作用，结合科学史观点讨论了模型的构成性角色，并提出区分两类情况的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清麦克斯韦电磁理论发展过程中模型与方程之间的真实关系，纠正长期沿用的误导性‘脚手架’隐喻。

Method: 通过文本分析和历史考察，检验1865年实际被抛弃的内容，追踪原模型中保留于方程中的成分，并引入‘模板’这一新隐喻进行对比说明。

Result: 揭示‘脚手架’隐喻并不成立，许多模型材料仍以其他名称存在于方程中；提出‘模板’作为更合适的替代隐喻，并给出判断模型是否具有构成性作用的标准。

Conclusion: 麦克斯韦的分子涡旋模型并非如‘脚手架’隐喻所言被完全抛弃，而是以转化形式持续发挥作用，应承认其在理论构建中的构成性地位。

Abstract: This paper discusses the metaphor, originating with Whittaker, according to which Maxwell's model of molecular vortices was the scaffolding by means of which the equations of electromagnetism were erected, removed afterwards without consequence for the building. It is argued here that the metaphor describes badly what happened, and it is shown that whoever upholds it today transcribes, within that same text, the quotations that belie it. The paper examines what was actually removed in 1865, which materials of the model still operate inside the equations under other names, and proposes in its place the figure of the template, taken from sheet-metal layout work. The discussion is situated with respect to the work of Daniel M. Siegel and Cameron Lazaroff-Puck, who maintain from the history of science the constitutive character of the model. It closes with a criterion for telling one case from the other.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [RACE-AIMC: Selective Inference for Heterogeneous Analog In-Memory Accelerators at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03149)
*Osama Yousuf,Martin Lueker-Boden*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了RACE-AIMC框架，通过统计方法在模拟存内计算（AIMC）中实现风险感知的认证集成，在保证精度的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIMC硬件存在制造缺陷和噪声导致计算失真，不同芯片表现各异，传统多芯片冗余或单芯片盲信方案均不理想，需一种兼顾能效与可靠性的决策机制。

Method: RACE-AIMC离线评估一组物理加速器，选择在给定能效预算下最优的一个，并计算其输出错误率的数学精确上界；在线阶段仅启用该加速器，通过轻量级检查决定是否采纳其结果或切换至备用方案。

Result: 在含噪声权重映射和多次独立测试的仿真中，所有认证误差上界均低于10%目标（平均7.83%±0.89%），70.88%±0.98%的输入直接由主加速器处理，系统精度与数字基线相当，能耗较始终运行全部加速器降低69.02%。

Conclusion: RACE-AIMC通过严谨的统计认证机制解决了AIMC芯片间差异带来的可靠性与能效矛盾，在保持高精度的同时大幅节省能量，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Analog in-memory computing (AIMC) speeds up neural-network inference by doing the arithmetic directly inside a memory array, instead of shuttling weights back and forth between memory and a processor. This saves energy, but the physical devices that store the weights are imperfect: programming errors, electrical noise, limited-resolution converters, and outright broken cells all distort the computation, and every physical chip is distorted in its own way. A designer with several such chips available faces an uncomfortable choice: run all of them and combine the answers (safe, but wasteful of energy), or trust a single chip blindly (cheap, but with no guarantee on how often it is wrong). This paper introduces RACE-AIMC (Risk-Aware Certified Ensemble for AIMC), a framework that resolves this choice with statistics rather than guesswork. Offline, RACE-AIMC studies a pool of physical accelerators, picks the single best one for a given energy budget, and computes a mathematically exact upper bound on how often that accelerator will be wrong when it chooses to answer. Online, only that one accelerator is switched on; a lightweight check decides whether to accept its answer or defer to a fallback. In our simulations using a noisy weight mapping and multiple independent test runs, every certified bound stayed under a 10% error target (mean bound 7.83% +- 0.89%, with 70.88% +- 0.98% of inputs answered directly). The resulting system matches the accuracy of a clean digital baseline while cutting modeled energy use by 69.02% relative to always running every accelerator in the pool.

</details>


### [188] [Embedding Single-Phase Grid-Forming Inverters in Three-Phase Unbalanced Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03154)
*Kamini Shahare,Fei Feng,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于在三相不平衡网络中建模单相构网型逆变器（GFM）的增强型牛顿潮流框架——GFM-3PF，具备良好的准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在三相不平衡电力系统中准确建模单相构网型逆变器（GFM）并提升大规模潮流计算的稳定性与效率。

Method: 提出一种两阶段求解策略：基于正序初始化和三相相域提升；结合考虑相间耦合与相依赖频率敏感负荷的相域建模方法；将单相GFM通过公共下垂控制频率变量嵌入三相牛顿潮流方程中。

Result: 在3节点、33节点和118节点测试系统上验证了GFM-3PF的有效性，结果表明该方法具有高精度、强鲁棒性和良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GFM-3PF为单相构网型逆变器在三相不平衡网络中的建模提供了有效且可扩展的潮流计算解决方案，适用于实际大规模系统的分析与应用。

Abstract: This letter introduces Grid-Forming Three-Phase Power Flow (GFM-3PF), an augmented Newton power flow framework for modeling single-phase grid-forming inverters (GFMs) in three-phase unbalanced networks. The contributions of this work are threefold: 1) developing a two-stage solution strategy based on positive-sequence initialization followed by three-phase phase-domain lifting for robust large-scale computation; 2) incorporating phase-domain unbalanced network modeling with controlled mutual coupling together with phase-dependent, frequency-sensitive load representation; and 3) embedding a single-phase GFM bus model directly into the three-phase unbalanced Newton power flow equations through a common droop-governed frequency state. GFM-3PF is validated on 3-bus, 33-bus, and 118-bus test systems, demonstrating its accuracy, robustness, and scalability.

</details>


### [189] [QSVT-Based Three-Phase Unbalanced Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03165)
*Kamini Shahare,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子奇异值变换（QSVT）的三相不平衡潮流求解器QSVT-3PF，适用于含单相电网形成型逆变器的系统，验证了其在标准测试系统中与经典牛顿法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含单相电网形成型逆变器的三相不平衡配电系统，传统潮流计算方法在病态和重载条件下可能面临收敛性差的问题，亟需更鲁棒的求解方法。

Method: 将牛顿法修正方程重构为适合QSVT的逆问题形式，采用归一化分块编码的相域雅可比矩阵，并引入正则化奇异值滤波以提升在病态和重载条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在IEEE 5节点系统和IEEE 123节点测试馈线上的仿真结果表明，QSVT-3PF在残差收敛、电压分布和最终运行点上均与经典牛顿法一致。

Conclusion: QSVT-3PF能够有效求解大规模、不平衡配电系统的潮流问题，展示了量子奇异值变换在电力系统分析中的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: This letter introduces QSVT-3PF, a quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) based solver for three-phase unbalanced power flow with embedded single-phase grid-forming inverter (GFM) operation. The contributions are threefold: 1) reformulating the Newton correction as a QSVT-compatible inverse problem using a normalized block-encoded phase-domain Jacobian; 2) introducing a regularized singular-value filter to improve robustness under ill-conditioned and stressed operating conditions; and 3) validating the proposed solver on an IEEE 5-bus system and the IEEE 123-node test feeder with single-phase GFM integration. Test results show that QSVT-3PF matches the classical Newton benchmark in residual convergence, voltage profile, and final operating point, demonstrating the feasibility of QSVT-3 for large, unbalanced distribution systems.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Hosting Capacity Assessment of Data Centers with Voltage Ride-Through Capability in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03030)
*Pengyu Ren,Wei Sun,Fei Teng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑电压穿越能力的数据中心与电网协同规划框架，通过动态仿真分析数据中心内部设备对电网故障的响应，发现电压穿越能力会显著影响数据中心的接入容量及其空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心作为电力电子主导的大功率负载，在电压扰动期间可能突然断开或切换至备用电源，导致系统需求大幅下降，从而引发电网稳定性问题。因此，需要研究其在暂态过程中的负荷保持能力，以确保安全并网。

Method: 构建了一个电压穿越（VRT）感知的数据中心-电网联合规划框架，结合输电层面的故障仿真与数据中心内部的动态响应模型。使用Python进行动态仿真生成并网点电压波形，并以此驱动包含IT负载、冷却系统、直流链路、UPS响应及变流器功率限制的内部模型。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上的研究表明：仅依赖稳态规划会高估可接入的数据中心容量；提升UPS变流器裕度可恢复承载容量；在相同电网故障下，构网模式比跟网模式提供更大的电压穿越裕度；VRT约束显著改变了数据中心的总承载容量及其在不同并网点的空间分配。

Conclusion: 数据中心的内部电压穿越能力是影响其并网可行性的关键因素，应将其纳入协同规划流程中，以更准确评估接入容量，避免因暂态负荷突降引发系统稳定性问题。

Abstract: Large data centers are emerging as concentrated, power-electronic grid loads whose abrupt disconnection or transfer to on-site backup supply during voltage disturbances can remove large demand from the power system, and may create a system-level stability problem. Their interconnection feasibility therefore depends not only on steady-state thermal and voltage limits, but also on whether internal power-conditioning systems can maintain IT service while limiting customer-initiated load reduction. This paper presents a voltage ride-through (VRT)-aware data center and grid co-planning framework that couples transmission-level fault simulation with an internal data center ride-through model. Python-based dynamic simulations generate point-of-interconnection (POI) voltage trajectories under selected network faults, and the resulting waveforms drive an internal model incorporating IT and cooling-load dynamics, DC-link, Uninterruptible Power Supply (UPS) response, and converter apparent power limits. The IEEE 118-bus case study shows that internal VRT capability can become a binding interconnection constraint: steady-state planning alone can overestimate feasible data center capacity, whereas increased UPS converter headroom progressively restores hosting capacity. Under the reduced-order response models studied, the grid-forming mode provides greater ride-through margin than the current-limited grid-following mode under the same network fault conditions. The results further show that VRT constraints can materially change both the total hosting capacity of data centers and its spatial allocation across candidate interconnection buses.

</details>


### [191] [Dynamic Operational Reserve Margin Assessment from Risk-Constrained Unit Commitment States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03066)
*Pratishtha Shukla,Shaked Regev,Evan J. R. Brody,Charles Foltz,Teja Kuruganti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出动态备用裕度（DRM）和备用风险包络（RRE）作为评估电力系统备用充足性的新指标，基于风险约束机组组合状态，量化5分钟内可调用的备用容量及其持续性，揭示传统指标无法捕捉的备用可及性和压力持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统备用裕度指标难以准确反映系统在不同运行条件下备用资源的实际可用性和压力持续性，需提出更具解释性和动态适应性的评估方法。

Method: 基于风险约束机组组合（RCUC）模型，定义时间变化的动态备用裕度（DRM）和以兆瓦为单位的备用风险包络（RRE），并引入低裕度持续时间指标来衡量备用压力的持续性，通过IEEE 14节点系统示例和大规模案例研究验证方法有效性。

Result: 研究表明备用需求和可调用备用能力随运行条件显著变化，机组组合决策能够自适应调整以维持备用充足性；DRM和RRE能更清晰地表征备用可及性与压力持续性，优于传统指标。

Conclusion: 所提出的DRM和RRE指标为电力系统运行中的备用充足性提供了更直观、动态且可解释的量化工具，有助于提升对复杂运行条件下系统可靠性的评估与管理能力。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Reserve Margin (DRM) as a time-varying operational adequacy metric derived from risk-constrained unit commitment (RCUC) states. DRM quantifies reserve adequacy using the additional generation capacity that committed generators can provide within a 5-minute response window relative to uncertainty and contingency reserve requirements. We introduce a complementary Reserve Risk Envelope (RRE) metric to quantify operational reserve headroom in directly interpretable MW terms. A low-margin duration metric is further developed to quantify the persistence of reserve stress over an operating horizon. We use an IEEE 14-bus example to illustrate these concepts, followed by large-scale RCUC case studies under multiple operating scenarios. Results demonstrate that reserve requirements and ramp-accessible reserve capability can vary substantially across operating conditions, and that commitment decisions adapt to maintain reserve adequacy under changing system conditions. The proposed DRM and RRE metrics provide an interpretable operational characterization of reserve adequacy, reveal reserve accessibility and stress persistence that are not captured by conventional reserve margin metrics.

</details>


### [192] [Finite-Sample Limits of Entropy-Based Structure Identification in Discretized Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03074)
*Pratishtha Shukla,James Nutaro*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在离散化测量下熵基方法在因果识别中的局限性，提出了分辨率-随机性比的概念，并分析了其对变量选择、预测风险和样本需求的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机系统中，当系统随机性超过离散化分辨率时，基于熵的方法会失去区分因果输入的能力，影响结构识别的可靠性。

Method: 通过引入分辨率-随机性比，分析熵基与均方误差基变量选择在Fuzzy Inductive Reasoning框架下的表现，并推导预测风险与样本复杂度的关系。

Result: 1) 熵基选择在阈值以下具有一致性，超过则失效；2) 使用熵选变量会导致可量化的额外预测风险；3) 可靠因果识别需要随输入组合数增长且反比于熵信号强度的样本量。

Conclusion: 熵基因果识别受限于离散化分辨率与系统随机性的相对关系，需谨慎结合预测目标与解释目标进行变量选择。

Abstract: Discretization fundamentally limits structure identification in stochastic systems. When system stochasticity exceeds the discretization resolution, entropy-based methods lose their ability to distinguish which input drives the output. We study this in Fuzzy Inductive Reasoning (FIR), a nonparametric framework for learning dynamical systems from discretized measurements, where the choice of input variables determines both predictive accuracy and the interpretability of the learned input--output relationships. Entropy-based selection targets explainability, i.e., identifying which variables causally drive the output, while mean-squared-error-based selection targets prediction. We introduce a resolution-stochasticity ratio that governs when entropy-based selection is reliable. Three results follow. First, entropy-based selection is consistent below this threshold but loses discriminative power above it, regardless of sample size. Second, using the entropy-selected variables for prediction instead of the MSE-selected ones incurs a closed-form excess prediction risk that grows with input complexity and shrinks with sample size. Third, reliable identification of the causally relevant inputs requires data that scales with the number of input combinations and inversely with the strength of the entropy signal. The theory is validated on a two-state Markov model and demonstrated on a distribution grid reliability dataset analyzing the impact of infrastructure investment, where the goal is to explain which investments drive reliability improvements rather than merely predict outcomes.

</details>


### [193] [On a Gradation for Asymptotic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03120)
*Yiğit Narter,Inigo Incer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渐近稳定性的分级方法，其中0级对应指数稳定性，m>0级对应代数衰减t^{-1/m}，并通过李雅普诺夫测试和示例验证了稳定性等级的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渐近稳定性无法量化收敛速率，因此需要一种能够刻画不同衰减速率的稳定性分级框架。

Method: 引入渐近稳定性的分级概念，定义不同级别的稳定性，并给出相应的直接和逆向李雅普诺夫判据，结合Hopf、Bautin等例子进行分析。

Result: 建立了可判定稳定性等级的李雅普诺夫条件，能够识别主导的稳定机制，并在多个动态系统示例中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定性分级框架能更精细地描述系统的收敛行为，为分析非指数收敛系统提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Classical asymptotic stability guarantees convergence but does not quantify the rate at which convergence occurs. This paper introduces a gradation of asymptotic stability where degree zero corresponds to exponential stability and degree $m>0$ corresponds to algebraic decay of order $t^{-1/m}$. We provide direct and converse Lyapunov tests for admissible degrees and conditions for certifying the exact stability degree. Hopf, Bautin, fractional-degree, and time-varying examples demonstrate how the degree identifies the leading stabilizing mechanism.

</details>


### [194] [A Reconfigurable Hybrid Convolutional-Fully Connected Neuromorphic Core for Biomedical Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03174)
*Sarah Johari,Suman Kumar,Abhishek Mishra,Anush Lingamoorthy,Nagarajan Kandasamy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于FPGA的可编程脉冲卷积神经网络（SCNN）架构，用于实时低氧分类的生物医学边缘应用，实现了高精度和低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种适用于边缘设备的低功耗、实时脉冲神经网络硬件架构，以应对生物医学信号处理中的资源限制和实时性需求。

Method: 设计了一个混合脉冲卷积-全连接（CNN-FC）拓扑结构，在FPGA上实现量化、分层的类脑硬件核心，并通过PyTorch-based软硬件协同设计流程部署训练参数。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上分别达到98%和86%的硬件准确率；在肩戴式PPG信号的五折交叉验证中平均硬件准确率为88.26%，动态功耗为1.455 W。

Conclusion: 所提出的FPGA-based SCNN架构能够在边缘实现高效、低功耗的生物医学分类，验证了其在实际应用场景中的可行性。

Abstract: This work presents a programmable FPGA-based architecture for spiking convolutional neural network (SCNN) inference, with real-time hypoxia classification serving as a biomedical edge application. The architecture implements a hybrid spiking convolutional-fully connected (CNN-FC) topology on a programmable, quantized, layer-based neuromorphic hardware core. Early layers perform spiking convolution using receptive-field connectivity with support for multi-channel kernels and stride, while deeper layers use fully connected spiking layers for classification. A PyTorch-based hardware-software co-design flow enables deployment of trained parameters with quantization and configurability support. The design is first validated on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, achieving hardware accuracies of up to 98% and 86%, respectively, at 16-bit precision. It is then applied to hypoxia classification using red and infrared photoplethysmography (PPG) signals acquired from a shoulder-mounted sensor, with skin tone included as an additional input channel. The resulting classifier achieves an average hardware accuracy of 88.26% across five folds at 16-bit precision while consuming 1.455 W of dynamic power, demonstrating the feasibility of low-power neuromorphic biomedical classification at the edge.

</details>


### [195] [Locating Power System Oscillation Sources by Extracting Interharmonics from Synchrophasor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03228)
*Sophia Xu,Wilsun Xu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了是否可以从相量数据中直接提取间谐波用于振荡源定位，并验证了在特定条件下相量测量单元（PMU）数据的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于波形数据较难获取，而相量数据更易获得，因此研究如何利用相量数据提取间谐波以实现振荡源定位。

Method: 通过敏感性分析研究提取条件，并开发基于云的软件工具，利用PMU同步相量数据进行间谐波提取和振荡源定位。

Result: 结果表明，在某些振荡现象中，相量数据能够支持间谐波的提取和振荡源定位。

Conclusion: 相量数据在满足一定条件时可用于间谐波提取和振荡源定位，拓展了PMU数据的应用范围。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that oscillating phasors arise from beating waves caused by interharmonics. This finding has led to an interharmonic-based oscillation source-location method. But waveform data needed for the method are less available than PMU synchrophasor data. This paper investigates whether interharmonics can be extracted directly from phasor data for similar applications. The results show that, for certain oscillation phenomena, phasor data can support interharmonic extraction and oscillation source location. Sensitivity studies have identified the requirements, and a cloud-based software tool is developed for PMU-based source location.

</details>


### [196] [Trajectory-Based Co-Optimization of Arrival Scheduling and Descent Path Design in the Terminal Maneuvering Area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03234)
*Yutian Pang,John-Paul Clarke*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四维终端进近调度方法，将飞机的横向路径延伸、下滑道捕获距离和襟翼展开速度统一决策，实现燃油效率与时间调度的协同优化，并通过案例研究验证了其在不同条件下的节油效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进近调度与下降程序设计分离，导致时间高效但可能燃油消耗大，且难以适应未来自动化或减员运行需求，因此需要一种能够协同优化调度与下降轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出一个四维终端进近调度器，在决策中同时考虑每架飞机的横向路径扩展、下滑道捕获距离和襟翼展开触发速度；通过离线评估每个候选的怠速推力下降轨迹，结合风况感知的反向规划与六自由度前向仿真，获取下降时间、油耗、最小航迹长度和稳定进近可行性，在滚动物料周期中基于尾流间隔和实测入流风条件为每架飞机确定一条已验证的下降路径和扩展方案。

Result: 在亚特兰大终端空域的两个案例研究表明：协同优化的连续下降可比饱和状态下节省约15%的机队燃油（自由下降环境），延迟减速可节省约23%；但在已公布的6条8L跑道进场航路中，由于航图规定的高度层限制移除了31%的设计空间，节油效果分别降至9–10%和20–21%；风的影响使单机油耗变化34–81%，但在整个场景中仅解释最多4%的机队油耗方差。

Conclusion: 将进近调度与下降程序联合优化能显著提升燃油效率，尤其在无高度限制的环境中效果更佳；尽管航图高度限制削弱了部分收益，但该方法仍具备明显节能潜力，且风对整体机队油耗的影响较小，适合用于未来自动化空中交通管理。

Abstract: Terminal arrival scheduling and descent procedure design are studied in two largely separate literatures. Scheduling models reduce each aircraft to a travel time and deliver target landing times, and fuel-efficient descent procedures are designed one aircraft at a time with the schedule taken as given, although both decide where an arriving aircraft absorbs delay before final approach. Existing formulations therefore cannot trade a slower, earlier-configuring descent against level track miles, a schedule that is efficient in time can be expensive in fuel, and autonomous or reduced-crew operations will need a single trajectory plan that ground automation and the flight management system both accept. To close this gap, we propose a four-dimensional terminal arrival scheduler that selects each aircraft's lateral path extension, glideslope-capture distance, and flap-deployment trigger speeds in one decision. We evaluate every candidate idle-thrust descent offline with a wind-aware backward plan and a six-degree-of-freedom forward simulation that returns descent time, fuel burn, minimum track length, and stabilized-approach feasibility, and a rolling-horizon scheduler commits one verified descent and one extension per aircraft under wake-separation constraints and observed entry winds. Two Atlanta terminal airspace case studies quantify the benefit. We show that co-optimized continuous descents save about 15\% of fleet fuel below saturation in a free-descent environment and that delayed deceleration saves about 23\%, while on the six published Runway 8L arrival flows the charted altitude floors remove 31\% of the design lattice and reduce the savings to 9--10\% and 20--21\%, respectively. We also find that wind moves single-aircraft descent fuel by 34--81\% yet explains at most 4\% of fleet fuel variance, because aircraft-specific wind effects average out across a scenario.

</details>


### [197] [Preference-Oriented Aggregation of Heterogeneous Distributed Energy Resources for Reserve Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03252)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Xizhen Xue,Shengshi Wang,Jiakun Fang,Wei Yao,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向偏好的分布式能源资源聚合方法，用于解决维度和类型异质性带来的灵活性损失问题，通过高维空间投影和考虑备用调度偏好的协调框架，有效提升了聚合精度与电网调度效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式能源资源（DERs）聚合方法在处理异构资源时过于保守，难以有效应对维度和类型异质性，导致灵活性损失严重，影响电网备用调度效率。

Method: 针对维度异质性，将Minkowski和重构为多面体投影问题，利用矩阵变换技术在高维空间统一表示并投影回可行域；针对类型异质性，设计分布式聚合-调度协调框架，引入备用调度偏好以捕捉关键的主动灵活性。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能有效应对两类异质性，显著减少灵活性损失，提升聚合精度，尤其适用于高备用需求的电力系统。

Conclusion: 所提偏好导向的聚合方法克服了传统方法在异构DERs聚合中的保守性，兼顾维度统一与类型差异，为高效 reserve dispatch 提供了更具灵活性和实用性的聚合方案。

Abstract: Aggregating distributed energy resources (DERs) aims to encode their collective flexibility into a single set for efficient grid dispatch. However, existing aggregation methods are overly conservative for heterogeneous DERs due to two main challenges: 1) dimensional heterogeneity, which complicates the combination of flexibilities across different time dimensions, and 2) type heterogeneity, where diverse and irregular DER profiles hinder accurate approximations, resulting in significant flexibility loss. To resolve these challenges, this paper propose a novel preference-oriented aggregation method for reserve dispatch. For dimensional heterogeneity, we extend existing techniques by reformulating the Minkowski sum as a polytope projection problem using a matrix transformation technique. By unifying DERs in a higher-dimensional space and projecting them back into the aggregate feasible region, the proposed technique effectively aggregates dimensionally heterogeneous DERs. For type heterogeneity, we further develop a distributed aggregation-dispatch coordination framework that incorporates reserve dispatch preferences into aggregation. This framework effectively captures the critical, active aggregate flexibility prioritized in optimal reserve dispatch, thereby significantly reducing the flexibility loss when aggregating type-heterogeneous DERs. Numerical tests validate the effectiveness of our method in addressing both heterogeneities and highlight its promising potential for power systems with high reserve requirements.

</details>


### [198] [Coupling-Aware Aggregation of Multi-Zone HVAC Loads under Uncertainty: A Two-level Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03253)
*Jingguan Liu,Han Jiang,Xiaomeng Ai,Shengshi Wang,Xizhen Xue,Shichang Cui,Jinming Hou,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种耦合感知的两层聚合框架，用于量化多区域HVAC负荷的聚合灵活性，通过降维和矩阵变换技术实现了高效、准确的聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多区域耦合导致建筑HVAC负荷聚合时存在复杂依赖性和不确定性传播，难以准确量化其灵活性。

Method: 在建筑层面采用高斯消元和坐标变换将高维热动态转化为低维解析表达式；在聚合商层面发展新的矩阵变换技术，构建不同维度多面体间的内近似，并转化为可分离的线性规划问题求解最优参数。

Result: 该框架能够清晰描述不确定性传播，解耦建筑内外部耦合关系，提供全维灵活性集合，并生成电源子空间中高维多区域HVAC灵活性的闭式图像。

Conclusion: 案例研究验证了该框架在准确性、可靠性和可扩展性方面的有效性，适用于大规模需求侧灵活性聚合分析。

Abstract: Aggregating building heating, ventilation, and air-conditioning (HVAC) loads unlocks substantial demand-side flexibility for power systems. Yet multi-zone coupling creates intricate interdependencies and uncertainty propagation, complicating the quantification of aggregate flexibility. To address this issue, this paper proposes a coupling-aware two-level aggregation framework. At the building level, tailored Gaussian elimination and coordinate transformation techniques are employed to recast the high-dimensional thermal dynamics as an equivalent lower-dimensional analytical expression. This expression streamlines the subsequent aggregator-level stage by (i) clarifying the propagation of zone-level uncertainties to the building-level interface, (ii) decoupling intra-building multi-zone coupling from inter-building aggregation, and (iii) providing full-dimensional building-level flexibility sets that enable tractable reformulation. At the aggregator level, existing geometric aggregation approaches are generalized by a newly developed matrix-transformation technique. This technique effectively constructs inner approximations between polytopes of different dimensions, producing closed-form images of high-dimensional multi-zone HVAC flexibility in power subspace. The resulting inner approximation is then recast as a customized separatable linear program that efficiently determines the optimal aggregate parameters. Case studies validate the effectiveness of our framework, highlighting its accuracy, reliability, and scalability.

</details>


### [199] [On the Degree of Safety: Beyond Safe or Unsafe with Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03319)
*Ruoyu Lin,Fabio Pasqualetti,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与表示无关的安全度量——不变性权威需求（IAD），用于量化实现控制不变性所需的控制权威，并区分了不可行性的内在与表示原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制屏障函数（CBF）只能提供状态是否安全的二元判断，无法量化安全程度，且其内部值和梯度依赖于集合的表示方式，不能独立反映安全度。此外，CBF约束的不可行性可能源于安全集本身或表示方法的问题，需加以区分。

Method: 通过分析相同安全集下不同CBF表示的内部值和梯度的任意性，论证其不能作为安全度量；提出‘内在不可行性’与‘表示不可行性’的概念区分；引入新的安全度量——不变性权威需求（IAD），该指标不依赖于CBF的具体表示形式，能够量化维持系统安全所需的控制能力。

Result: 证明了CBF的内部值和梯度不能独立于表示来定义安全程度；明确了CBF约束不可行性的两种来源；提出了IAD这一表示无关的安全度量，并验证其可用于指导安全集或执行器的修复设计。

Conclusion: 安全性的量化应基于系统内在属性而非CBF的数学表示，所提出的IAD为评估和改进系统安全性提供了新的工具，有助于在控制器设计中实现更可靠的前向不变性保障。

Abstract: A valid control barrier function (CBF) certifies if its represented safe set can be rendered forward invariant, and the sign of its value indicates whether a state is safe or not, but it does not quantify a degree of safety beyond the binary indication. In this paper, we show that among valid CBFs representing the same safe set, interior values and gradients can be changed arbitrarily, so neither quantity determines a degree of safety that is independent of how the set is represented. We also show that whether a candidate CBF-based inequality constraint is feasible does not by itself quantify a degree of safety. In particular, infeasibility can occur either because the safe set is not controlled invariant or because the candidate CBF representation fails. This motivates our distinction between intrinsic and representational infeasibility. Finally, we introduce the invariance authority demand (IAD), a representation-independent degree of safety that quantifies the control authority required for controlled invariance and can be used to guide set or actuator repair.

</details>


### [200] [Traffic Congestion Control for ARZ Model with an Arbitrarily Large Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03345)
*Yidan Cao,Xiang Xu,Lu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在任意大输入延迟的情况下，Aw-Rascle-Zhang（ARZ）交通模型的稳定化问题，提出了一种基于反步法的边界控制器，并通过仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARZ交通模型在考虑输入延迟时面临显著的分析挑战，特别是当线性化模型为具有近端反射的2×2双曲型偏微分方程系统时，需要有效的控制策略来实现稳定化。

Method: 采用反步法设计边界控制器，将输入延迟建模为输运PDE，从而将整个系统重构为3×3双曲型PDE系统；设计反步变换以映射到稳定的目标准则系统，并利用逐次逼近法构造满足两个边界约束的核函数，结合小增益定理分析输入-状态稳定性。

Result: 成功设计出能够在任意大输入延迟下稳定线性化ARZ模型的延迟补偿控制器，理论证明完整且仿真结果表明该控制器相比无补偿控制器更有效，且在真实交通数据下表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于反步法的延迟补偿控制策略能够有效解决具有大延迟的ARZ交通模型的稳定化问题，为双曲型PDE系统的延迟控制提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the stabilization problem for Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic model in the presence of an arbitrarily large input delay. The linearized ARZ model is a $2 \times 2$ hyperbolic partial differential equation (PDE) system with proximal reflection, which introduces significant analytical challenges when combined with input delays. To tackle this problem, we propose a backstepping-based boundary controller capable of stabilizing the linearized ARZ model under these conditions. The input delay is modeled as a transport PDE, which reformulates the entire system into a $3 \times 3$ hyperbolic PDE system. A backstepping transformation is designed to map the original system into a stable target system, enabling the design of a delay-compensated controller. A key technical contribution of this work is that for hyperbolic PDEs with delays, we develop a characteristic-region-wise construction for kernel functions subject to two boundary constraints and close the proof via successive approximation. Another contribution is that we utilize the small-gain theorem for input-to-state stability (ISS) of hyperbolic PDEs. Two simulations are provided to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed delay-compensated controller: one compares it with a controller without compensation, and the other employs real traffic vehicle data to validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [201] [Taylor-Informed Indirect Adaptive Predictive Control Using Jacobian-Frozen Affine Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03348)
*Tam W. Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于泰勒展开的间接自适应预测控制框架，用于非线性采样数据系统，利用雅可比冻结仿射预测器和结构化字典实现高效建模与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性采样数据系统的建模与控制难题，传统方法在远离工作点时精度下降，且计算复杂度高，因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率的自适应预测控制策略。

Method: 采用有限泰勒展开近似非线性系统动态，通过递归最小二乘法在线识别多项式系数；在每个采样时刻，基于当前工作点计算雅可比矩阵并冻结于预测区间，构建仿射预测模型；引入前向欧拉/泰勒结构引导的简化字典，剔除偶数次单项式并进一步剪枝以降低模型复杂度。

Result: 数值仿真表明，随着泰勒阶次提高，系统在远离展开点时的跟踪精度显著提升，同时控制能耗保持相当；所提方法优于低阶模型，并实现了有效的模型降阶。

Conclusion: 该框架结合泰勒结构信息与自适应辨识，有效提升了非线性系统的预测控制性能，具备良好的跟踪能力与实用性，且完整MATLAB实现已公开以支持可重复性研究。

Abstract: This paper develops a Taylor-informed indirect adaptive predictive control framework for nonlinear sampled-data systems using Jacobian-frozen affine predictors. A finite Taylor expansion approximates the sampled nonlinear dynamics, and recursive least squares (RLS) identifies its polynomial coefficients online. At each sampling instant, the Jacobian of the identified map is evaluated at the current operating point and frozen over the prediction horizon, yielding an affine predictor for model predictive control. In contrast to generic nonlinear feature dictionaries, the implemented polynomial dictionary is a forward-Euler/Taylor-structure-informed reduced dictionary. Exact joint-odd symmetry eliminates even-total-degree monomials, whereas additional forward-Euler-informed pruning constitutes a deliberate model reduction. Numerical simulations on an unstable nonlinear benchmark compare different Taylor degrees. The results show that higher-order models improve tracking accuracy as the operating point moves farther from the expansion point while maintaining comparable control effort. The complete MATLAB implementation is publicly available to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [202] [Taylor-Informed Predictive Cost Adaptive Control for Quadrotors with Online Gravity-Trim Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03351)
*Tam W. Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泰勒展开的预测成本自适应控制方法（PCAC），用于具有在线重力补偿调整的四旋翼飞行器，通过高阶动力学近似提升预测与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高四旋翼在负载突变和复杂轨迹跟踪中的动态适应能力，需消除传统固定重力补偿的局限性，实现更精确的在线参数识别与控制。

Method: 利用非线性动力学关于悬停状态的一阶、二阶和三阶泰勒展开构建稀疏采样数据字典，采用逐行递推最小二乘法进行在线辨识，并在预测时域内固定当前状态下的雅可比矩阵。同时利用垂直动力学估计质量和重力补偿输入。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在突发负载变化和激进螺旋轨迹跟踪中，高阶预测器显著提升了预测精度和轨迹跟踪性能，同时保持了标准PCAC结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的Taylor-informed PCAC方法通过在线学习实现自适应重力补偿和质量估计，有效增强了四旋翼系统在强扰动下的鲁棒性和控制精度。

Abstract: This paper develops Taylor-informed predictive cost adaptive control (PCAC) for quadrotors with online gravity-trim adaptation. First-, second-, and third-order expansions of the nonlinear dynamics about nominal hover define sparse sampled-data dictionaries for row-wise recursive least-squares identification with variable-rate forgetting. At each step, the identified predictor is linearized at the current state, and its Jacobian is fixed over the prediction horizon. The identified vertical dynamics also estimate the vehicle mass and gravity-trim input, eliminating fixed nominal gravity compensation. Simulations with an abrupt payload change and aggressive helix tracking show that the higher-order predictors improve prediction and tracking while preserving the standard PCAC formulation.

</details>


### [203] [Synchronization of directed hypergraphs with heterogeneities via dynamic coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03698)
*Aiwin Thomas Vadakkan,Pietro De Lellis,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有参数失配的非线性智能体组成的有向超图中，通过动态扩散耦合实现完全同步的问题，提出了主稳定性函数框架，并验证了积分作用对同步的补偿效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于许多现实网络中的高阶交互无法简化为成对耦合，因此需要在超图框架下研究非线性系统的完全同步问题，尤其是在存在参数失配的情况下。

Method: 采用动态扩散耦合机制，构建包含比例和积分双层超图结构的主稳定性函数（MSF）框架，分析同步流形的不变性与局部稳定性，并结合数值仿真验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了仅当参数失配在横向动力学中结构等价于恒定扰动时，才能实现完全同步；提出了适用于有向超图的MSF框架，给出了预测同步区域的谱判据，并通过Lorenz振子和非线性舆论动力学的仿真验证了积分层对比例层不稳定效应的补偿能力。

Conclusion: 动态扩散耦合可在满足特定结构条件下实现非线性超图系统的完全同步，所提出的MSF框架为高阶交互网络的同步分析与控制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Many real-world networks involve interactions among three or more agents that cannot be reduced to pairwise coupling, making hypergraphs a natural modeling framework. In this work, we study complete synchronization in directed hypergraphs of nonlinear agents with parameter mismatches under dynamic diffusive coupling. Although proportional-integral coupling schemes are known to achieve consensus in heterogeneous linear networks, their ability to enforce complete synchronization in nonlinear systems is more limited, generally yielding only bounded synchronization. We show that complete synchronization can be attained only when the effect of parameter mismatches is structurally equivalent, in the transverse dynamics, to a constant disturbance. Under this condition, we derive invariance and local stability conditions for the synchronization manifold and develop a Master Stability Function framework for directed hypergraphs with dynamic coupling. The proposed approach accounts for distinct proportional and integral hypergraph layers and provides spectral criteria for predicting synchronization regions. Numerical simulations on directed hypergraphs of Lorenz oscillators validate the theoretical predictions and show how the integral action can compensate destabilizing effects induced by the proportional layer. We further demonstrate the applicability of the framework to a pinning-control problem in nonlinear opinion dynamics, where dynamic diffusive coupling achieves complete leader-follower consensus in the presence of heterogeneity.

</details>


### [204] [Can Julia land on the Moon? On the development of a GNC simulation framework for the Argonaut lunar lander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03843)
*Francesco Capolupo,Frederik Markus*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在欧洲航天局（ESA）的Argonaut月球着陆器计划中，使用Julia语言开发的月球着陆仿真框架ATLAS。该框架用于设计和分析制导、导航与控制（GNC）算法，支持高保真动力学建模和大规模蒙特卡洛仿真，能够在单一环境中实现从早期原型设计到复杂仿真分析的无缝衔接。研究表明，相比传统的MATLAB/Simulink体系，Julia在性能、灵活性和并行计算方面具有显著优势，适用于机构主导的研究和快速设计迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升月球着陆任务中GNC算法的设计效率与仿真能力，需要一个兼具高性能和灵活性的开发环境。传统MATLAB/Simulink生态系统在计算效率和可扩展性方面存在局限，因此探索Julia语言在航天任务仿真中的适用性成为必要。

Method: 开发了一个名为ATLAS的模块化仿真工具套件，集成平移与旋转动力学、变质量特性、推进剂晃动、传感器与执行器模型以及多速率仿真环境下的飞行代表性GNC算法，并在Julia平台上实现从原型设计到大规模蒙特卡洛分析的全流程支持。

Result: Julia语言能够有效支持高保真、计算密集型的闭环仿真，具备良好的并行化能力和快速迭代特性，在性能和灵活性上优于传统MATLAB/Simulink流程，适合用于早期设计研究和大规模仿真分析。

Conclusion: Julia为航天机构提供了强大的研究与设计工具，尤其在GNC算法开发中展现出替代或补充传统工具链的潜力，有助于推动未来深空探测任务的高效开发。

Abstract: No, the Julia programming language cannot land on the Moon - but it can play a crucial role in designing and analysing the Guidance, Navigation, and Control (GNC) algorithms required for doing so. This paper presents the development of a lunar landing simulation framework implemented in Julia at the European Space Agency (ESA), within the Argonaut lunar lander programme. ATLAS (Argonaut Tools for Landing Analysis and Simulation) is a modular suite of analysis and simulation tools that cover the complete descent and landing phase of Argonaut, integrating high fidelity translational and rotational dynamics, varying mass properties, propellant sloshing, detailed sensor and actuator models, and flight-representative GNC algorithms within a multi-rate simulation environment. The framework is intended to bridge early-phase prototyping and large-scale Monte Carlo analysis within a single environment. This work evaluates the advantages and limitations of adopting Julia compared to established GNC development practices based on the MATLAB/Simulink ecosystem. The results show that Julia provides a powerful, flexible, and high-performance environment for agency-driven research, early-phase design studies, and computationally intensive closed-loop simulations enabling large-scale, parallelizable simulations and rapid design iteration cycles.

</details>


### [205] [Data-Based Clustering and Control of Similar Biological Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03921)
*Peilin Zhang,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Idris Kempf*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于相似性的分层控制框架，用于减少大规模基因表达动态系统控制中的计算负担，通过聚类具有相似动态特性的系统实现可扩展且可靠的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的控制策略在处理大量细胞群体时面临计算负担重和生物异质性的问题，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: 基于输入-输出数据，使用数据驱动的方法量化控制相关的相似性，并对系统进行聚类；采用分层的领导者-跟随者控制架构，每个簇使用一个控制器。

Result: 在基因表达动态的仿真中验证了该方法的有效性，表明基于相似性的分组能够显著减少需解决的控制问题数量，并保持闭环系统的良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的相似性聚类方法结合分层控制架构，能够有效实现对异质生物系统的可扩展、可靠控制，适用于合成生物学等应用领域。

Abstract: Cybergenetic control of gene expression enables applications in synthetic biology, drug development, and biomanufacturing. Microfluidic platforms allow the parallel control of large cell populations. However, the resulting computational burden and intrinsic biological heterogeneity limit the scalability of conventional control strategies. In this work, we propose a similarity-based framework to reduce the computational requirement of controlling large numbers of dynamical systems. Building on existing data-driven methods for quantifying control-relevant similarity from input-output data, we cluster systems with similar dynamics without requiring explicit system identification. Based on this grouping, we develop a hierarchical leader-follower control architecture, where a single controller is designed for each cluster and applied to all members. This significantly reduces the number of control problems that need to be solved. Furthermore, we analyse the closed-loop behaviour within clusters and develop data-driven conditions under which the clustered closed-loop systems remain well-posed. The proposed approach is demonstrated in simulations of gene expression dynamics, showing that similarity-based grouping enables scalable and reliable control of heterogeneous biological systems.

</details>


### [206] [Quantifying Power to Voltage and Frequency Dynamics for Oscillation Propagation Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03929)
*Onur Alican,Dionysios Moutevelis,Marc Cheah-Mañe,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频域线性分析的框架，用于评估电力系统中各节点电压和发电单元频率对多时间尺度振荡的敏感性，并提出了频率敏感性指数（FSI）和电压敏感性指数（VSI），通过IEEE 68节点系统验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆变型电源（IBRs）接入电力系统后引发多时间尺度动态与振荡，且振荡源、频率及传播路径难以识别，威胁系统安全运行，亟需有效的分析工具。

Method: 基于线性电磁暂态（EMT）模型推导功率注入与网络各点电压、频率变量之间的传递函数，构建频域分析框架，并从中解析得出频率敏感性指数（FSI）和电压敏感性指数（VSI）。

Result: 所提指标能够量化系统在宽频谱范围内对不同类型振荡的敏感性，识别关键薄弱节点和传播特征，并在IEEE 68节点系统中验证了其有效性，时域仿真结果表明该方法具有高精度。

Conclusion: 该框架为高比例逆变型资源接入下的电力系统振荡检测与传播分析提供了有效工具，有助于提升系统安全性与稳定性。

Abstract: The integration of Inverter-Based Resources (IBRs) into power systems introduces multi-timescale dynamics and oscillations which may propagate to distant areas and endanger the safe system operation. These oscillations pose a significant challenge as their source, frequency, and propagation pathways are often challenging to identify in large interconnected systems, comprising numerous synchronous machines and IBRs. For the above reasons, analytical tools that identify the sensitivity of the system to oscillations within a large frequency spectrum, affecting both voltage and frequency variables across various network locations, are of interest. This paper addresses this topic by introducing a frequency-domain framework based on linear analysis, which characterizes the sensitivity to oscillations of each network bus voltage and of each generation unit frequency. Within this framework, two quantitative indicators are proposed, namely the Frequency Sensitivity Index (FSI) and Voltage Sensitivity Index (VSI). These indexes are derived analytically from the transfer functions which relate the active and reactive power injections to each bus with the voltage and frequency variables across the network, derived from the linear Electromagnetic Transient (EMT) power system model. The proposed indices quantify the sensitivity of the system to oscillations of different type and frequency, providing insights for both oscillation detection and propagation analysis. The methodology is applied to a case study based on the modified IEEE 68-bus benchmark system under partial and full IBR penetration, while its accuracy is validated through EMT time-domain simulations using linear and nonlinear models developed in Matlab/Simulink environment.

</details>


### [207] [Formation Matrix and Energy-based Control of Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04158)
*Martín Crespo,Sergio Junco,Matías Nacusse*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的多机器人系统编队控制方法，通过弹簧-阻尼网络和键合图模型实现平面内的编队保持与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决多智能体系统在平面运动中保持期望编队并避免碰撞的问题，需要一种能够自然描述能量交换与相互作用结构的控制框架。

Method: 采用基于端口-哈密顿系统的控制互联（CbI）方法，利用图论构建描述智能体间距离与相对速度的编队矩阵，并结合键合图模型设计能量基控制器。

Result: 成功实现了leader-following和基于位置的编队控制，闭环系统具有良好的稳定性，仿真验证了该方法在多种场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过能量成形与互联结构设计，为多智能体编队控制提供了系统化、物理直观且稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an energy-based controller for a multiagent robotic system designed to achieve and maintain a specific formation while moving on a plane and avoiding collisions between agents. The controller emulates a network of elementary spring-damper modules connecting pairs of agents. This network, with its de-energized states representing the desired formation, determines the system's dynamics, which is fully encapsulated by a bond graph model. The modeling is further enhanced through the introduction of a formation matrix, using a graph-theoretic approach, that describes both the distances and relative velocities among the agents of the arrangement. This matrix mathematically represents the interconnection and energy-exchange structure of the bond graph, allowing us to put it in correspondence with the control-by-interconnection CbI-scheme of the IDA-PBC theory, facilitating the solution of the formation control problem within the port-Hamiltonian system framework. Furthermore, the paper presents leader-following and position-based formation control systems based on the CbI scheme, including a stability analysis of the corresponding closed-loop systems. The theoretical findings are validated through numerical simulations across various scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Evaluating GNNs for Success Prediction in Artist Collaboration Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02920)
*Wiktor Dowgiałło*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究引入了波兰音乐场景的新数据集，并利用图神经网络（GNN）框架预测艺术家 popularity，比较其与多层感知机（MLP）的性能，发现节点特征比网络拓扑更具预测能力，而在跨国网络中GNN表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解欧洲不同音乐网络的结构特性，并评估图神经网络在基于艺术家元数据和网络位置进行 popularity 预测中的有效性。

Method: 采用先前研究中的方法分析波兰音乐网络，并构建GNN模型与MLP模型进行对比，使用F1-macro分数等指标评估模型性能。

Result: 波兰与跨国网络表现出相似的统计特性；GNN在特定情况下与MLP表现相当，但MLP整体更优；节点特征（如流派、厂牌）比网络结构更具预测力；在跨国家网络中GNN性能提升，可能捕捉到网络间的桥接结构。

Conclusion: 艺术家 popularity 预测更依赖于内部节点特征而非网络拓扑，但在更大规模、跨地域的网络中，图神经网络能更好地利用关系信息，显示出潜在优势。

Abstract: As the music industry becomes an increasingly collaborative effort, understanding the underlying structures of the artist network has become a focal point in cultural data analytics. This study expands on the previous analyses of the Italian and Danish networks by introducing a novel dataset of the Polish music scene. By utilizing methodologies used in the prior studies, this work enables a direct comparison between three distinct European music landscapes and allows to merged the created networks into one. Furthermore, this research introduces a framework to test the efficacy of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for artist popularity predictions based on the metadata and the position in the network. The statistical analysis revealed that the Polish and tri-national network exhibit similar properties and clustering behaviours, consistent with prior models. An evaluation of the predictive architectures reveals that while GNN models achieve a comparable F1-macro scores to the Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) in specific cases however the MLP remains a superior model regarding the success metric. The results suggest that internal node features - such as genre and label affiliation might carry more predictive capabilities than the topology of the network. The higher performance of the GNN models in the tri-national network might also suggests that the relational features become more informative when the network spans multiple linguistic and geographic boundaries, with the GNNs potentially capturing complex 'bridge' structures between the merged networks.

</details>


### [209] [Statistical Feature Augmentation for Anomaly Detection in Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02965)
*Philipp Schlinge,Jean-Luc Schnipper,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种统计特征增强方法，用于在动态网络中显式编码行为交互统计信息，提升了异常检测性能，并支持细粒度的行为重要性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型难以直接从原始事件流中捕捉短期行为交互信号（如发送者强度或交互惯性），需要更好的方法来融合时间/结构与特征信息。

Method: 提出一种统计特征增强方法，将行为交互统计量显式编码到输入特征空间中，并在连续和离散时间架构的七种模型上进行评估。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集（Reddit、Wikipedia、MOOC）上的实验表明，该增强方法 consistently 提升了异常检测性能，并支持基于独立特征维度的细粒度事后分析。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合了经典网络分析与深度学习，在动态网络建模中具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic networks are being applied in many domains, from social media to logistics systems, each with their own set of special characteristics. A model employed on this type of data must capture the duality between temporal/structural and feature-based information. Yet state-of-the-art deep learning models often struggle to learn especially short-term behavioral interaction signals, such as sender intensity or interaction inertia, directly from raw event streams. To address this gap, we propose a statistical feature augmentation method that explicitly encodes behavioral interaction statistics into the input feature space. We evaluate our proposed method on an anomaly detection task across three real-world datasets (Reddit, Wikipedia, MOOC) and seven models spanning both continuous-time and discrete-time architectures. As a baseline, we apply the same models trained on the original embeddings. Our results show, that augmentation consistently improves detection performance. Beyond performance, the enriched input enables fine-grained post-hoc analysis of behavioral importance, since each statistic occupies a dedicated input dimension. In particular, this work showcases a promising approach for merging classical network analysis with deep learning.

</details>


### [210] [Toward Collective-Centric Evaluation of Preference Inference for Participatory Democracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02990)
*Pierre-Antoine Lequeu,Salim Hafid,Paul Lerner,Nazanin Shafiabadi,Laurène Cave,David Mas,Jean-Philippe Cointet,Benjamin Piwowarski,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大规模参与式民主平台中，偏好推断（PI）模型如何影响集体决策的偏好格局，提出了一种以集体为中心的评估框架，并发布了目前最大的多语言数据集，揭示了仅以预测准确率衡量PI模型是不够的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Polis和Remesh等大规模在线协商平台中，由于投票数据极度稀疏，需依赖偏好推断模型补全缺失投票，但现有方法可能扭曲共识、冲突和少数意见的表达，缺乏对PI模型如何影响集体偏好结构的系统性理解。

Method: 提出一种超越个体预测准确率的集体中心型评估框架，用于衡量推断结果是否保留了原始偏好结构的关键特征，并在包含9万多名参与者、100万次投票和22种语言的四个真实世界协商数据集上，对多种现有PI模型进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，尽管不同PI模型具有相似的预测准确率，但在保持集体结构（如共识模式、分歧分布和少数群体支持）方面存在显著差异，说明仅靠准确率不足以评估民主场景中的PI模型。

Conclusion: 准确性不是评估民主环境中偏好推断模型的充分标准，必须结合对集体偏好结构保真度的评估，本文提出的框架有助于开发在扩展协商规模的同时不损害民主完整性的AI系统。

Abstract: To scale up collective decision-making, participatory democracy platforms such as Polis and Remesh enable online deliberation among thousands of participants. However, at this scale, participants cannot review every opinion submitted by others, producing highly sparse voting data that misrepresent patterns of consensus, conflict, and minority support. Platforms therefore increasingly rely on Preference Inference (PI) models to predict missing votes. Yet this automation is not neutral: inferred preferences can artificially amplify, suppress, or reorder existing patterns of support, ultimately reshaping how the outcomes of a deliberation are interpreted. More generally, we lack a systematic understanding of how existing PI methods affect the collective preference landscape. To address this gap, we benchmark several existing PI approaches in this context. Moving beyond conventional user-centric evaluations centered on the accuracy of individual predictions, we introduce a collective-centric evaluation framework that measures whether inferred votes preserve salient properties of the broader preference landscape. We further contribute the largest multilingual dataset of its kind: four consultations spanning over 90k participants, 1M votes, and 22 languages. Our experiments show that models with comparable predictive accuracy can differ substantially in the degree to which they preserve the collective structure. These results demonstrate that accuracy alone is insufficient for evaluating \emph{PI} in democratic settings. By contributing a novel comprehensive and collective-centric evaluation benchmark for the task of PI, this work aims to support the development of AI systems that scale deliberation without compromising the integrity of its democratic outcomes.

</details>


### [211] [Constructing the Field of Philanthropic and Nonprofit Studies: Evidence from Citation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03291)
*Jin Ai,Richard S. Steinberg,Chao Guo,Filipi Nascimento Silva*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合期刊与关键词检索、引文网络分析、自然语言处理及大语言模型辅助聚类标注，绘制了慈善与非营利研究（PNPS）领域的知识图谱，揭示其由非营利组织、社会运动和志愿行动三大核心构成的松散结构，并发现该领域比以往认知更广泛地覆盖生物医学与技术等学科。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管慈善与非营利研究（PNPS）作为跨学科领域迅速发展，但其知识结构和边界仍不清晰，亟需系统性梳理以明确学科定位与整合方向。

Method: 结合期刊与关键词检索、引文网络分析、自然语言处理以及大语言模型辅助的聚类标签方法，基于Web of Science中60,917篇文献进行主题社区识别、结构连接分析及主流期刊代表性评估。

Result: PNPS是一个由‘非营利组织’、‘社会运动’和‘自愿行动’三大权力中心主导的松散关联领域；学科影响范围超出传统社科与人文学科，延伸至生物医学与技术等领域；主流期刊对整体学术版图的覆盖有限。

Conclusion: PNPS领域具有更广泛的跨学科基础，未来应加强不同研究领域之间的整合，提升学科凝聚力与理论连贯性。

Abstract: Philanthropic and nonprofit studies (PNPS) has grown rapidly as an interdisciplinary field, yet its intellectual structure and boundaries remain only partially visible. This study maps the field by combining journal- and keyword-based retrieval with citation network analysis, natural language processing, and large language model-assisted cluster labeling. Using 60,917 Web of Science articles, we identify major topical communities, examine their structural connections, and assess how well mainstream PNPS journals represent the broader landscape of related scholarship. The results show that PNPS is a loosely connected field dominated by three distinct "power centers": nonprofit organizations, social movements, and voluntary action. The analysis also reveals a broader disciplinary footprint than commonly recognized, extending beyond the social sciences and humanities into areas such as biomedicine and technology. These findings clarify the organization of PNPS and highlight opportunities for stronger integration across research domains.

</details>


### [212] [When Vision Meets Graphs: A Survey on Graph Reasoning and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03816)
*Xinjian Zhao,Wei Pang,Zhixuan Yu,Xiangru Jian,Xiaozhuang Song,Yaoyao Xu,Zhongkai Xue,Dingshuo Chen,Shu Wu,Philip Torr,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文综述了“视觉与图”这一新兴领域，探讨将图的可视化作为推理和学习的一等输入，提出了三个研究方向：用于图推理的视觉、用于图学习的视觉以及科学图分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图神经网络在图机器学习中占据主导地位，但科学家通常通过可视化来理解图结构。现有方法大多将图视为符号结构，忽视了其视觉形式，而当前强大的视觉与视觉-语言模型为此提供了新的机遇。

Method: 对现有工作进行系统梳理，并划分为三个方向：视觉用于图推理、视觉用于图学习、面向具有标准化绘图规范的科学图领域。

Result: 提供了‘视觉与图’领域的首个系统性综述，明确了当前方法的能力与局限。

Conclusion: 呼吁重视图的视觉表示，推动构建能像科学家一样感知和推理图的基础模型。

Abstract: Graphs are a fundamental data structure underlying many problems in the natural and social sciences. Over the past decade, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have dominated graph machine learning, supported by solid theoretical foundations. Yet scientists often understand graph structure through vision: chemists read molecular diagrams and social scientists inspect network visualizations. Despite decades of work on graph visualization, most graph learning pipelines still treat graphs purely as symbolic structures, rarely leveraging the visual form of graphs. We argue that this gap deserves renewed attention in the era of powerful vision and vision-language models. This survey provides a first systematic overview of the emerging area we term vision meets graphs, which treats visual depictions of graphs as first-class inputs for reasoning and learning. We organize existing work into three threads. Vision for Graph Reasoning studies how models can use visual depictions of graphs to understand structure and carry out multi-step reasoning. Vision for Graph Learning explores how visual features can complement or augment graph encoders beyond known limitations of message passing. Scientific Graphs examines domains where standardized depiction conventions support both reasoning and learning. Our goal is to clarify what current methods can and cannot do, and to outline a path toward foundation models that perceive and reason about graphs as scientists do.

</details>


### [213] [Fair Top-k Katz Centrality via Graph Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03899)
*Ivan Qin,Prudence Wong,Lutz Oettershagen*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Katz中心性在top-k排名中的公平性问题，提出了Fair Top-k Katz Centrality Design问题，并证明其强不可近似性；基于边界驱动的编辑结构，设计了高效的BLADE算法，在保持原排名结构的同时实现公平top-k排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图中心性公平性干预方法多关注全局分数或修改算子，较少直接控制top-k排名中的群体代表性，因此需要针对top-k场景设计更精确的公平性优化方法。

Method: 提出Fair Top-k Katz Centrality Design问题，推导Katz中心性的闭式敏感度表达式，发现有效边编辑具有边界驱动特性，并据此设计BLADE算法，采用基于得分的定向批次和热启动Katz更新来提升效率。

Result: 理论证明该问题在最坏情况下不可近似；实验表明BLADE在合成和真实网络中均能以更少的边编辑达到目标群体比例，且可扩展至大规模图。

Conclusion: 通过边界驱动的边编辑策略，可在top-k Katz中心性排名中高效实现公平性目标，BLADE算法在效果与效率上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: Centrality measures are widely used to rank nodes in networked data, but fairness interventions for graph centrality typically target global score mass or modify the centrality operator rather than controlling who appears in the displayed top-k ranking. We study this top-k setting for Katz centrality. Given a target group proportion, an admissible set of directed edge additions, and a fairness tolerance, the goal is to find the smallest edit set whose resulting Katz top-k ranking satisfies the target representation constraint. We formalize this problem as Fair Top-k Katz Centrality Design and show that the minimum-edit objective is strongly inapproximable, ruling out worst-case polynomial-time approximation guarantees unless P = NP. We then derive closed-form Katz sensitivity expressions showing that useful edits are boundary-driven: they must help promotable nodes outside the top-k set overtake opposing nodes inside it. Based on this structure, we develop BLADE, a scalable boundary-link algorithm that avoids dense Katz-kernel maintenance by using score-based direct-target batches and warm-started Katz updates. Experiments on synthetic and real-world networks show that BLADE reaches the desired top-k representation using far fewer edits than natural baselines, while scaling to large real-world graphs and preserving the original ranking structure.

</details>
